// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  MAIN
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  // Initialize AdMob
  MobileAds.instance.initialize();

  try {
    await Firebase.initializeApp();
  } catch (e, st) {
    debugPrint('Firebase init failed: $e\n$st');
  }

  await _applyPortraitLock();

  // Pre‑check permissions before launching the UI
  final permissionsGranted = await PermissionService.areAllRequiredGranted();

  runApp(NexusWeaveApp(initialPermissionsGranted: permissionsGranted));
}

Future<void> _applyPortraitLock() =>
    SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations([DeviceOrientation.portraitUp]);

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PORTRAIT-LOCK OBSERVER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _PortraitLockObserver extends WidgetsBindingObserver {
  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {
      _applyPortraitLock();
    }
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PERMISSION SERVICE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class PermissionService {
  /// Returns the list of permissions required based on Android version.
  static Future<List<Permission>> getRequiredPermissions() async {
    final permissions = <Permission>[
      Permission.camera,
      Permission.microphone,
      Permission.notification,
      Permission.location, // 🔹 Added location permission
    ];

    // Storage permissions depend on Android version
    if (await _isAndroid13OrAbove()) {
      permissions.addAll([
        Permission.photos,
        Permission.videos,
        Permission.audio,
      ]);
    } else if (await _isAndroid11OrAbove()) {
      // Android 11-12: need READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE for broad media access
      permissions.add(Permission.storage);
    } else {
      // Android 10 and below: legacy external storage works with READ/WRITE
      permissions.add(Permission.storage);
    }

    return permissions;
  }

  static Future<bool> _isAndroid13OrAbove() async {
    if (!await _isAndroid) return false;
    final info = await DeviceInfoPlugin().androidInfo;
    return info.version.sdkInt >= 33;
  }

  static Future<bool> _isAndroid11OrAbove() async {
    if (!await _isAndroid) return false;
    final info = await DeviceInfoPlugin().androidInfo;
    return info.version.sdkInt >= 30;
  }

  static Future<bool> get _isAndroid async {
    try {
      await DeviceInfoPlugin().androidInfo;
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Check if all required permissions are granted.
  static Future<bool> areAllRequiredGranted() async {
    final permissions = await getRequiredPermissions();
    for (final perm in permissions) {
      if (await perm.status != PermissionStatus.granted) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /// Request all required permissions one by one.
  /// Returns true if all essential permissions were granted.
  static Future<bool> requestRequiredPermissions({
    required BuildContext context,
    VoidCallback? onAllGranted,
  }) async {
    final permissions = await getRequiredPermissions();

    for (final perm in permissions) {
      final status = await perm.status;
      if (status.isGranted) continue;

      // Request permission
      final result = await perm.request();
      if (result.isPermanentlyDenied) {
        // Show dialog to open settings
        // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
        _showSettingsDialog(context, perm);
        return false;
      } else if (!result.isGranted) {
        // Denied but not permanently – we can ask again later.
        return false;
      }
    }

    onAllGranted?.call();
    return true;
  }

  static void _showSettingsDialog(BuildContext context, Permission permission) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Permission Required'),
        content: Text(
          '${permission.toString().split('.').last} is required for full functionality. '
          'Please enable it in app settings.',
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              openAppSettings();
              Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
            },
            child: const Text('Open Settings'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ENHANCED PERMISSION GATE WIDGET
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class PermissionGate extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback onPermissionsGranted;

  const PermissionGate({super.key, required this.onPermissionsGranted});

  @override
  State<PermissionGate> createState() => _PermissionGateState();
}

class _PermissionGateState extends State<PermissionGate>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  late Animation<double> _fadeAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _animationController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 600),
    );
    _fadeAnimation = CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _animationController,
      curve: Curves.easeOut,
    );
    _animationController.forward();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _animationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleRequestPermissions() async {
    await PermissionService.requestRequiredPermissions(
      context: context,
      onAllGranted: widget.onPermissionsGranted,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topCenter,
            end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
            colors: [
              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
              Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary.withOpacity(0.1),
              Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
            ],
          ),
        ),
        child: FadeTransition(
          opacity: _fadeAnimation,
          child: Center(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32.0),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  // App logo or icon
                  Container(
                    width: 50,
                    height: 50,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      color: Theme.of(
                        context,
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      ).colorScheme.primary.withOpacity(0.1),
                    ),
                    child: Icon(
                      Icons.verified_user_rounded,
                      size: 40,
                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  // Title
                  Text(
                    'Welcome to NexVibe',
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
                    ),
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  // Certified badge
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: 16,
                      vertical: 8,
                    ),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primaryContainer,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          Icons.check_circle,
                          size: 18,
                          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Text(
                          'Certified & Secure',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  // Description
                  Text(
                    'To provide you with the best experience, NexVibe needs access to:',
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                      color: Theme.of(
                        context,
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      ).colorScheme.onSurface.withOpacity(0.8),
                    ),
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  // Permission items
                  _PermissionItem(
                    icon: Icons.camera_alt_outlined,
                    label: 'Camera',
                    description: 'For capturing photos and videos',
                  ),
                  _PermissionItem(
                    icon: Icons.mic_none_outlined,
                    label: 'Microphone',
                    description: 'For recording audio with your videos',
                  ),
                  _PermissionItem(
                    icon: Icons.photo_library_outlined,
                    label: 'Media Storage',
                    description: 'To save and access your creations',
                  ),
                  _PermissionItem(
                    icon: Icons.notifications_none_outlined,
                    label: 'Notifications',
                    description: 'Stay updated with important alerts',
                  ),
                  // 🔹 Location permission item added
                  _PermissionItem(
                    icon: Icons.location_on_outlined,
                    label: 'Location',
                    description: 'To provide location-based features',
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 32),
                  // Continue button
                  SizedBox(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    height: 50,
                    child: ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: _handleRequestPermissions,
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                        foregroundColor: Theme.of(
                          context,
                        ).colorScheme.onPrimary,
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                        ),
                        elevation: 2,
                      ),
                      child: const Text(
                        'Continue',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  // Privacy note
                  Text(
                    'Your privacy is our priority. Permissions are only used for core features.',
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                      color: Theme.of(
                        context,
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      ).colorScheme.onSurface.withOpacity(0.5),

            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

    required this.label,
    required this.description,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 48,
            height: 48,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary.withOpacity(0.1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Icon(
              icon,
              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
              size: 24,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  label,
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text(
                  description,
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                    color: Theme.of(
                      context,
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    ).colorScheme.onSurface.withOpacity(0.6),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ROOT APP
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class NexusWeaveApp extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool initialPermissionsGranted;

  const NexusWeaveApp({super.key, required this.initialPermissionsGranted});

  @override
  State<NexusWeaveApp> createState() => _NexusWeaveAppState();
}

class _NexusWeaveAppState extends State<NexusWeaveApp> {
  final _observer = _PortraitLockObserver();
  late bool _permissionsGranted;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(_observer);
    _permissionsGranted = widget.initialPermissionsGranted;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(_observer);
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onPermissionsGranted() {
    setState(() {
      _permissionsGranted = true;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'NexVibe',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      themeMode: ThemeMode.system,
      theme: AppThemeData.light(),
      darkTheme: AppThemeData.dark(),
      home: _permissionsGranted
          ? const SplashScreen() // Assuming SplashScreen is defined elsewhere
          : PermissionGate(onPermissionsGranted: _onPermissionsGranted),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  THEME DATA
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const PageTransitionsTheme _iosTransitions = PageTransitionsTheme(
  builders: {
    TargetPlatform.android: CupertinoPageTransitionsBuilder(),
    TargetPlatform.iOS: CupertinoPageTransitionsBuilder(),
    TargetPlatform.macOS: CupertinoPageTransitionsBuilder(),
    TargetPlatform.windows: CupertinoPageTransitionsBuilder(),
    TargetPlatform.linux: CupertinoPageTransitionsBuilder(),
    TargetPlatform.fuchsia: CupertinoPageTransitionsBuilder(),
  },
);

abstract final class AppThemeData {
  static ThemeData light() => ThemeData(
    useMaterial3: true,
    brightness: Brightness.light,
    scaffoldBackgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF2F2F7),
    colorScheme: const ColorScheme.light(
      primary: Color(0xFF007AFF),
      surface: Colors.white,
      onPrimary: Colors.white,
      onSurface: Color(0xFF1C1C1E),
      primaryContainer: Color(0xFFE5F1FF),
    ),
    pageTransitionsTheme: _iosTransitions,
  );

  static ThemeData dark() => ThemeData(
    useMaterial3: true,
    brightness: Brightness.dark,
    scaffoldBackgroundColor: Colors.black,
    colorScheme: const ColorScheme.dark(
      primary: Color(0xFF0A84FF),
      surface: Color(0xFF1C1C1E),
      onPrimary: Colors.white,
      onSurface: Colors.white,
      primaryContainer: Color(0xFF1A3A5C),
    ),
    pageTransitionsTheme: _iosTransitions,
  );
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  APP COLORS — semantic tokens resolved per brightness
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class AppColors {
  final bool isDark;
  const AppColors(this.isDark);

  // Surfaces
  Color get background => isDark ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
  Color get cardBg => isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
  Color get fieldBg =>
      isDark ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
  Color get cardBorder =>
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      isDark ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.08) : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.07);
  Color get divider =>
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      isDark ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.08) : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.07);

  // Brand
  Color get primary =>
      isDark ? const Color(0xFF0A84FF) : const Color(0xFF007AFF);
  Color get primaryDeep =>
      isDark ? const Color(0xFF0051D5) : const Color(0xFF004CC8);
  Color get accent => const Color(0xFF5E5CE6);

  // Status
  Color get success =>
      isDark ? const Color(0xFF30D158) : const Color(0xFF34C759);
  Color get error => isDark ? const Color(0xFFFF453A) : const Color(0xFFFF3B30);
  Color get warning =>
      isDark ? const Color(0xFFFFD60A) : const Color(0xFFFFCC00);

  // Text
  Color get primaryText => isDark ? Colors.white : const Color(0xFF1C1C1E);
  Color get secondaryText => isDark
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.48)
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      : const Color(0xFF3C3C43).withOpacity(0.60);
  Color get tertiaryText => isDark
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25)
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      : const Color(0xFF3C3C43).withOpacity(0.30);

  // Buttons
  Color get buttonSecBg =>
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      isDark ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.08) : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05);
  Color get buttonSecBorder =>
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      isDark ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.14) : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.10);
  Color get buttonSecText =>
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      isDark ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.90) : const Color(0xFF1C1C1E);

  // Misc
  Color get snackBg =>
      isDark ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E) : const Color(0xFF1C1C1E);
  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
  Color get glow => primary.withOpacity(0.45);
  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
  Color get logoBorder => Colors.white.withOpacity(isDark ? 0.15 : 0.40);

  // Blobs / rings
  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
  Color get blob1 => primary.withOpacity(isDark ? 0.18 : 0.07);
  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
  Color get blob2 => accent.withOpacity(isDark ? 0.14 : 0.05);
  double get ringOpacity => isDark ? 0.08 : 0.09;

  // Particles
  double get particleOpacity => isDark ? 1.0 : 0.38;

  // Gradients
  List<Color> get headingGradient => isDark
      ? [Colors.white, const Color(0xFFB0D4FF)]
      : [const Color(0xFF1C1C1E), const Color(0xFF007AFF)];

  List<Color> get brandGradient => isDark
      ? [
          const Color(0xFF60C8FF),
          const Color(0xFF0A84FF),
          const Color(0xFF5E5CE6),
        ]
      : [
          const Color(0xFF007AFF),
          const Color(0xFF0051D5),
          const Color(0xFF5E5CE6),
        ];

  List<Color> get logoGradient => [
    isDark ? const Color(0xFF2A93FF) : const Color(0xFF2A93FF),
    isDark ? const Color(0xFF003EB3) : const Color(0xFF0051D5),
  ];
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  RESPONSIVE EXTENSION
//  Base canvas: 390 × 844 px  (iPhone 14 Pro)
//  sp() → width-relative scale | hp() → height-relative scale
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
extension AppContext on BuildContext {
  Size get screenSize => MediaQuery.sizeOf(this);
  double get sw => screenSize.width;
  double get sh => screenSize.height;

  /// Width-proportional size (font sizes, icon sizes, horizontal spacing)
  double sp(double v) => v * sw / 390.0;

  /// Height-proportional size (vertical spacing, vertical offsets)
  double hp(double v) => v * sh / 844.0;

  bool get isDark => Theme.of(this).brightness == Brightness.dark;
  AppColors get colors => AppColors(isDark);
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  UNIVERSAL SKELETON LOADER EXTENSION (NEW)
//  Automatically works on ANY future class using: `.skeletonize(enabled: true)`
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
extension SkeletonExtension on Widget {
  /// Wraps any widget in a Shimmer Skeleton effect.
  /// If [enabled] is true, the widget turns into a glowing skeleton block.
  Widget skeletonize({bool enabled = true}) {
    if (!enabled) return this;
    return _ShimmerMask(child: this);
  }
}

class _ShimmerMask extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  const _ShimmerMask({required this.child});

  @override
  State<_ShimmerMask> createState() => _ShimmerMaskState();
}

class _ShimmerMaskState extends State<_ShimmerMask>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1500),
    )..repeat();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Automatically adapts to dark/light mode securely.
    final baseColor = context.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
        : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA);
    final highlightColor = context.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF3A3A3C)
        : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);

    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _controller,
      builder: (context, child) {
        return ShaderMask(
          blendMode: BlendMode.srcATop,
          shaderCallback: (bounds) {
            return LinearGradient(
              begin: Alignment(-2.0 + (_controller.value * 4.0), 0.0),
              end: Alignment(-1.0 + (_controller.value * 4.0), 0.0),
              colors: [baseColor, highlightColor, baseColor],
              stops: const [0.0, 0.5, 1.0],
            ).createShader(bounds);
          },
          child: child,
        );
      },
      child: widget.child,
    );
  }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PARTICLE MODEL + FACTORY
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _Particle {
  final double x, y, size, speed, opacity, hue;
  const _Particle({
    required this.x,
    required this.y,
    required this.size,
    required this.speed,
    required this.opacity,
    required this.hue,
  });
}

List<_Particle> _buildParticles(int seed, int count) {
  final rng = math.Random(seed);
  return List.generate(
    count,
    (_) => _Particle(
      x: rng.nextDouble(),
      y: rng.nextDouble(),
      size: rng.nextDouble() * 2.0 + 0.6,
      speed: rng.nextDouble() * 0.004 + 0.001,
      opacity: rng.nextDouble() * 0.50 + 0.10,
      hue: rng.nextDouble() * 60.0 + 195.0,
    ),
  );
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PARTICLE PAINTER
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _ParticlePainter extends CustomPainter {
  final List<_Particle> particles;
  final double tick;
  final double opacityMult;

  const _ParticlePainter(this.particles, this.tick, this.opacityMult);

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final paint = Paint()
      ..maskFilter = const MaskFilter.blur(BlurStyle.normal, 1.0);
    for (final p in particles) {
      final dy = (p.y + tick * p.speed * 4.0) % 1.0;
      final dx =
          (p.x + math.sin(tick * math.pi * 2.0 * p.speed * 2.5) * 0.015) % 1.0;
      paint.color = HSLColor.fromAHSL(
        (p.opacity * opacityMult).clamp(0.0, 1.0),
        p.hue,
        0.85,
        0.70,
      ).toColor();
      canvas.drawCircle(
        Offset(dx * size.width, dy * size.height),
        p.size,
        paint,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_ParticlePainter old) =>
      old.tick != tick || old.opacityMult != opacityMult;
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  MESH BACKGROUND — two radial blobs, responsive + themed
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _MeshBackground extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MeshBackground();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = context.colors;
    return SizedBox.expand(
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          Positioned(
            top: context.hp(-100),
            left: context.sp(-80),
            child: _Blob(size: context.sp(380), color: c.blob1),
          ),
          Positioned(
            bottom: context.hp(-60),
            right: context.sp(-100),
            child: _Blob(size: context.sp(320), color: c.blob2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Blob extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;
  final Color color;
  const _Blob({required this.size, required this.color});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        gradient: RadialGradient(colors: [color, Colors.transparent]),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  DECORATIVE RING — themed, responsive
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _DecorativeRing extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;
  final double opacityMult;
  const _DecorativeRing({required this.size, this.opacityMult = 1.0});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = context.colors;
    final base = c.ringOpacity * opacityMult;
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        border: Border.all(color: c.primary.withOpacity(base), width: 1.0),
      ),
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      child: Container(
        width: size * 0.65,
        height: size * 0.65,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          shape: BoxShape.circle,
          border: Border.all(
            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
            color: c.accent.withOpacity(base * 0.60),
            width: 1.0,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  LOGO BADGE — responsive size, themed gradient
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _LogoBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;
  final double iconSize;
  const _LogoBadge({required this.size, required this.iconSize});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = context.colors;
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          colors: c.logoGradient,
        ),
        border: Border.all(color: c.logoBorder, width: 1.5),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
            color: c.glow.withOpacity(0.50),
            blurRadius: size * 0.33,
            offset: Offset(0, size * 0.10),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Icon(Icons.hub_rounded, color: Colors.white, size: iconSize),
    );
  }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  IOS BUTTON — stateful (owns press state), themed, responsive
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _IosButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  final bool isPrimary;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _IosButton({
    required this.label,
    required this.icon,
    required this.isPrimary,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  State<_IosButton> createState() => _IosButtonState();
}

class _IosButtonState extends State<_IosButton> {
  bool _pressed = false;

  void _onTapDown(TapDownDetails _) => setState(() => _pressed = true);
  void _onTapUp(TapUpDetails _) {
    setState(() => _pressed = false);
    widget.onTap();
  }

  void _onTapCancel() => setState(() => _pressed = false);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = context.colors;
    final h = context.hp(56).clamp(50.0, 64.0);
    final r = context.sp(16).clamp(13.0, 18.0);
    final fSize = context.sp(17).clamp(14.0, 20.0);
    final iSize = context.sp(20).clamp(16.0, 24.0);
    final gap = context.sp(10);

    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: _onTapDown,
      onTapUp: _onTapUp,
      onTapCancel: _onTapCancel,
      child: AnimatedScale(
        scale: _pressed ? 0.96 : 1.0,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 110),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        child: AnimatedOpacity(
          opacity: _pressed ? 0.82 : 1.0,
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 110),
          child: Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: h,
            decoration: widget.isPrimary
                ? BoxDecoration(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(r),
                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                      colors: [c.primary, c.primaryDeep],
                      begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                      end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                    ),
                    boxShadow: [
                      BoxShadow(
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.45),
                        blurRadius: 24,
                        offset: const Offset(0, 8),
                      ),
                      BoxShadow(
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
                        blurRadius: 40,
                        offset: const Offset(0, 16),
                      ),
                    ],
                  )
                : BoxDecoration(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(r),
                    color: c.buttonSecBg,
                    border: Border.all(color: c.buttonSecBorder, width: 1.0),
                  ),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  widget.icon,
                  color: widget.isPrimary ? Colors.white : c.buttonSecText,
                  size: iSize,
                ),
                SizedBox(width: gap),
                Text(
                  widget.label,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: fSize,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    letterSpacing: -0.2,
                    color: widget.isPrimary ? Colors.white : c.buttonSecText,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ── TOP-LEVEL GRADIENT PALETTE ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Deterministic per-account gradients shared by AccountLogin, MyHomePage,
// MyProfile, and _SavedAccountTile.
const List<List<Color>> _kGradientPalette = [
  [Color(0xFF007AFF), Color(0xFF5AC8FA)],
  [Color(0xFF6C63FF), Color(0xFF48C6EF)],
  [Color(0xFFFF6B6B), Color(0xFFFFD93D)],
  [Color(0xFF43E97B), Color(0xFF38F9D7)],
  [Color(0xFFBF5AF2), Color(0xFFFF375F)],
  [Color(0xFF11998E), Color(0xFF38EF7D)],
  [Color(0xFFFF9F0A), Color(0xFFFFD60A)],
  [Color(0xFF2193B0), Color(0xFF6DD5ED)],
];

/// Returns the gradient palette entry whose index is derived from the email
/// string, guaranteeing the same user always gets the same colours.
List<Color> _gradientForEmail(String email) {
  if (email.isEmpty) return _kGradientPalette[0];
  return _kGradientPalette[email.hashCode.abs() % _kGradientPalette.length];
}

int _gradientIndexForEmail(String email) {
  if (email.isEmpty) return 0;
  return email.hashCode.abs() % _kGradientPalette.length;
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SPLASH SCREEN CLASS
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class SplashScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SplashScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SplashScreen> createState() => _SplashScreenState();
}

class _SplashScreenState extends State<SplashScreen>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  // Controllers
  late final AnimationController _particleCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _logoCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _textCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _pulseCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _exitCtrl;

  // Animations
  late final Animation<double> _logoScale;
  late final Animation<double> _logoOpacity;
  late final Animation<double> _fromOpacity;
  late final Animation<Offset> _fromSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _brandOpacity;
  late final Animation<Offset> _brandSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _pulseAnim;
  late final Animation<double> _exitFade;

  final List<_Particle> _particles = _buildParticles(42, 55);

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    // Continuous particle drift
    _particleCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(seconds: 20),
    )..repeat();

    // Logo elastic pop-in
    _logoCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900),
    );
    _logoScale = Tween<double>(
      begin: 0.30,
      end: 1.0,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _logoCtrl, curve: Curves.elasticOut));
    _logoOpacity = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _logoCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.0, 0.40, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );

    // Text staggered reveal
    _textCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 700),
    );
    _fromOpacity = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _textCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.0, 0.60, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _fromSlide = Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.5), end: Offset.zero)
        .animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _textCtrl,
            curve: const Interval(0.0, 0.60, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
          ),
        );
    _brandOpacity = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _textCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.30, 1.0, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _brandSlide = Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.40), end: Offset.zero)
        .animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _textCtrl,
            curve: const Interval(0.30, 1.0, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
          ),
        );

    // Pulsing glow
    _pulseCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1400),
    )..repeat(reverse: true);
    _pulseAnim = Tween<double>(
      begin: 0.4,
      end: 1.0,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _pulseCtrl, curve: Curves.easeInOut));

    // Exit fade-out
    _exitCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 450),
    );
    _exitFade = Tween<double>(
      begin: 1.0,
      end: 0.0,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _exitCtrl, curve: Curves.easeIn));

    _runSequence();
  }

  // ── FIX: check Firebase auth state to decide destination ──────────────────
  Future<void> _runSequence() async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
    if (!mounted) return;
    _logoCtrl.forward();

    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 480));
    if (!mounted) return;
    _textCtrl.forward();

    // Total visible ≈ 2 s from app launch
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 1320));
    if (!mounted) return;

    // ── Route decision: logged-in → MyHomePage, not logged-in → EntryScreen
    // Do not run _exitCtrl (fade) here — it fades the splash to transparent and
    // causes a black flash before the next route paints. No transition.
    final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;

    Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) =>
            user != null ? const MyHomePage() : const EntryScreen(),
        transitionDuration: Duration.zero,
        reverseTransitionDuration: Duration.zero,
        opaque: true,
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _particleCtrl.dispose();
    _logoCtrl.dispose();
    _textCtrl.dispose();
    _pulseCtrl.dispose();
    _exitCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = context.colors;
    final logoSize = context.sp(110).clamp(82.0, 130.0);
    final iconSize = context.sp(50).clamp(38.0, 60.0);

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value:
          (context.isDark
                  ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light
                  : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
              .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: FadeTransition(
        opacity: _exitFade,
        child: Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: c.background,
          body: Stack(
            fit: StackFit.expand,
            children: [
              // ① Background blobs
              const _MeshBackground(),

              // ② Particle field
              Positioned.fill(
                child: AnimatedBuilder(
                  animation: _particleCtrl,
                  builder: (_, _) => CustomPaint(
                    painter: _ParticlePainter(
                      _particles,
                      _particleCtrl.value,
                      c.particleOpacity,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),

              // ③ Logo + text centered
              Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    // Pulsing glow halo around logo
                    AnimatedBuilder(
                      animation: _pulseAnim,
                      builder: (_, child) => Container(
                        width: logoSize * 1.30,
                        height: logoSize * 1.30,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          boxShadow: [
                            BoxShadow(
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              color: c.glow.withOpacity(
                                0.35 * _pulseAnim.value,
                              ),
                              blurRadius: 60,
                              spreadRadius: 18,
                            ),
                            BoxShadow(
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              color: c.accent.withOpacity(
                                0.20 * _pulseAnim.value,
                              ),
                              blurRadius: 90,
                              spreadRadius: 28,
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        alignment: Alignment.center,
                        child: child,
                      ),
                      child: ScaleTransition(
                        scale: _logoScale,
                        child: FadeTransition(
                          opacity: _logoOpacity,
                          child: _LogoBadge(size: logoSize, iconSize: iconSize),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),

                    SizedBox(height: context.hp(36)),

                    // "Powered by"
                    SlideTransition(
                      position: _fromSlide,
                      child: FadeTransition(
                        opacity: _fromOpacity,
                        child: Text(
                          'Powered by',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: context.sp(13).clamp(11.0, 15.0),
                            letterSpacing: 3.0,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                            color: c.tertiaryText,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),

                    SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),

                    // "NexusWeave" gradient shimmer
                    SlideTransition(
                      position: _brandSlide,
                      child: FadeTransition(
                        opacity: _brandOpacity,
                        child: ShaderMask(
                          shaderCallback: (bounds) => LinearGradient(
                            colors: c.brandGradient,
                          ).createShader(bounds),
                          child: Text(
                            'NexusWeave',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: context.sp(34).clamp(26.0, 40.0),
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                              letterSpacing: -0.5,
                              color: Colors.white, // required for ShaderMask
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              // ④ Bottom tagline
              Positioned(
                bottom: context.hp(52),
                left: 0,
                right: 0,
                child: FadeTransition(
                  opacity: _brandOpacity,
                  child: Text(
                    'Connect · Create · Collaborate',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: context.sp(12).clamp(10.0, 14.0),
                      letterSpacing: 1.8,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                      color: c.tertiaryText,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ENTRY SCREEN
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class EntryScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  /// Passed when navigating back after account creation.
  final String? successMessage;
  const EntryScreen({super.key, this.successMessage});

  @override
  State<EntryScreen> createState() => _EntryScreenState();
}

class _EntryScreenState extends State<EntryScreen>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  // Controllers
  late final AnimationController _entranceCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _particleCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _floatCtrl;

  // Animations
  late final Animation<double> _logoFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _logoSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _headingFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _headingSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _subFade;
  late final Animation<double> _btn1Fade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _btn1Slide;
  late final Animation<double> _btn2Fade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _btn2Slide;
  late final Animation<double> _floatAnim;

  final List<_Particle> _particles = _buildParticles(7, 45);

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _particleCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(seconds: 25),
    )..repeat();

    _floatCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 3000),
    )..repeat(reverse: true);
    _floatAnim = Tween<double>(
      begin: -7.0,
      end: 7.0,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _floatCtrl, curve: Curves.easeInOut));

    _entranceCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1200),
    );

    _logoFade = _fade(0.00, 0.30);
    _logoSlide = _slide(0.00, 0.30);
    _headingFade = _fade(0.15, 0.50);
    _headingSlide = _slide(0.15, 0.50);
    _subFade = _fade(0.30, 0.60);
    _btn1Fade = _fade(0.45, 0.75);
    _btn1Slide = _slide(0.45, 0.75);
    _btn2Fade = _fade(0.60, 0.90);
    _btn2Slide = _slide(0.60, 0.90);

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      _entranceCtrl.forward();
      if (widget.successMessage != null) {
        _showSuccessSnack(widget.successMessage!);
      }
    });
  }

  Animation<double> _fade(double s, double e) =>
      Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
        CurvedAnimation(
          parent: _entranceCtrl,
          curve: Interval(s, e, curve: Curves.easeOut),
        ),
      );

  Animation<Offset> _slide(double s, double e) =>
      Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.35), end: Offset.zero).animate(
        CurvedAnimation(
          parent: _entranceCtrl,
          curve: Interval(s, e, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
        ),
      );

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _entranceCtrl.dispose();
    _particleCtrl.dispose();
    _floatCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _showSuccessSnack(String text) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    final c = context.colors;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..clearSnackBars()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          backgroundColor: c.success,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          margin: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
            context.sp(20),
            0,
            context.sp(20),
            context.hp(32),
          ),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          duration: text == 'You just created your account.'
              ? const Duration(seconds: 2)
              : const Duration(seconds: 1),
          content: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const Icon(
                Icons.check_circle_rounded,
                color: Colors.white,
                size: 18,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Flexible(
                child: Text(
                  text,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = context.colors;
    final logoSize = context.sp(96).clamp(72.0, 112.0);
    final iconSize = context.sp(44).clamp(34.0, 52.0);
    final hPad = context.sp(28).clamp(20.0, 40.0);

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value:
          (context.isDark
                  ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light
                  : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
              .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        body: Stack(
          fit: StackFit.expand,
          children: [
            // ① Background mesh
            const _MeshBackground(),

            // ② Particles
            Positioned.fill(
              child: AnimatedBuilder(
                animation: _particleCtrl,
                builder: (_, _) => CustomPaint(
                  painter: _ParticlePainter(
                    _particles,
                    _particleCtrl.value,
                    c.particleOpacity,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // ③ Decorative rings
            Positioned(
              top: context.hp(-80),
              right: context.sp(-80),
              child: _DecorativeRing(size: context.sp(300).clamp(200.0, 360.0)),
            ),
            Positioned(
              bottom: context.hp(-120),
              left: context.sp(-60),
              child: _DecorativeRing(
                size: context.sp(280).clamp(180.0, 340.0),
                opacityMult: 0.70,
              ),
            ),

            // ④ Main content
            SafeArea(
              child: LayoutBuilder(
                builder: (context, constraints) {
                  return SingleChildScrollView(
                    physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                    child: ConstrainedBox(
                      constraints: BoxConstraints(
                        minHeight: constraints.maxHeight,
                      ),
                      child: IntrinsicHeight(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: hPad),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                            children: [
                              const Spacer(flex: 2),

                              // Floating logo
                              AnimatedBuilder(
                                animation: _floatAnim,
                                builder: (_, child) => Transform.translate(
                                  offset: Offset(0, _floatAnim.value),
                                  child: child,
                                ),
                                child: SlideTransition(
                                  position: _logoSlide,
                                  child: FadeTransition(
                                    opacity: _logoFade,
                                    child: _LogoBadge(
                                      size: logoSize,
                                      iconSize: iconSize,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),

                              SizedBox(height: context.hp(40)),

                              // Heading
                              SlideTransition(
                                position: _headingSlide,
                                child: FadeTransition(
                                  opacity: _headingFade,
                                  child: ShaderMask(
                                    shaderCallback: (bounds) => LinearGradient(
                                      colors: c.headingGradient,
                                      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                                      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                                    ).createShader(bounds),
                                    child: Text(
                                      'Welcome to\nNexVibe',
                                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        fontSize: context
                                            .sp(38)
                                            .clamp(28.0, 44.0),
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                        height: 1.15,
                                        letterSpacing: -1.0,
                                        color: Colors.white,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),

                              SizedBox(height: context.hp(14)),

                              // Subtitle
                              FadeTransition(
                                opacity: _subFade,
                                child: Text(
                                  'Your unified space to connect,\n'
                                  'create and collaborate seamlessly.',
                                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: context.sp(15).clamp(13.0, 17.0),
                                    height: 1.55,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                                    color: c.secondaryText,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),

                              const Spacer(flex: 3),

                              // ── Log In button → navigates to AccountLogin ──
                              SlideTransition(
                                position: _btn1Slide,
                                child: FadeTransition(
                                  opacity: _btn1Fade,
                                  child: _IosButton(
                                    label: 'Log In',
                                    icon: Icons.lock_open_rounded,
                                    isPrimary: true,
                                    onTap: () {
                                      Navigator.of(context).push(
                                        PageRouteBuilder(
                                          pageBuilder: (_, a, _) =>
                                              const AccountLogin(),
                                          transitionsBuilder:
                                              (
                                                _,
                                                a,
                                                _,
                                                child,
                                              ) => SlideTransition(
                                                position:
                                                    Tween<Offset>(
                                                      begin: const Offset(0, 1),
                                                      end: Offset.zero,
                                                    ).animate(
                                                      CurvedAnimation(
                                                        parent: a,
                                                        curve:
                                                            Curves.easeOutCubic,
                                                      ),
                                                    ),
                                                child: child,
                                              ),
                                          transitionDuration: const Duration(
                                            milliseconds: 420,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    },
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),

                              SizedBox(height: context.hp(14)),

                              // Create Account button → navigates to AccountCreation
                              SlideTransition(
                                position: _btn2Slide,
                                child: FadeTransition(
                                  opacity: _btn2Fade,
                                  child: _IosButton(
                                    label: 'Create Account',
                                    icon: Icons.person_add_rounded,
                                    isPrimary: false,
                                    onTap: () {
                                      Navigator.of(context).push(
                                        PageRouteBuilder(
                                          pageBuilder: (_, a, _) =>
                                              const AccountCreation(),
                                          transitionsBuilder:
                                              (
                                                _,
                                                a,
                                                _,
                                                child,
                                              ) => SlideTransition(
                                                position:
                                                    Tween<Offset>(
                                                      begin: const Offset(1, 0),
                                                      end: Offset.zero,
                                                    ).animate(
                                                      CurvedAnimation(
                                                        parent: a,
                                                        curve:
                                                            Curves.easeOutCubic,
                                                      ),
                                                    ),
                                                child: child,
                                              ),
                                          transitionDuration: const Duration(
                                            milliseconds: 400,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    },
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),

                              SizedBox(height: context.hp(22)),

                              // Terms line
                              FadeTransition(
                                opacity: _btn2Fade,
                                child: Text(
                                  'Thanks for choosing NexVibe.',
                                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: context.sp(11).clamp(9.0, 13.0),
                                    color: c.tertiaryText,
                                    height: 1.5,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),

                              SizedBox(height: context.hp(36)),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ACCOUNT LOGIN
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//  MODEL FOR A SAVED / LOGGED-IN ACCOUNT
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class _SavedAccount {
  final String uid;
  final String firstName;
  final String lastName;
  final String email;
  final List<Color> gradientColors;
  final int gradientIndex;
  final String? savedPassword;
  final String? profilePhotoUrl;
  final Timestamp? blueTickExpiry;

  const _SavedAccount({
    required this.uid,
    required this.firstName,
    required this.lastName,
    required this.email,
    required this.gradientColors,
    required this.gradientIndex,
    this.savedPassword,
    this.profilePhotoUrl,
    this.blueTickExpiry,
  });

  String get initials {
    final f = firstName.isNotEmpty ? firstName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final l = lastName.isNotEmpty ? lastName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    return '$f$l';
  }

  String get fullName => '$firstName $lastName'.trim();

  bool get hasActiveBlueTick {
    if (blueTickExpiry == null) return false;
    return blueTickExpiry!.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'uid': uid,
    'firstName': firstName,
    'lastName': lastName,
    'email': email,
    'gradientIndex': gradientIndex,
    if (savedPassword != null) 'savedPassword': savedPassword,
    if (profilePhotoUrl != null && profilePhotoUrl!.isNotEmpty)
      'profilePhotoUrl': profilePhotoUrl,
    if (blueTickExpiry != null)
      'blueTickExpiry': blueTickExpiry!.toDate().toIso8601String(),
  };

  factory _SavedAccount.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    final idx = (map['gradientIndex'] as int? ?? 0).clamp(
      0,
      _kGradientPalette.length - 1,
    );
    final rawPhoto = (map['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?)?.trim();
    Timestamp? expiry;
    final expiryStr = map['blueTickExpiry'] as String?;
    if (expiryStr != null) {
      try {
        expiry = Timestamp.fromDate(DateTime.parse(expiryStr));
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    return _SavedAccount(
      uid: map['uid'] as String? ?? '',
      firstName: (map['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim(),
      lastName: (map['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim(),
      email: (map['email'] as String? ?? '').trim(),
      gradientColors: List<Color>.from(_kGradientPalette[idx]),
      gradientIndex: idx,
      savedPassword: map['savedPassword'] as String?,
      profilePhotoUrl: rawPhoto != null && rawPhoto.isNotEmpty
          ? rawPhoto
          : null,
      blueTickExpiry: expiry,
    );
  }
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//  ACCOUNT LOGIN WIDGET
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class AccountLogin extends StatefulWidget {
  const AccountLogin({super.key});

  @override
  State<AccountLogin> createState() => _AccountLoginState();
}

class _AccountLoginState extends State<AccountLogin>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  final _emailCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _emailFocus = FocusNode();
  final _passwordFocus = FocusNode();

  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _savePassword = false;

  Timer? _emailDebounce;
  bool _isCheckingEmail = false;
  bool? _isEmailValid;

  late final AnimationController _entranceCtrl;
  late final Animation<double> _headerFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _headerSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _formFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _formSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _accountsFade;

  List<_SavedAccount> _savedAccounts = [];
  final List<StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot>> _accountSubscriptions = [];

  static const _kAccountsKey = 'nexvibe_saved_accounts_v1';

  bool get _canLogin =>
      _isEmailValid == true &&
      _emailCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty &&
      _passwordCtrl.text.isNotEmpty;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _entranceCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900),
    );

    _headerFade = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.00, 0.40, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _headerSlide = Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.30), end: Offset.zero)
        .animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _entranceCtrl,
            curve: const Interval(0.00, 0.40, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
          ),
        );
    _formFade = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.20, 0.65, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _formSlide = Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.25), end: Offset.zero)
        .animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _entranceCtrl,
            curve: const Interval(0.20, 0.65, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
          ),
        );
    _accountsFade = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.50, 1.00, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (mounted) _entranceCtrl.forward();
    });

    _emailCtrl.addListener(_onEmailChanged);
    _passwordCtrl.addListener(_onFieldChanged);

    _loadSavedAccounts();
  }

  void _onEmailChanged() {
    final email = _emailCtrl.text.trim();
    if (_savedAccounts.any((a) => a.email == email)) {
      setState(() {
        _isEmailValid = true;
        _isCheckingEmail = false;
      });
      if (_emailDebounce?.isActive ?? false) _emailDebounce!.cancel();
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isEmailValid = null);
    if (_emailDebounce?.isActive ?? false) _emailDebounce!.cancel();

    if (email.isEmpty) return;

    _emailDebounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 600), () {
      _checkEmailExists(email);
    });
  }

  Future<void> _checkEmailExists(String email) async {
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _isCheckingEmail = true);

    try {
      final snap1 = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .where('email', isEqualTo: email)
          .limit(1)
          .get();
      final snap2 = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .where('email', isEqualTo: email.toLowerCase())
          .limit(1)
          .get();

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isEmailValid = snap1.docs.isNotEmpty || snap2.docs.isNotEmpty;
        _isCheckingEmail = false;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isCheckingEmail = false;
        _isEmailValid = null;
      });
    }
  }

  void _onFieldChanged() => setState(() {});

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailDebounce?.cancel();
    _emailCtrl.dispose();
    _passwordCtrl.dispose();
    _emailFocus.dispose();
    _passwordFocus.dispose();
    _entranceCtrl.dispose();
    for (final sub in _accountSubscriptions) {
      sub.cancel();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadSavedAccounts() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final raw = prefs.getString(_kAccountsKey);
      if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty) return;
      final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
      if (decoded is! List) return;
      final list = decoded
          .whereType<Map<String, dynamic>>()
          .map(_SavedAccount.fromJson)
          .toList();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _savedAccounts = list);
      _setupRealTimeListeners();
    } catch (_) {
      // Silently ignore any read/parse error
    }
  }

  void _setupRealTimeListeners() {
    for (final sub in _accountSubscriptions) {
      sub.cancel();
    }
    _accountSubscriptions.clear();

    for (int i = 0; i < _savedAccounts.length; i++) {
      final account = _savedAccounts[i];
      if (account.uid.isEmpty) continue;
      final docRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(account.uid);
      final sub = docRef.snapshots().listen((snapshot) {
        if (!snapshot.exists) return;
        final data = snapshot.data()!;
        final updatedFirstName = (data['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
        final updatedLastName = (data['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
        final updatedPhoto = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?)?.trim();
        final updatedExpiry = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;

        bool changed = false;
        if (account.firstName != updatedFirstName) changed = true;
        if (account.lastName != updatedLastName) changed = true;
        if (account.profilePhotoUrl != updatedPhoto) changed = true;
        if (account.blueTickExpiry != updatedExpiry) changed = true;

        if (!changed) return;

        final index = _savedAccounts.indexWhere((a) => a.uid == account.uid);
        if (index == -1) return;

        final updatedAccount = _SavedAccount(
          uid: account.uid,
          firstName: updatedFirstName,
          lastName: updatedLastName,
          email: account.email,
          gradientColors: account.gradientColors,
          gradientIndex: account.gradientIndex,
          savedPassword: account.savedPassword,
          profilePhotoUrl: updatedPhoto != null && updatedPhoto.isNotEmpty
              ? updatedPhoto
              : null,
          blueTickExpiry: updatedExpiry,
        );

        setState(() {
          _savedAccounts[index] = updatedAccount;
        });
        _persistAccounts(_savedAccounts);
      });
      _accountSubscriptions.add(sub);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _persistAccounts(List<_SavedAccount> accounts) async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final encoded = jsonEncode(accounts.map((a) => a.toJson()).toList());
      await prefs.setString(_kAccountsKey, encoded);
    } catch (_) {
      // Silently ignore write errors
    }
  }

  Future<void> _upsertAccount(_SavedAccount incoming) async {
    final updated = List<_SavedAccount>.from(_savedAccounts)
      ..removeWhere((a) => a.email == incoming.email)
      ..insert(0, incoming);
    if (updated.length > 10) updated.removeLast();
    setState(() => _savedAccounts = updated);
    await _persistAccounts(updated);
    _setupRealTimeListeners();
  }

  String _friendlyError(String code) {
    switch (code) {
      case 'user-not-found':
        return 'No account found with this email.';
      case 'wrong-password':
        return 'Incorrect password. Please try again.';
      case 'invalid-email':
        return 'Please enter a valid email address.';
      case 'user-disabled':
        return 'This account has been disabled. Contact support.';
      case 'too-many-requests':
        return 'Too many attempts. Please wait a moment and try again.';
      case 'network-request-failed':
        return 'Network error. Check your connection and try again.';
      case 'invalid-credential':
        return 'Email or password is incorrect. Please try again.';
      default:
        return 'Sign in failed. Please try again.';
    }
  }

  void _showErrorSnack(String message) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..clearSnackBars()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          margin: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
            context.sp(20),
            0,
            context.sp(20),
            context.hp(32),
          ),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),
          content: Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(
                CupertinoIcons.exclamationmark_circle_fill,
                color: Colors.white,
                size: 18,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Flexible(
                child: Text(
                  message,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
  }

  Future<void> _handleLogin() async {
    if (!_canLogin || _isLoading) return;
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final emailEntered = _emailCtrl.text.trim();
      final snap1 = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .where('email', isEqualTo: emailEntered)
          .limit(1)
          .get();
      final snap2 = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .where('email', isEqualTo: emailEntered.toLowerCase())
          .limit(1)
          .get();

      if (snap1.docs.isEmpty && snap2.docs.isEmpty) {
        if (!mounted) return;
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
        _showErrorSnack('No account found with this email.');
        return;
      }

      final credential = await FirebaseAuth.instance.signInWithEmailAndPassword(
        email: _emailCtrl.text.trim(),
        password: _passwordCtrl.text,
      );

      final uid = credential.user!.uid;
      final email = _emailCtrl.text.trim();

      String firstName = '';
      String lastName = '';
      String? profilePhotoUrl;
      Timestamp? blueTickExpiry;
      try {
        final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
            .collection('users')
            .doc(uid)
            .get();
        if (doc.exists) {
          final data = doc.data() ?? {};
          firstName = (data['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
          lastName = (data['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
          final p = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?)?.trim();
          profilePhotoUrl = p != null && p.isNotEmpty ? p : null;
          blueTickExpiry = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
        }
      } catch (_) {
        firstName = email.split('@').first;
      }

      final gradIdx = _gradientIndexForEmail(email);
      await _upsertAccount(
        _SavedAccount(
          uid: uid,
          firstName: firstName,
          lastName: lastName,
          email: email,
          gradientColors: List<Color>.from(_kGradientPalette[gradIdx]),
          gradientIndex: gradIdx,
          savedPassword: _savePassword ? _passwordCtrl.text : null,
          profilePhotoUrl: profilePhotoUrl,
          blueTickExpiry: blueTickExpiry,
        ),
      );

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);

      Navigator.of(context).pushAndRemoveUntil(
        PageRouteBuilder(
          pageBuilder: (_, a, _) => const MyHomePage(),
          transitionsBuilder: (_, a, _, child) => FadeTransition(
            opacity: CurvedAnimation(parent: a, curve: Curves.easeOut),
            child: child,
          ),
          transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
        ),
        (route) => false,
      );
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      _showErrorSnack(_friendlyError(e.code));
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      _showErrorSnack('Something went wrong. Please try again.');
    }
  }

  void _loginWithSavedAccount(_SavedAccount account) {
    _emailCtrl.text = account.email;
    if (account.savedPassword != null) {
      _passwordCtrl.text = account.savedPassword!;
      _savePassword = true;
      setState(() {});
      _handleLogin();
    } else {
      _passwordCtrl.clear();
      _savePassword = false;
      setState(() {});
      _passwordFocus.requestFocus();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _confirmRemoveAccount(_SavedAccount account) async {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    final result = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (ctx) => Container(
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(
          left: context.sp(16),
          right: context.sp(16),
          bottom: context.sp(16) + MediaQuery.of(ctx).viewInsets.bottom,
        ),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(16)),
        ),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Container(
              margin: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: context.sp(12)),
              width: 40,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isDark ? Colors.white24 : Colors.black12,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
              ),
            ),
            Text(
              'Remove Account',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: context.sp(18),
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: context.sp(12)),
            Text(
              'Are you sure to remove ${account.fullName} from saved accounts?',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
