              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: context.sp(15),
                color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : Colors.black54,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: context.sp(24)),
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: CupertinoButton(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: context.sp(14)),
                    color: isDark ? Colors.white12 : Colors.black12,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(false),
                    child: Text(
                      'Cancel',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(width: context.sp(12)),
                Expanded(
                  child: CupertinoButton(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: context.sp(14)),
                    color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(true),
                    child: const Text(
                      'Yes',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            SizedBox(height: context.sp(8)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (result == true && mounted) {
      await _removeSavedAccount(account);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _removeSavedAccount(_SavedAccount account) async {
    final updated = List<_SavedAccount>.from(_savedAccounts)..remove(account);
    setState(() => _savedAccounts = updated);
    await _persistAccounts(updated);
    _setupRealTimeListeners();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    final hPad = context.sp(24).clamp(20.0, 36.0);

    final bgColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF000000) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final cardColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
        : const Color(0xFFFFFFFF);
    final separatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA);
    final labelColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFAEAEB2)
        : const Color(0xFF8E8E93);
    final primaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
        : const Color(0xFF000000);
    final secondaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value: (isDark ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
          .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: PopScope(
        canPop: false,
        onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {
          if (didPop) return;
          final nav = Navigator.of(context);
          if (nav.canPop()) {
            nav.pop();
          } else {
            nav.pushReplacement(
              PageRouteBuilder(
                pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => const EntryScreen(),
                transitionDuration: Duration.zero,
              ),
            );
          }
        },
        child: Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: bgColor,
          body: SafeArea(
            child: GestureDetector(
              onTap: () => FocusScope.of(context).unfocus(),
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                // Bouncing physics removed – now uses default platform scroll physics
                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: hPad),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(16)),
                      // Back button
                      GestureDetector(
                        onTap: () {
                          final nav = Navigator.of(context);
                          if (nav.canPop()) {
                            nav.pop();
                          } else {
                            nav.pushReplacement(
                              PageRouteBuilder(
                                pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => const EntryScreen(),
                                transitionDuration: Duration.zero,
                              ),
                            );
                          }
                        },
                        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            vertical: context.sp(4),
                          ),
                          child: Row(
                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                            children: [
                              Icon(
                                CupertinoIcons.back,
                                color: iosBlue,
                                size: context.sp(20),
                              ),
                              SizedBox(width: context.sp(4)),
                              Text(
                                'Back',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: iosBlue,
                                  fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(32)),
                      // Heading
                      SlideTransition(
                        position: _headerSlide,
                        child: FadeTransition(
                          opacity: _headerFade,
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                'Sign In',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: context.sp(34).clamp(28.0, 40.0),
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                  letterSpacing: -1.0,
                                  color: primaryText,
                                ),
                              ),
                              SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),
                              Text(
                                'Enter your email and password\n'
                                'to access your NexVibe account.',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: context.sp(15).clamp(13.0, 17.0),
                                  color: secondaryText,
                                  height: 1.45,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(32)),
                      // Form card
                      SlideTransition(
                        position: _formSlide,
                        child: FadeTransition(
                          opacity: _formFade,
                          child: _buildFormCard(
                            cardColor: cardColor,
                            separatorColor: separatorColor,
                            labelColor: labelColor,
                            primaryText: primaryText,
                            iosBlue: iosBlue,
                            isDark: isDark,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(14)),
                      Align(
                        alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                        child: GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {},
                          child: Text(
                            'Forgot Password?',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: iosBlue,
                              fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Transform.scale(
                            scale: 0.8,
                            child: CupertinoSwitch(
                              value: _savePassword,
                              onChanged: _passwordCtrl.text.isNotEmpty
                                  ? (val) => setState(() => _savePassword = val)
                                  : null,
                              activeTrackColor: iosBlue,
                            ),
                          ),
                          SizedBox(width: context.sp(6)),
                          Text(
                            'Remember password',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: secondaryText,
                              fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(28)),
                      // Log In button
                      _buildLoginButton(
                        iosBlue: iosBlue,
                        enabled: _canLogin,
                        isLoading: _isLoading,
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(44)),
                      // Saved accounts
                      if (_savedAccounts.isNotEmpty)
                        FadeTransition(
                          opacity: _accountsFade,
                          child: _buildSavedAccountsSection(
                            cardColor: cardColor,
                            labelColor: labelColor,
                            primaryText: primaryText,
                            secondaryText: secondaryText,
                            separatorColor: separatorColor,
                            isDark: isDark,
                          ),
                        ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(48)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFormCard({
    required Color cardColor,
    required Color separatorColor,
    required Color labelColor,
    required Color primaryText,
    required Color iosBlue,
    required bool isDark,
  }) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(context.sp(14).clamp(10.0, 16.0)),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: isDark
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.30)
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.06),
            blurRadius: 22,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          _IosTextField(
            controller: _emailCtrl,
            focusNode: _emailFocus,
            label: 'Email',
            hint: 'you@gmail.com',
            keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
            textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
            onSubmitted: (_) {
              if (_isEmailValid == true) _passwordFocus.requestFocus();
            },
            labelColor: labelColor,
            primaryText: primaryText,
            isDark: isDark,
            prefixIcon: CupertinoIcons.mail,
            iosBlue: iosBlue,
            suffixWidget: Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(16)),
              child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                child: _isCheckingEmail
                    ? SizedBox(
                        key: const ValueKey('loading'),
                        width: context.sp(16),
                        height: context.sp(16),
                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                          strokeWidth: 2,
                          color: iosBlue,
                        ),
                      )
                    : _isEmailValid == true
                    ? Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.check_mark_circled_solid,
                        key: const ValueKey('valid'),
                        color: const Color(0xFF34C759),
                        size: context.sp(20),
                      )
                    : _isEmailValid == false
                    ? Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.xmark_circle_fill,
                        key: const ValueKey('invalid'),
                        color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                        size: context.sp(20),
                      )
                    : const SizedBox.shrink(key: ValueKey('none')),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          if (_emailCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty && _isEmailValid != null)
            Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                left: context.sp(52),
                right: context.sp(16),
                bottom: context.sp(8),
              ),
              child: Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                child: Text(
                  _isEmailValid == true
                      ? 'Email exists'
                      : 'Email doesn\'t exist, create account first',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: context.sp(12),
                    color: _isEmailValid == true
                        ? const Color(0xFF34C759)
                        : const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: context.sp(52)),
            child: Divider(height: 1, thickness: 0.5, color: separatorColor),
          ),
          _IosTextField(
            controller: _passwordCtrl,
            focusNode: _passwordFocus,
            label: 'Password',
            hint: 'Enter password',
            obscureText: _obscurePassword,
            textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
            onSubmitted: (_) => _handleLogin(),
            labelColor: labelColor,
            primaryText: primaryText,
            isDark: isDark,
            prefixIcon: CupertinoIcons.lock,
            iosBlue: iosBlue,
            enabled: _isEmailValid == true,
            suffixWidget: GestureDetector(
              onTap: () => setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(16)),
                child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                  duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),
                  child: Icon(
                    _obscurePassword
                        ? CupertinoIcons.eye
                        : CupertinoIcons.eye_slash,
                    key: ValueKey(_obscurePassword),
                    color: labelColor,
                    size: context.sp(20),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLoginButton({
    required Color iosBlue,
    required bool enabled,
    required bool isLoading,
  }) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: double.infinity,
      height: context.hp(52).clamp(48.0, 58.0),
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: enabled && !isLoading ? _handleLogin : null,
        child: AnimatedContainer(
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              colors: enabled
                  ? [iosBlue, const Color(0xFF5AC8FA)]
                  : [
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      iosBlue.withOpacity(0.5),
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      const Color(0xFF5AC8FA).withOpacity(0.5),
                    ],
              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            ),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
              context.sp(14).clamp(10.0, 16.0),
            ),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                color: iosBlue.withOpacity(enabled ? 0.35 : 0.15),
                blurRadius: 18,
                offset: const Offset(0, 6),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Center(
            child: isLoading
                ? const SizedBox(
                    width: 22,
                    height: 22,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      strokeWidth: 2.5,
                    ),
                  )
                : Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      const Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.person_crop_circle_fill_badge_checkmark,
                        color: Colors.white,
                        size: 18,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(
                        'Authenticate',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          letterSpacing: -0.3,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSavedAccountsSection({
    required Color cardColor,
    required Color labelColor,
    required Color primaryText,
    required Color secondaryText,
    required Color separatorColor,
    required bool isDark,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: context.sp(4), bottom: context.hp(10)),
          child: Text(
            'SAVED ACCOUNTS',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: context.sp(12).clamp(10.0, 14.0),
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              color: labelColor,
              letterSpacing: 0.6,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: cardColor,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
              context.sp(14).clamp(10.0, 16.0),
            ),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: isDark
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.30)
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.06),
                blurRadius: 22,
                offset: const Offset(0, 4),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: List.generate(_savedAccounts.length, (i) {
              final account = _savedAccounts[i];
              final isLast = i == _savedAccounts.length - 1;
              return Column(
                children: [
                  _SavedAccountTile(
                    account: account,
                    primaryText: primaryText,
                    secondaryText: secondaryText,
                    onTap: () => _loginWithSavedAccount(account),
                    onRemove: () => _confirmRemoveAccount(account),
                  ),
                  if (!isLast)
                    Padding(
                      padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: context.sp(74)),
                      child: Divider(
                        height: 1,
                        thickness: 0.5,
                        color: separatorColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              );
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//  IOS-STYLE TEXT FIELD ROW
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class _IosTextField extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final FocusNode focusNode;
  final String label;
  final String hint;
  final bool obscureText;
  final TextInputType? keyboardType;
  final TextInputAction? textInputAction;
  final ValueChanged<String>? onSubmitted;
  final Color labelColor;
  final Color primaryText;
  final bool isDark;
  final IconData prefixIcon;
  final Color iosBlue;
  final Widget? suffixWidget;
  final bool enabled;

  const _IosTextField({
    required this.controller,
    required this.focusNode,
    required this.label,
    required this.hint,
    this.obscureText = false,
    this.keyboardType,
    this.textInputAction,
    this.onSubmitted,
    required this.labelColor,
    required this.primaryText,
    required this.isDark,
    required this.prefixIcon,
    required this.iosBlue,
    this.suffixWidget,
    this.enabled = true,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      height: context.hp(54).clamp(50.0, 62.0),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          SizedBox(width: context.sp(16)),
          Icon(prefixIcon, color: labelColor, size: context.sp(20)),
          SizedBox(width: context.sp(12)),
          Expanded(
            child: TextField(
              controller: controller,
              focusNode: focusNode,
              obscureText: obscureText,
              keyboardType: keyboardType,
              textInputAction: textInputAction,
              onSubmitted: onSubmitted,
              enabled: enabled,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: primaryText,
                fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
              ),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                labelText: label,
                labelStyle: TextStyle(
                  color: labelColor,
                  fontSize: context.sp(15).clamp(13.0, 17.0),
                ),
                hintText: hint,
                hintStyle: TextStyle(
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  color: labelColor.withOpacity(0.45),
                  fontSize: context.sp(15).clamp(13.0, 17.0),
                ),
                border: InputBorder.none,
                isDense: true,
                disabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
              ),
              cursorColor: iosBlue,
            ),
          ),
          ?suffixWidget,
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//  SAVED ACCOUNT LIST TILE
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class _SavedAccountTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final _SavedAccount account;
  final Color primaryText;
  final Color secondaryText;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final VoidCallback onRemove;

  const _SavedAccountTile({
    required this.account,
    required this.primaryText,
    required this.secondaryText,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.onRemove,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final avatarSize = context.sp(46).clamp(40.0, 54.0);
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: context.sp(16),
          vertical: context.sp(11),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            _GradientAvatar(
              initials: account.initials,
              gradientColors: account.gradientColors,
              size: avatarSize,
              fontSize: context.sp(16).clamp(13.0, 18.0),
              photoUrl: account.profilePhotoUrl,
            ),
            SizedBox(width: context.sp(14)),
            Expanded(
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Flexible(
                    child: Text(
                      account.fullName,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: primaryText,
                        fontSize: context.sp(15).clamp(13.0, 17.0),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      ),
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (account.hasActiveBlueTick) ...[
                    SizedBox(width: context.sp(6)),
                    Icon(
                      CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                      color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                      size: context.sp(16),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: onRemove,
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(6)),
                child: Container(
                  width: context.sp(26),
                  height: context.sp(26),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    color: secondaryText.withOpacity(0.12),
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  ),
                  child: Icon(
                    CupertinoIcons.xmark,
                    color: secondaryText,
                    size: context.sp(12),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  MY HOME PAGE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class MyHomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const MyHomePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<MyHomePage> createState() => _MyHomePageState();
}

class _MyHomePageState extends State<MyHomePage> with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  int _currentTab = 0;

  // ── Logged-in user info (loaded from SharedPreferences via email lookup) ───
  String _firstName = '';
  String _lastName = '';
  String _profilePhotoUrl = '';
  bool _isLoadingPhoto = true;
  List<Color> _avatarGradient = [
    const Color(0xFF007AFF),
    const Color(0xFF5AC8FA),
  ];

  // Tab-switch fade controller
  late final AnimationController _tabFadeCtrl;
  late final Animation<double> _tabFade;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _tabFadeCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),
    )..value = 1.0;

    _tabFade = CurvedAnimation(parent: _tabFadeCtrl, curve: Curves.easeOut);

    _loadCurrentUser();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabFadeCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // ── Load name + gradient from SharedPreferences + photo URL from Firestore ─
  Future<void> _loadCurrentUser() async {
    final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    if (user == null) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoadingPhoto = false);
      return;
    }

    final email = (user.email ?? '').trim();
    bool foundInPrefs = false;

    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final raw = prefs.getString('nexvibe_saved_accounts_v1');
      if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
        final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
        if (decoded is List) {
          for (final item in decoded) {
            if (item is Map<String, dynamic> && item['email'] == email) {
              final account = _SavedAccount.fromJson(item);
              if (!mounted) return;
              setState(() {
                _firstName = account.firstName;
                _lastName = account.lastName;
                _avatarGradient = account.gradientColors;
                final p = account.profilePhotoUrl;
                if (p != null && p.isNotEmpty) {
                  _profilePhotoUrl = p;
                  _isLoadingPhoto = false;
                }
              });
              foundInPrefs = true;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
      /* ignore */
    }

    // Fallback: use email prefix if not found in prefs
    if (!foundInPrefs) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _firstName = email.split('@').first;
        _lastName = '';
        _avatarGradient = _gradientForEmail(email);
      });
    }

    if (!mounted) return;

    // Load profile photo URL from Firestore
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(user.uid)
          .get();
      if (doc.exists && mounted) {
        final photoUrl = (doc.data()?['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
        setState(() {
          _profilePhotoUrl = photoUrl;
          _isLoadingPhoto = false;
        });
      } else if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoadingPhoto = false);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoadingPhoto = false);
    }
  }

  // Generates a deterministic gradient based on email
  List<Color> _gradientForEmail(String email) {
    final hash = email.hashCode.abs();
    final hue1 = (hash % 360).toDouble();
    final hue2 = ((hash * 7) % 360).toDouble();
    return [
      HSLColor.fromAHSL(1.0, hue1, 0.8, 0.55).toColor(),
      HSLColor.fromAHSL(1.0, hue2, 0.7, 0.6).toColor(),
    ];
  }

  // Fetch the number of pending requests for the red badge
  Stream<int> _getPendingRequestsCount(String uid) {
    return FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('connections')
        .where('participants', arrayContains: uid)
        .snapshots()
        .map(
          (snap) => snap.docs.where((doc) {
            final data = doc.data();
            return data['status'] == 'pending' && data['receiverId'] == uid;
          }).length,
        );
  }

  // Store concatenated initials
  String get _initials {
    final f = _firstName.isNotEmpty ? _firstName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final l = _lastName.isNotEmpty ? _lastName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final result = '$f$l';
    return result.isNotEmpty ? result : '?';
  }

  void _switchTab(int index) {
    if (index == _currentTab) return;
    _tabFadeCtrl.reverse().then((_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _currentTab = index);
      _tabFadeCtrl.forward();
    });
  }

  void _openMyProfile() {
    Navigator.of(context)
        .push(
          PageRouteBuilder(
            pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => const MyProfile(),
            transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
              position:
                  Tween<Offset>(
                    begin: const Offset(1, 0),
                    end: Offset.zero,
                  ).animate(
                    CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
                  ),
              child: child,
            ),
            transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 380),
          ),
        )
        .then((_) {
          if (mounted) _loadCurrentUser();
        });
  }

  void _openUserSearch() {
    final currentUid = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '';
    if (currentUid.isEmpty) return;
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => _UserSearchScreen(
          currentUserId: currentUid,
          isDark: context.isDark,
        ),
        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
          position: Tween<Offset>(
            begin: const Offset(0, 1),
            end: Offset.zero,
          ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic)),
          child: child,
        ),
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;

    // iOS colour tokens
    final bgColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF000000) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final cardColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
        : const Color(0xFFFFFFFF);
    final separatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA);
    final navSeparatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final secondaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    final navBarBg = isDark
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E).withOpacity(0.94)
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        : Colors.white.withOpacity(0.94);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    final currentUid = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '';

    return PopScope(
      canPop: false,
      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {
        if (!didPop) SystemNavigator.pop();
      },
      child: AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
        value: (isDark ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
            .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
        child: Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: bgColor,
          body: Column(
            children: [
              // ── Custom nav bar ─────────────────────────────────────────────
              _buildNavBar(
                isDark: isDark,
                navBarBg: bgColor,
                separatorColor: navSeparatorColor,
                secondaryText: secondaryText,
                currentUid: currentUid,
              ),

              // ── Tab content ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
              Expanded(
                child: FadeTransition(
                  opacity: _tabFade,
                  child: IndexedStack(
                    index: _currentTab,
                    children: [
                      _ChatsTab(
                        bgColor: bgColor,
                        cardColor: cardColor,
                        separatorColor: separatorColor,
                        secondaryText: secondaryText,
                        isDark: isDark,
                        currentUserId: currentUid,
                      ),
                      _GroupTab(
                        bgColor: bgColor,
                        cardColor: cardColor,
                        secondaryText: secondaryText,
                        isDark: isDark,
                      ),
                      _JobTab(
                        bgColor: bgColor,
                        cardColor: cardColor,
                        secondaryText: secondaryText,
                        isDark: isDark,
                      ),
                      _buildAlertTab(
                        bgColor: bgColor,
                        cardColor: cardColor,
                        secondaryText: secondaryText,
                        isDark: isDark,
                      ),
                      _SettingsTab(
                        bgColor: bgColor,
                        cardColor: cardColor,
                        secondaryText: secondaryText,
                        isDark: isDark,
                        currentUserId: currentUid,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),

              // ── iOS-style bottom navigation bar ────────────────────────────
              _buildBottomNavBar(
                isDark: isDark,
                navBarBg: navBarBg,
                separatorColor: navSeparatorColor,
                secondaryText: secondaryText,
                iosBlue: iosBlue,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── Nav bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildNavBar({
    required bool isDark,
    required Color navBarBg,
    required Color separatorColor,
    required Color secondaryText,
    required String currentUid,
  }) {
    final statusH = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top;
    const navH = 52.0;

    return Container(
      height: statusH + navH,
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: statusH),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: navBarBg,
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5)),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(16)),
        child: Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            // App brand – now using iOS system font
            Text(
              'NexVibe',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontFamily: '.SF Pro Display', // iOS system font
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                fontSize: context.sp(25).clamp(25.0, 34.0),
                letterSpacing: -0.8,
                color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
              ),
            ),

            const Spacer(),

            // Friend Requests icon
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: () {
                if (currentUid.isEmpty) return;
                Navigator.of(context).push(
                  PageRouteBuilder(
                    pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => _RequestsScreen(
                      currentUserId: currentUid,
                      isDark: isDark,
                    ),
                    transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
                      position:
                          Tween<Offset>(
                            begin: const Offset(0, 1),
                            end: Offset.zero,
                          ).animate(
                            CurvedAnimation(
                              parent: anim,
                              curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                            ),
                          ),
                      child: child,
                    ),
                    transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                  ),
                );
              },
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(13)),
                child: Stack(
                  clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      CupertinoIcons.person_badge_plus_fill,
                      size: context.sp(22).clamp(20.0, 26.0),
                      color: secondaryText,
                    ),
                    if (currentUid.isNotEmpty)
                      StreamBuilder<int>(
                        stream: _getPendingRequestsCount(currentUid),
                        builder: (context, snapshot) {
                          final count = snapshot.data ?? 0;
                          if (count == 0) return const SizedBox.shrink();
                          return Positioned(
                            right: -4,
                            top: -4,
                            child: Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                                color: Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                count > 9 ? '9+' : '$count',
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                  fontSize: 10,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // Search icon
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: _openUserSearch,
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(13)),
                child: Icon(
                  CupertinoIcons.search,
                  size: context.sp(22).clamp(20.0, 26.0),
                  color: secondaryText,
                ),
              ),
            ),

            SizedBox(width: context.sp(8)),

            // Profile avatar with iOS loader
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: _openMyProfile,
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              child: _GradientAvatar(
                initials: _initials,
                gradientColors: _avatarGradient,
                size: 34,
                fontSize: context.sp(11).clamp(9.0, 13.0),
                photoUrl: _profilePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty ? _profilePhotoUrl : null,
                isLoading: _isLoadingPhoto,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── Alert tab ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildAlertTab({
    required Color bgColor,
    required Color cardColor,
    required Color secondaryText,
    required bool isDark,
  }) {
    final primaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
        : const Color(0xFF000000);
    const accentColor = Color(0xFFFF9500);

    return Container(
      color: bgColor,
      child: SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                context.sp(16),
                context.sp(12),
                context.sp(16),
                0,
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'Alerts',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: primaryText,
                      fontSize: context.sp(22).clamp(20.0, 26.0),
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      letterSpacing: -0.4,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),
            Expanded(
              child: Center(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(40)),
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        width: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
                        height: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          color: cardColor,
                          boxShadow: [
                            BoxShadow(
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              color: accentColor.withOpacity(
                                isDark ? 0.18 : 0.12,
                              ),
                              blurRadius: 24,
                              spreadRadius: 2,
                              offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                            ),
                            BoxShadow(
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              color: Colors.black.withOpacity(
                                isDark ? 0.20 : 0.06,
                              ),
                              blurRadius: 12,
                              offset: const Offset(0, 3),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        child: Icon(
                          CupertinoIcons.bell_fill,
                          size: context.sp(30).clamp(24.0, 38.0),
                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                          color: accentColor.withOpacity(0.55),
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(22)),
                      Text(
                        'No Alerts',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: primaryText,
                          fontSize: context.sp(19).clamp(16.0, 22.0),
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          letterSpacing: -0.4,
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),
                      Text(
                        'Notifications and alerts will appear here.',
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: secondaryText,
                          fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                          height: 1.5,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── iOS-style bottom navigation bar ───────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildBottomNavBar({
    required bool isDark,
    required Color navBarBg,
    required Color separatorColor,
    required Color secondaryText,
    required Color iosBlue,
  }) {
    final tabs = [
      {'icon': CupertinoIcons.chat_bubble_2_fill, 'label': 'Chats'},
      {'icon': CupertinoIcons.person_2_fill, 'label': 'Groups'},
      {'icon': CupertinoIcons.briefcase_fill, 'label': 'Jobs'},
      {'icon': CupertinoIcons.bell_fill, 'label': 'Alerts'},
      {'icon': CupertinoIcons.gear_alt_fill, 'label': 'Settings'},
    ];

    return ClipRect(
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 20, sigmaY: 20),
        child: Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: navBarBg,
            border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5)),
          ),
          child: SafeArea(
            top: false,
            child: Padding(
              // Increased vertical padding for a slightly taller bottom bar
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: context.sp(8),
                vertical: context.sp(10), // was 6, now taller
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: List.generate(tabs.length, (i) {
                  final isActive = i == _currentTab;
                  final item = tabs[i];

                  return Expanded(
                    child: GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => _switchTab(i),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: AnimatedContainer(
                        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                        curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: context.sp(6)),
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            AnimatedScale(
                              scale: isActive ? 1.12 : 1.0,
                              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                              child: Icon(
                                item['icon'] as IconData,
                                size: context.sp(24).clamp(20.0, 28.0),
                                color: isActive ? iosBlue : secondaryText,
                              ),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(
                              height: context.hp(4),
                            ), // slightly increased spacing
                            AnimatedDefaultTextStyle(
                              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: isActive ? iosBlue : secondaryText,
                                fontSize: context
                                    .sp(11)
                                    .clamp(10.0, 13.0), // slightly larger label
                                fontWeight: isActive
                                    ? FontWeight.w600
                                    : FontWeight.w400,
                              ),
                              child: Text(item['label'] as String),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Helper Widget: _ActionCard (Enhanced – gradient, elevated, glossy effect)
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
class _ActionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final Color accentColor;
  final Color cardColor;
  final Color primaryText;
  final Color secondaryText;
  final bool isDark;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _ActionCard({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.accentColor,
    required this.cardColor,
    required this.primaryText,
    required this.secondaryText,
    required this.isDark,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Expanded(
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Container(
          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            vertical: context.sp(14),
            horizontal: context.sp(8),
          ),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
              colors: isDark
                  ? [
                      cardColor,
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      cardColor.withOpacity(0.9),
                    ]
                  : [
                      cardColor,
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      cardColor.withOpacity(0.95),
                    ],
            ),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: isDark
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.3)
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.06),
                blurRadius: 10,
                offset: const Offset(0, 4),
              ),
              BoxShadow(
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                color: accentColor.withOpacity(isDark ? 0.1 : 0.05),
                blurRadius: 8,
                offset: const Offset(0, 2),
              ),
            ],
            border: Border.all(
              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
              color: accentColor.withOpacity(isDark ? 0.2 : 0.15),
              width: 1.2,
            ),
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              // Advanced icon with circular background
              Container(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(8)),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  color: accentColor.withOpacity(0.12),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  icon,
                  size: context.sp(24).clamp(20.0, 28.0),
                  color: accentColor,
                ),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: context.hp(12)),
              Text(
                label,
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: primaryText,
                  fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  letterSpacing: -0.2,
                ),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: context.hp(6)),
              Text(
                _getSubtitle(),
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: secondaryText,
                  fontSize: context.sp(11).clamp(10.0, 13.0),
                  height: 1.3,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _getSubtitle() {
    switch (label) {
      case 'Create Jobs':
        return 'Post an opening';
      case 'Search Jobs':
        return 'Find opportunities';
      case 'Provide Services':
        return 'Offer your skills';
      case 'Search Services':
        return 'Find best services';
      default:
        return '';
    }
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CREATE JOB SCREEN
//  (Navigation bar height 40, title left‑aligned, real‑time profile card,
//   Blue Tick status, and Create Vacancy button)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class CreateJob extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool isDark;

  const CreateJob({super.key, required this.isDark});

  @override
  State<CreateJob> createState() => _CreateJobState();
}

class _CreateJobState extends State<CreateJob> {
  String _firstName = '';
  String _lastName = '';
  String _profilePhotoUrl = '';
  bool _hasBlueTick = false;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  late StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot>? _userSub;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    if (user != null) {
      _userSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(user.uid)
          .snapshots()
          .listen(
            (doc) {
              if (!mounted) return;
              if (doc.exists) {
                final data = doc.data()!;
                final expiry = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
                final active =
                    expiry != null && expiry.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
                setState(() {
                  _firstName = (data['firstName'] as String?) ?? '';
                  _lastName = (data['lastName'] as String?) ?? '';
                  _profilePhotoUrl = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
                  _hasBlueTick = active;
                  _isLoading = false;
                });
              } else {
                setState(() => _isLoading = false);
              }
            },
            onError: (_) {
              if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
            },
          );
    } else {
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _userSub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  String get _displayName => '$_firstName $_lastName'.trim();
  String get _initials {
    final f = _firstName.isNotEmpty ? _firstName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final l = _lastName.isNotEmpty ? _lastName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final res = '$f$l';
    return res.isNotEmpty ? res : '?';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final primaryText = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
        : const Color(0xFF000000);
    final secondaryText = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    final bgColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF000000)
        : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final cardColor = widget.isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
    final separatorColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    const accentColor = Color(0xFFAF52DE); // purple accent for jobs
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value:
          (widget.isDark
                  ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light
                  : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
              .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        body: Column(
          children: [
            // Custom navigation bar (height = 40 + top padding)
            Container(
              height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 40,
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: bgColor,
                border: Border(
                  bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 8,
                          vertical: 8,
                        ),
                        child: Icon(
                          CupertinoIcons.back,
                          color: iosBlue,
                          size: context.sp(20),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text(
                      'Create Vacancy',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: primaryText,
                        fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        letterSpacing: -0.2,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    // Placeholder for symmetry
                    const SizedBox(width: 44),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // Content
            Expanded(
              child: SafeArea(
                top: false,
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: context.sp(16),
                    vertical: context.sp(20),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      // Enhanced Profile Card Dashboard
                      _buildProfileCard(
                        context,
                        primaryText: primaryText,
                        secondaryText: secondaryText,
                        cardColor: cardColor,
                        accentColor: accentColor,
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(32)),

                      // Create Vacancy button
                      Center(
                        child: CupertinoButton(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                          onPressed: () {
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(
                                content: Text('Create vacancy flow'),
                              ),
                            );
                          },
                          child: Container(
                            width: double.infinity,
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              vertical: context.sp(14),
                            ),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                                colors: [Color(0xFFAF52DE), Color(0xFF8E44AD)],
                              ),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                              boxShadow: [
                                BoxShadow(
                                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                  color: accentColor.withOpacity(0.25),
                                  blurRadius: 12,
                                  offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            child: Row(
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                              children: [
                                const Icon(
                                  CupertinoIcons.plus_app,
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                  size: 20,
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                Text(
                                  'Create Vacancy',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    fontSize: context.sp(16).clamp(14.0, 18.0),
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                    letterSpacing: -0.2,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProfileCard(
    BuildContext context, {
    required Color primaryText,
    required Color secondaryText,
    required Color cardColor,
    required Color accentColor,
  }) {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return Container(
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(16)),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: cardColor,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        ),
        child: const Center(child: CupertinoActivityIndicator()),
      );
    }

    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(16)),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(widget.isDark ? 0.2 : 0.06),
            blurRadius: 16,
            offset: const Offset(0, 6),
          ),
        ],
        border: Border.all(
          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
          color: accentColor.withOpacity(widget.isDark ? 0.15 : 0.1),
          width: 1,
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          // Avatar
          _GradientAvatar(
            initials: _initials,
            gradientColors: _gradientForName(_displayName),
            size: 64,
            fontSize: 24,
            photoUrl: _profilePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty ? _profilePhotoUrl : null,
            isLoading: false,
          ),
          SizedBox(width: context.sp(14)),

          // Name & Blue Tick
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Flexible(
                      child: Text(
                        _displayName.isNotEmpty ? _displayName : 'Your Name',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: primaryText,
                          fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                          letterSpacing: -0.3,
                        ),
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (_hasBlueTick) ...[
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                        color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                        size: context.sp(18).clamp(16.0, 20.0),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
                SizedBox(height: context.hp(4)),
                Text(
                  _hasBlueTick ? 'Verified Account' : 'Standard Account',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: secondaryText,
                    fontSize: context.sp(13).clamp(11.0, 14.0),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CHATS TAB  — Full Messenger-style conversation list
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _ChatsTab extends StatefulWidget {
  final Color bgColor;
  final Color cardColor;
  final Color separatorColor;
  final Color secondaryText;
  final bool isDark;
  final String currentUserId;

  const _ChatsTab({
    required this.bgColor,
    required this.cardColor,
    required this.separatorColor,
    required this.secondaryText,
    required this.isDark,
    required this.currentUserId,
  });

  @override
  State<_ChatsTab> createState() => _ChatsTabState();
}

class _ChatsTabState extends State<_ChatsTab> {
  final TextEditingController _searchCtrl = TextEditingController();
  String _searchQuery = '';

  // FIX: dedicated FocusNode + explicit autofocus: false so keyboard + cursor/focus indicator
  // only appears when the user explicitly taps the search field. Prevents any automatic
  // or repeated keyboard popup on tab switch / rebuild / setState.
  late final FocusNode _searchFocusNode;

  // ── FIX: current user name + photo so every tile can forward them to ChatScreen ──
  String _currentUserName = '';
  String _currentUserPhoto = '';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _searchFocusNode = FocusNode();
    _loadCurrentUserInfo(); // ← FIX: fetch once for all tiles
  }

  // ── FIX: one-shot Firestore fetch of the logged-in user's display info ────
  Future<void> _loadCurrentUserInfo() async {
    if (widget.currentUserId.isEmpty) return;
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      if (!mounted || !doc.exists) return;
      final d = doc.data()!;
      final fn = (d['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
      final ln = (d['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
      final name = [fn, ln].where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).join(' ');
      final photo = (d['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _currentUserName = name;
          _currentUserPhoto = photo;
        });
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchCtrl.dispose();
    _searchFocusNode.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _openNewChat() {
    if (widget.currentUserId.isEmpty) return;
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => _UserSearchScreen(
          currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
          isDark: context.isDark,
        ),
        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
          position: Tween<Offset>(
            begin: const Offset(0, 1),
            end: Offset.zero,
          ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic)),
          child: child,
        ),
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final primaryText = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
        : const Color(0xFF000000);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    if (widget.currentUserId.isEmpty) {
      return Container(color: widget.bgColor);
    }

    return Container(
      color: widget.bgColor,
      child: SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // ── Header ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                context.sp(16),
                context.sp(12),
                context.sp(16),
                0,
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'Chats',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: primaryText,
                      fontSize: context.sp(22).clamp(20.0, 26.0),
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      letterSpacing: -0.4,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            SizedBox(height: context.sp(6)),

            // ── Search bar ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(16)),
              child: Container(
                height: 36,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: widget.isDark
                      ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                      : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: TextField(
                  controller: _searchCtrl,
                  focusNode: _searchFocusNode,
                  autofocus: false,
                  onTapOutside: (event) => _searchFocusNode.unfocus(),
                  onChanged: (v) =>
                      setState(() => _searchQuery = v.trim().toLowerCase()),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: primaryText,
                    fontSize: context.sp(15),
                  ),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    border: InputBorder.none,
                    hintText: 'Search',
                    hintStyle: TextStyle(
                      color: widget.secondaryText,
                      fontSize: context.sp(15),
                    ),
                    prefixIcon: Icon(
                      CupertinoIcons.search,
                      color: widget.secondaryText,
                      size: 16,
                    ),
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                    isDense: true,
                    suffixIcon: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty
                        ? GestureDetector(
                            onTap: () {
                              _searchCtrl.clear();
                              setState(() => _searchQuery = '');
                              _searchFocusNode.unfocus();
                            },
                            child: Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.xmark_circle_fill,
                              color: widget.secondaryText,
                              size: 16,
                            ),
                          )
                        : null,
                  ),
                  cursorColor: iosBlue,
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // ── Little breathing space after search (exactly as requested)
            SizedBox(height: context.sp(8)),

            // ── Tiny gap before conversation list (makes UI feel polished)
            SizedBox(height: context.sp(4)),

            // ── Conversation list via Firestore stream ──────────────────────
            Expanded(
              child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                    .collection('conversations')
                    .where('participants', arrayContains: widget.currentUserId)
                    .snapshots(),
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  // FIX: Handle stream errors explicitly
                  if (snapshot.hasError) {
                    return Center(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                        child: Text(
                          'Could not load chats. Please check your connection.',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: widget.secondaryText,
                            fontSize: 14,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }

                  // FIX: Only show the spinner the very first time data is
                  // loading. When returning from ChatScreen, snapshot.hasData
                  // is already true (cached), so we skip the brief flash.
                  if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting &&
                      !snapshot.hasData) {
                    return const Center(child: CupertinoActivityIndicator());
                  }

                  final allDocs = snapshot.data?.docs.toList() ?? []
                    ..sort((a, b) {
                      final aTime =
                          (a.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)['lastMessageTime']
                              as Timestamp?;
                      final bTime =
                          (b.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)['lastMessageTime']
                              as Timestamp?;
                      if (aTime == null && bTime == null) return 0;
                      if (aTime == null) return -1;
                      if (bTime == null) return 1;
                      return bTime.compareTo(aTime);
                    });

                  // FIX: Exclude archived AND soft-deleted conversations, but
                  // automatically revive a soft-deleted conversation the moment
                  // a new message (sent OR received) arrives after the deletion
                  // horizon. This is determined purely from data already in the
                  // conversation document — no extra Firestore reads needed.
                  final nonArchivedDocs = allDocs.where((doc) {
                    final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;

                    // ── 1. archived check — always respected ────────────────
                    final archivedMap =
                        data['archived'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
                    if (archivedMap[widget.currentUserId] as bool? ?? false) {
                      return false;
                    }

                    // ── 2. soft-delete check ────────────────────────────────
                    final deletedForMap =
                        data['deletedFor'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
                    final isDeletedFor =
                        deletedForMap[widget.currentUserId] as bool? ?? false;

                    // Not deleted → always show.
                    if (!isDeletedFor) return true;

                    // Deleted → check whether lastMessageTime is strictly
                    // after deletedAt[uid]. If so, a new message arrived after
                    // the deletion (could be from either participant) and the
                    // conversation should reappear.
                    final deletedAtMap =
                        data['deletedAt'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
                    final deletedAtRaw = deletedAtMap[widget.currentUserId];
                    final deletedAt = deletedAtRaw is Timestamp
                        ? deletedAtRaw.toDate()
                        : null;
                    final lastMsgTs = data['lastMessageTime'] as Timestamp?;

                    if (deletedAt == null || lastMsgTs == null) return false;

                    final hasNewMessage = lastMsgTs.toDate().isAfter(deletedAt);

                    // Fire-and-forget: clear the stale deletedFor flag so
                    // Firestore stays consistent. ChatScreen still uses its
                    // _myDeletedAt horizon to hide pre-deletion messages.
                    if (hasNewMessage) {
                      FirebaseFirestore.instance
                          .collection('conversations')
                          .doc(doc.id)
                          .update({
                            'deletedFor.${widget.currentUserId}':
                                FieldValue.delete(),
                          })
                          .catchError((_) {});
                    }

                    return hasNewMessage;
                  }).toList();

                  // Filter by search query (only non-archived / non-deleted)
                  final filtered = nonArchivedDocs.where((doc) {
                    if (_searchQuery.isEmpty) return true;
                    final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                    final names =
