                        (data['participantNames'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ??
                                {})
                            .values
                            .map((v) => (v as String? ?? '').toLowerCase())
                            .join(' ');
                    return names.contains(_searchQuery);
                  }).toList();

                  if (filtered.isEmpty) {
                    if (_searchQuery.isEmpty) {
                      return _buildEmptyState(context, primaryText);
                    } else {
                      return Center(
                        child: Text(
                          'No results for "$_searchQuery"',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: widget.secondaryText,
                            fontSize: context.sp(15),
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                    }
                  }

                  // Pull-to-refresh + zero padding for perfect tight UI
                  return RefreshIndicator(
                    onRefresh: () async {
                      await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 800));
                    },
                    color: iosBlue,
                    child: ListView.builder(
                      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      itemCount: filtered.length,
                      itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                        final data = filtered[i].data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                        final conversationId = filtered[i].id;

                        final participants = List<String>.from(
                          data['participants'] ?? [],
                        );
                        final otherUid = participants.firstWhere(
                          (uid) => uid != widget.currentUserId,
                          orElse: () => '',
                        );

                        final participantNames =
                            data['participantNames'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ??
                            {};
                        final participantPhotos =
                            data['participantPhotos']
                                as Map<String, dynamic>? ??
                            {};

                        final otherName =
                            (participantNames[otherUid] as String?)?.trim() ??
                            '';
                        final otherPhoto =
                            (participantPhotos[otherUid] as String?) ?? '';
                        final lastMessage =
                            (data['lastMessage'] as String?) ?? '';
                        final lastSenderId =
                            (data['lastSenderId'] as String?) ?? '';
                        final timestamp = data['lastMessageTime'] as Timestamp?;
                        final unreadCounts =
                            data['unreadCounts'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
                        final unreadCount =
                            (unreadCounts[widget.currentUserId] as int?) ?? 0;
                        // FEATURE: Track other user's unread count to determine seen status.
                        // If last message is mine and other user's unread count is 0 → they saw it.
                        final otherUnreadCount =
                            (unreadCounts[otherUid] as int?) ?? 1;
                        final isLastMine = lastSenderId == widget.currentUserId;

                        return _ConversationTile(
                          conversationId: conversationId,
                          otherUserId: otherUid,
                          otherName: otherName,
                          otherPhotoUrl: otherPhoto,
                          lastMessage: lastMessage,
                          timestamp: timestamp?.toDate(),
                          unreadCount: unreadCount,
                          otherUnreadCount: otherUnreadCount,
                          isLastMine: isLastMine,
                          currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
                          // ── FIX: pass current user info to every tile ─────
                          currentUserName: _currentUserName,
                          currentUserPhotoUrl: _currentUserPhoto,
                          // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
                          isDark: widget.isDark,
                          bgColor: widget.bgColor,
                          separatorColor: widget.separatorColor,
                          secondaryText: widget.secondaryText,
                          primaryText: primaryText,
                          isArchiveTab: false,
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ENHANCED: Replaced the plain one-liner with a polished multi-element
  // empty-state UI. Shows a glowing chat icon, a clear headline, a friendly
  // subtitle, and a tappable "Start a Chat" pill button.
  Widget _buildEmptyState(BuildContext context, Color primaryText) {
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(40)),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            // ── Glowing gradient icon bubble ──────────────────────────────
            Container(
              width: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
              height: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                gradient: const LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  colors: [Color(0xFF007AFF), Color(0xFF5AC8FA)],
                ),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    color: iosBlue.withOpacity(widget.isDark ? 0.35 : 0.25),
                    blurRadius: 28,
                    spreadRadius: 4,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 8),
                  ),
                  BoxShadow(
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    color: Colors.black.withOpacity(
                      widget.isDark ? 0.20 : 0.06,
                    ),
                    blurRadius: 12,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 3),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: Icon(
                CupertinoIcons.chat_bubble_2_fill,
                size: context.sp(30).clamp(24.0, 38.0),
                color: Colors.white,
              ),
            ),

            SizedBox(height: context.hp(22)),

            // ── Headline ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Text(
              'No Conversations Yet',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: primaryText,
                fontSize: context.sp(19).clamp(16.0, 22.0),
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                letterSpacing: -0.4,
              ),
            ),

            SizedBox(height: context.hp(10)),

            // ── Subtitle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Text(
              'Your conversations will appear here.\nTap the search icon above to find\nsomeone and start chatting.',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: widget.secondaryText,
                fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                height: 1.55,
              ),
            ),

            SizedBox(height: context.hp(28)),

            // ── "Find Someone to Chat" CTA pill button ────────────────────
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: _openNewChat,
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              child: Container(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: context.sp(28),
                  vertical: context.sp(13),
                ),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: const LinearGradient(
                    colors: [Color(0xFF007AFF), Color(0xFF5AC8FA)],
                  ),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
                  boxShadow: [
                    BoxShadow(
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      color: iosBlue.withOpacity(0.30),
                      blurRadius: 16,
                      offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const Icon(
                      CupertinoIcons.search,
                      color: Colors.white,
                      size: 16,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text(
                      'Find Someone to Chat',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(13.0, 16.0),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        letterSpacing: -0.2,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  CONVERSATION TILE — one row in the chat list
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
class _ConversationTile extends StatefulWidget {
  final String conversationId;
  final String otherUserId;
  final String otherName; // from participantNames — may be empty
  final String otherPhotoUrl; // from participantPhotos — may be empty
  final String lastMessage;
  final DateTime? timestamp;
  final int unreadCount;
  // FEATURE: other user's unread count → used for seen indicator in the tile
  final int otherUnreadCount;
  final bool isLastMine;
  final String currentUserId;
  // ── FIX: current user's display info forwarded to ChatScreen ─────────────
  final String currentUserName;
  final String currentUserPhotoUrl;
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  final bool isDark;
  final Color bgColor;
  final Color separatorColor;
  final Color secondaryText;
  final Color primaryText;
  final bool isArchiveTab;

  const _ConversationTile({
    required this.conversationId,
    required this.otherUserId,
    required this.otherName,
    required this.otherPhotoUrl,
    required this.lastMessage,
    required this.timestamp,
    required this.unreadCount,
    this.otherUnreadCount = 1,
    required this.isLastMine,
    required this.currentUserId,
    this.currentUserName = '', // ← FIX: new field, defaults safe
    this.currentUserPhotoUrl = '', // ← FIX: new field, defaults safe
    required this.isDark,
    required this.bgColor,
    required this.separatorColor,
    required this.secondaryText,
    required this.primaryText,
    required this.isArchiveTab,
  });

  @override
  State<_ConversationTile> createState() => _ConversationTileState();
}

class _ConversationTileState extends State<_ConversationTile> {
  // Resolved from Firestore when participantNames entry is blank
  String _resolvedName = '';
  String _resolvedPhoto = '';
  bool _fetchedFromFirestore = false;

  // ── NEW: Real‑time subscription to the other user's Firestore document ──
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>>? _otherUserSub;
  String? _streamedName;
  String? _streamedPhoto;

  // ── NEW: Live blue-tick status for the OTHER user.
  // Activates only when blueTickExpiry > now, and auto-expires by re-checking
  // on every snapshot push from Firestore (same data shape used by BlueTick
  // and BiographPage). Renders the same CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill
  // (color 0xFF007AFF) used in BiographPage.
  bool _otherHasBlueTick = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _resolvedName = widget.otherName;
    _resolvedPhoto = widget.otherPhotoUrl;

    // ── FIX: trigger fetch when either name OR photo is missing ───────────
    final needsFetch =
        (_resolvedName.isEmpty || _resolvedPhoto.isEmpty) &&
        widget.otherUserId.isNotEmpty;
    if (needsFetch) {
      _fetchOtherUserFromFirestore();
    }

    // ── NEW: Subscribe to real‑time updates of the other user's profile ──
    if (widget.otherUserId.isNotEmpty) {
      _otherUserSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.otherUserId)
          .snapshots()
          .listen((doc) {
            if (!mounted || !doc.exists) return;
            final data = doc.data()!;
            final fn = (data['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
            final ln = (data['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
            final name = [fn, ln].where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).join(' ');
            final photo = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';

            // ── NEW: Blue-tick activation purely from Firestore data.
            // Active only while blueTickExpiry is strictly in the future.
            // When the expiry passes, this evaluates to false and the tick
            // disappears automatically on the next snapshot.
            final exp = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
            final hasTick = exp != null && exp.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());

            setState(() {
              if (name.isNotEmpty) _streamedName = name;
              _streamedPhoto = photo;
              _otherHasBlueTick = hasTick;
            });
          }, onError: (_) {});
    }
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(_ConversationTile old) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(old);
    // IMPROVED: Now checks for photo updates from the stream to ensure avatars dynamically change
    if (widget.otherName != old.otherName ||
        widget.otherPhotoUrl != old.otherPhotoUrl) {
      setState(() {
        if (widget.otherName.isNotEmpty) {
          _resolvedName = widget.otherName;
        }
        _resolvedPhoto = widget.otherPhotoUrl;
      });
    }
    // ── FIX: retry fetch if photo still missing after a stream update ─────
    final needsFetch =
        (_resolvedName.isEmpty || _resolvedPhoto.isEmpty) &&
        widget.otherUserId.isNotEmpty &&
        !_fetchedFromFirestore;
    if (needsFetch) _fetchOtherUserFromFirestore();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _otherUserSub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchOtherUserFromFirestore() async {
    _fetchedFromFirestore = true;
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.otherUserId)
          .get();
      if (!mounted || !doc.exists) return;
      final data = doc.data()!;
      final firstName = (data['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
      final lastName = (data['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
      final name = [firstName, lastName].where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).join(' ');
      final photo = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
      setState(() {
        _resolvedName = name.isNotEmpty ? name : 'Unknown';
        _resolvedPhoto = photo;
      });

      // Back-fill participantNames so the next load doesn't need a fetch.
      if (name.isNotEmpty) {
        FirebaseFirestore.instance
            .collection('conversations')
            .doc(widget.conversationId)
            .update({
              'participantNames.${widget.otherUserId}': name,
              if (photo.isNotEmpty)
                'participantPhotos.${widget.otherUserId}': photo,
            })
            .catchError((_) {});
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _resolvedName = 'Unknown');
    }
  }

  String _formatTime(DateTime? dt) {
    if (dt == null) return '';
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final diff = now.difference(dt);
    if (diff.inDays == 0) {
      final h = dt.hour;
      final m = dt.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
      final period = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
      final h12 = h % 12 == 0 ? 12 : h % 12;
      return '$h12:$m $period';
    } else if (diff.inDays == 1) {
      return 'Yesterday';
    } else if (diff.inDays < 7) {
      const days = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
      return days[dt.weekday - 1];
    }
    return '${dt.month}/${dt.day}/${dt.year % 100}';
  }

  String _initialsForName(String name) {
    final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
    if (parts.isEmpty || parts.first.isEmpty) return '?';
    final first = parts.first[0].toUpperCase();
    final last = parts.length > 1 && parts.last.isNotEmpty
        ? parts.last[0].toUpperCase()
        : '';
    return '$first$last';
  }

  void _showActionSheet(BuildContext context) {
    final iosBlue = const Color(0xFF007AFF);
    final destructiveRed = const Color(0xFFFF3B30);
    final actionText = widget.isArchiveTab ? 'Unarchive' : 'Archive';
    final actionIcon = widget.isArchiveTab
        ? CupertinoIcons.arrow_uturn_left_circle_fill
        : CupertinoIcons.archivebox_fill;

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (ctx) => Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: widget.isDark
              ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
              : const Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
            topLeft: Radius.circular(20),
            topRight: Radius.circular(20),
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          top: false,
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Container(
                width: 36,
                height: 5,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  color: widget.secondaryText.withOpacity(0.6),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2.5),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              // Archive / Unarchive
              ListTile(
                leading: Icon(actionIcon, color: iosBlue, size: 25),
                title: Text(
                  actionText,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: widget.primaryText,
                    fontSize: 15,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
                onTap: () {
                  Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
                  _toggleArchive();
                },
              ),
              // FIX: Only show Delete option when NOT in the archive tab.
              // In ArchiveChats screen, slide-down popup shows only Unarchive + Cancel.
              if (!widget.isArchiveTab) ...[
                Divider(
                  height: 1,
                  thickness: 0.5,
                  color: widget.separatorColor,
                ),
                ListTile(
                  leading: Icon(
                    CupertinoIcons.trash_fill,
                    color: destructiveRed,
                    size: 25,
                  ),
                  title: Text(
                    'Delete',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: destructiveRed,
                      fontSize: 15,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    ),
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
                    _deleteConversation();
                  },
                ),
              ],
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              // Cancel
              ListTile(
                title: Center(
                  child: Text(
                    'Cancel',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: iosBlue,
                      fontSize: 15,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                onTap: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _toggleArchive() async {
    final targetArchived = widget.isArchiveTab ? false : true;
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .update({'archived.${widget.currentUserId}': targetArchived});
    } catch (_) {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }

  // FIX: Per-user soft-delete with a timestamp checkpoint.
  //
  // Two fields are written atomically:
  //   • deletedFor.<uid>  = true   → hides the conversation tile in _ChatsTab
  //   • deletedAt.<uid>   = now    → ChatScreen uses this as a "horizon" and
  //                                   only shows messages AFTER this timestamp.
  //
  // The other participant's data is completely untouched. When either user
  // sends a new message afterwards, only the sender's deletedFor flag is
  // cleared (deletedAt is intentionally kept so truly old messages stay
  // hidden), and the conversation reappears in the sender's list showing
  // only the new messages.
  Future<void> _deleteConversation() async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .update({
            'deletedFor.${widget.currentUserId}': true,
            'deletedAt.${widget.currentUserId}': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
          });
    } catch (_) {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    // Use resolved values (either from parent props or Firestore fetch),
    // but prefer the real‑time streamed values when available.
    final displayName = (_streamedName != null && _streamedName!.isNotEmpty)
        ? _streamedName!
        : (_resolvedName.isNotEmpty ? _resolvedName : 'Loading…');
    final displayPhoto = (_streamedPhoto != null && _streamedPhoto!.isNotEmpty)
        ? _streamedPhoto!
        : (_resolvedPhoto.isNotEmpty
              ? _resolvedPhoto
              : (widget.otherPhotoUrl.isNotEmpty ? widget.otherPhotoUrl : ''));

    final initials = _initialsForName(displayName);
    final hasUnread = widget.unreadCount > 0;

    // FIX: Client-side Decryption logic for latest message previews
    String decryptedLastMessage = widget.lastMessage;
    if (widget.lastMessage.isNotEmpty) {
      if (widget.lastMessage == '🚫 Message unsent') {
        decryptedLastMessage = widget.lastMessage;
      } else {
        // Attempt decryption (Assumes AES-GCM IV is bundled with ciphertext as seen in MessageEncryption)
        final attempt = MessageEncryption.decryptText(
          widget.lastMessage,
          widget.currentUserId,
          widget.otherUserId,
        );
        // If decryption succeeds (doesn't return an error token), we use it.
        if (attempt != '[Decryption error]' &&
            attempt != '[Decryption failed]') {
          decryptedLastMessage = attempt;
        }
      }
    }

    // ENHANCED TRUNCATION: show "You: …" for sent, plain text for received.
    // If the message contains multiple lines, truncate it cleanly to the first line.
    String previewText = 'Say hello 👋';
    if (decryptedLastMessage.isNotEmpty) {
      final lines = decryptedLastMessage.trim().split('\n');
      final firstLine = lines.first;
      final textToUse = lines.length > 1 ? '$firstLine ...' : firstLine;
      previewText = widget.isLastMine ? 'You: $textToUse' : textToUse;
    }

    // FEATURE: seen indicator — last mine message was read by the other user
    // when their unread count is 0.
    final seenByOther = widget.isLastMine && widget.otherUnreadCount == 0;

    return GestureDetector(
      // ── FIX: forward current user info so ChatScreen nav/seen avatars work ─
      onTap: () {
        Navigator.of(context).push(
          PageRouteBuilder(
            pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => ChatScreen(
              conversationId: widget.conversationId,
              otherUserId: widget.otherUserId,
              otherName: displayName,
              otherPhotoUrl: displayPhoto,
              currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
              currentUserName: widget.currentUserName, // ← FIX
              currentUserPhotoUrl: widget.currentUserPhotoUrl, // ← FIX
            ),
            transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
              position:
                  Tween<Offset>(
                    begin: const Offset(1, 0),
                    end: Offset.zero,
                  ).animate(
                    CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
                  ),
              child: child,
            ),
            transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
          ),
        );
      },
      onLongPress: () => _showActionSheet(context),
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: context.sp(16),
              vertical: context.sp(10),
            ),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                // ── Avatar: photo > gradient+initials fallback ──────────────
                // ── FIX: uses displayPhoto (Firestore-resolved) for BiographPage ─
                GestureDetector(
                  behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                  onTap: () {
                    final parts = displayName.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
                    final firstName = parts.isNotEmpty ? parts.first : '';
                    final lastName = parts.length > 1
                        ? parts.sublist(1).join(' ')
                        : '';
                    Navigator.of(context).push(
                      PageRouteBuilder(
                        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => BiographPage(
                          uid: widget.otherUserId,
                          firstName: firstName,
                          lastName: lastName,
                          profilePhotoUrl:
                              displayPhoto, // ← FIX: resolved photo
                          isDark: widget.isDark,
                        ),
                        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) =>
                            SlideTransition(
                              position:
                                  Tween<Offset>(
                                    begin: const Offset(1, 0),
                                    end: Offset.zero,
                                  ).animate(
                                    CurvedAnimation(
                                      parent: anim,
                                      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                              child: child,
                            ),
                        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                  child: _GradientAvatar(
                    initials: initials,
                    gradientColors: _gradientForName(displayName),
                    size: 52,
                    fontSize: 18,
                    photoUrl: displayPhoto.isNotEmpty ? displayPhoto : null,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(width: context.sp(12)),

                // ── Name + preview text (left column) ───────────────────────
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      // Name row with blue tick
                      Row(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          Flexible(
                            child: Text(
                              displayName,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: widget.primaryText,
                                fontSize: context.sp(16).clamp(14.0, 18.0),
                                fontWeight: hasUnread
                                    ? FontWeight.w600
                                    : FontWeight.w500,
                                letterSpacing: -0.2,
                              ),
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                              maxLines: 1,
                            ),
                          ),
                          if (_otherHasBlueTick) ...[
                            const SizedBox(width: 4),
                            const Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                              color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                              size: 16,
                            ),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(3)),
                      // Preview text
                      Text(
                        previewText,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: hasUnread
                              ? widget.primaryText
                              : widget.secondaryText,
                          fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 15.0),
                          fontWeight: hasUnread
                              ? FontWeight.w500
                              : FontWeight.w400,
                        ),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        maxLines: 1,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                // ── Right column: timestamp + seen/unread ──────────────────
                // Fixed alignment: all items stacked to the right edge
                SizedBox(width: context.sp(8)),
                Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    // Timestamp
                    Text(
                      _formatTime(widget.timestamp),
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: hasUnread ? iosBlue : widget.secondaryText,
                        fontSize: context.sp(12).clamp(11.0, 13.0),
                        fontWeight: hasUnread
                            ? FontWeight.w500
                            : FontWeight.w400,
                      ),
                    ),
                    // Only add spacing if there's something below
                    if (hasUnread || seenByOther)
                      SizedBox(height: context.hp(3)),
                    // Unread badge or seen indicator (mutually exclusive)
                    if (hasUnread)
                      Container(
                        constraints: const BoxConstraints(
                          minWidth: 20,
                          minHeight: 20,
                        ),
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5),
                        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                          color: Color(0xFF34C759),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(10)),
                        ),
                        child: Center(
                          child: Text(
                            widget.unreadCount > 99
                                ? '99+'
                                : '${widget.unreadCount}',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 11,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      )
                    else if (seenByOther)
                      _GradientAvatar(
                        initials: initials,
                        gradientColors: _gradientForName(displayName),
                        size: 16,
                        fontSize: 7,
                        photoUrl: displayPhoto.isNotEmpty ? displayPhoto : null,
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          // Inset divider below avatar width
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(
              left: context.sp(16) + 52 + context.sp(12),
            ),
            child: Divider(
              height: 1,
              thickness: 0.5,
              color: widget.separatorColor,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ENCRYPTION SERVICE — AES‑GCM with key derived from participants' UIDs
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class MessageEncryption {
  static const String _pepper = 'YourSecretPepper_ChangeInProduction!';

  static encrypt.Key _deriveKey(String uid1, String uid2) {
    final List<String> sorted = [uid1, uid2]..sort();
    final String combined = '${sorted[0]}|${sorted[1]}|$_pepper';
    final bytes = utf8.encode(combined);
    final hash = sha256.convert(bytes);
    return encrypt.Key.fromBase64(base64Encode(hash.bytes));
  }

  static String encryptText(String plaintext, String uid1, String uid2) {
    if (plaintext.isEmpty) return '';
    final key = _deriveKey(uid1, uid2);
    final iv = encrypt.IV.fromSecureRandom(12);
    final encrypter = encrypt.Encrypter(
      encrypt.AES(key, mode: encrypt.AESMode.gcm),
    );
    final encrypted = encrypter.encrypt(plaintext, iv: iv);
    return '${base64Encode(iv.bytes)}:${encrypted.base64}';
  }

  static String decryptText(String ciphertext, String uid1, String uid2) {
    if (ciphertext.isEmpty) return '';
    try {
      final parts = ciphertext.split(':');
      if (parts.length != 2) return '[Decryption error]';
      final ivBytes = base64Decode(parts[0]);
      final encryptedBase64 = parts[1];
      final key = _deriveKey(uid1, uid2);
      final iv = encrypt.IV(ivBytes);
      final encrypter = encrypt.Encrypter(
        encrypt.AES(key, mode: encrypt.AESMode.gcm),
      );
      final decrypted = encrypter.decrypt64(encryptedBase64, iv: iv);
      return decrypted;
    } catch (_) {
      return '[Decryption failed]';
    }
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CHAT SCREEN — Individual conversation with end‑to‑end encryption
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class ChatScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String conversationId;
  final String otherUserId;
  final String otherName;
  final String otherPhotoUrl;
  final String currentUserId;
  final String currentUserName;
  final String currentUserPhotoUrl;

  const ChatScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.conversationId,
    required this.otherUserId,
    required this.otherName,
    required this.otherPhotoUrl,
    required this.currentUserId,
    this.currentUserName = '',
    this.currentUserPhotoUrl = '',
  });

  @override
  State<ChatScreen> createState() => _ChatScreenState();
}

class _ChatScreenState extends State<ChatScreen> {
  final TextEditingController _inputCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final ScrollController _scrollCtrl = ScrollController();
  bool _isSending = false;

  String _selfName = '';
  String _selfPhoto = '';
  String _dynamicOtherName = '';
  String _dynamicOtherPhoto = '';

  DateTime? _myDeletedAt;
  bool _deletedAtLoaded = false;

  Map<String, dynamic>? _replyToMsg;
  String? _editingMsgId;

  int _otherUnreadCount = 1;
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot>? _convDocSub;
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>>? _otherUserSub;
  bool _otherHasBlueTick = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _selfName = widget.currentUserName;
    _selfPhoto = widget.currentUserPhotoUrl;
    _dynamicOtherName = widget.otherName;
    _dynamicOtherPhoto = widget.otherPhotoUrl;
    _loadSelfProfile();
    _loadDeletedAt();
    _subscribeToConvDoc();
    if (_dynamicOtherPhoto.isEmpty && widget.otherUserId.isNotEmpty) {
      _loadOtherUserPhoto();
    }

    if (widget.otherUserId.isNotEmpty) {
      _otherUserSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.otherUserId)
          .snapshots()
          .listen((doc) {
            if (!mounted || !doc.exists) return;
            final data = doc.data()!;
            final fn = (data['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
            final ln = (data['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
            final name = [fn, ln].where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).join(' ');
            final photo = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
            final exp = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
            final hasTick = exp != null && exp.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());

            setState(() {
              if (name.isNotEmpty) _dynamicOtherName = name;
              if (photo.isNotEmpty) _dynamicOtherPhoto = photo;
              _otherHasBlueTick = hasTick;
            });
          }, onError: (_) {});
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadSelfProfile() async {
    if (widget.currentUserId.isEmpty) return;
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      if (!mounted || !doc.exists) return;
      final d = doc.data()!;
      final fn = (d['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
      final ln = (d['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
      final name = [fn, ln].where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).join(' ');
      final photo = (d['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
      setState(() {
        if (name.isNotEmpty) _selfName = name;
        _selfPhoto = photo;
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _loadOtherUserPhoto() async {
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.otherUserId)
          .get();
      if (!mounted || !doc.exists) return;
      final photo = (doc.data()!['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
      if (photo.isNotEmpty && mounted) {
        setState(() => _dynamicOtherPhoto = photo);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  String get _effectiveMyName =>
      _selfName.isNotEmpty ? _selfName : widget.currentUserName;

  String get _effectiveMyPhoto =>
      _selfPhoto.isNotEmpty ? _selfPhoto : widget.currentUserPhotoUrl;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _inputCtrl.dispose();
    _scrollCtrl.dispose();
    _convDocSub?.cancel();
    _otherUserSub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _subscribeToConvDoc() {
    _convDocSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('conversations')
        .doc(widget.conversationId)
        .snapshots()
        .listen((snap) {
          if (!mounted || !snap.exists) return;
          final data = snap.data()!;
          final unreadCounts =
              data['unreadCounts'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
          final otherCount = (unreadCounts[widget.otherUserId] as int?) ?? 1;

          final participantPhotos =
              data['participantPhotos'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
          final updatedOtherPhoto =
              participantPhotos[widget.otherUserId] as String?;

          final participantNames =
              data['participantNames'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
          final updatedOtherName =
              participantNames[widget.otherUserId] as String?;

          if (mounted) {
            setState(() {
              _otherUnreadCount = otherCount;
              if (updatedOtherPhoto != null) {
                _dynamicOtherPhoto = updatedOtherPhoto;
              }
              if (updatedOtherName != null) {
                _dynamicOtherName = updatedOtherName;
              }
            });
          }
        });
  }

  Future<void> _loadDeletedAt() async {
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .get();
      DateTime? horizon;
      if (doc.exists) {
        final data = doc.data()!;
        final deletedAtMap = data['deletedAt'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
        final raw = deletedAtMap[widget.currentUserId];
        if (raw is Timestamp) horizon = raw.toDate();
      }
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _myDeletedAt = horizon;
        _deletedAtLoaded = true;
      });
      _markAsRead();
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _deletedAtLoaded = true);
      _markAsRead();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _markAsRead() async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .update({'unreadCounts.${widget.currentUserId}': 0});
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _markMessagesRead(List<QueryDocumentSnapshot> docs) async {
    final batch = FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
    bool hasPending = false;
    for (final doc in docs) {
      final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final readBy = List<String>.from(data['readBy'] as List? ?? []);
      if (!readBy.contains(widget.currentUserId)) {
        batch.update(doc.reference, {
          'readBy': FieldValue.arrayUnion([widget.currentUserId]),
        });
        hasPending = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasPending) batch.commit().catchError((_) {});
  }

  String _encrypt(String plaintext) {
    return MessageEncryption.encryptText(
      plaintext,
      widget.currentUserId,
      widget.otherUserId,
    );
  }

  String _decrypt(String ciphertext) {
    return MessageEncryption.decryptText(
      ciphertext,
      widget.currentUserId,
      widget.otherUserId,
    );
  }

  Future<bool> _hasInternetConnection() async {
    final result = await Connectivity().checkConnectivity();
    // ignore: unrelated_type_equality_checks
    return result != ConnectivityResult.none;
  }

  Future<void> _sendMessage() async {
    if (_editingMsgId != null) {
      await _confirmEdit();
      return;
    }

    final text = _inputCtrl.text.trim();
    if (text.isEmpty || _isSending) return;

    // ── Check internet connection before sending ─────────────────────────
    if (!await _hasInternetConnection()) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: const Text("No connection, you can't send any message"),
            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isSending = true);
    _inputCtrl.clear();

    final replySnapshot = _replyToMsg;
    if (mounted) setState(() => _replyToMsg = null);

    try {
      final now = FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
      final msgRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('messages')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .collection('messages')
          .doc();

      final convRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .doc(widget.conversationId);

      final convSnap = await convRef.get();

      Map<String, dynamic>? encryptedReply;
      if (replySnapshot != null) {
        final originalReplyText = replySnapshot['text'] as String? ?? '';
        encryptedReply = {
          'messageId': replySnapshot['messageId'],
          'senderId': replySnapshot['senderId'],
          'senderName': replySnapshot['senderName'],
          'text': _encrypt(originalReplyText),
        };
      }

      final Map<String, dynamic> msgPayload = {
        'senderId': widget.currentUserId,
        'text': _encrypt(text),
        'timestamp': now,
        'readBy': [widget.currentUserId],
        'edited': false,
        'unsent': false,
        'replyTo': encryptedReply,
      };

      if (!convSnap.exists) {
        final ids = [widget.currentUserId, widget.otherUserId]..sort();
        await convRef.set({
          'participants': ids,
          'participantNames': {
            widget.currentUserId: _effectiveMyName,
            widget.otherUserId: widget.otherName,
          },
          'participantPhotos': {
            widget.currentUserId: _effectiveMyPhoto,
            widget.otherUserId: widget.otherPhotoUrl,
          },
          'lastMessage': _encrypt(text),
          'lastMessageTime': now,
          'lastSenderId': widget.currentUserId,
          'unreadCounts': {widget.currentUserId: 0, widget.otherUserId: 1},
          'createdAt': now,
        });
        await msgRef.set(msgPayload);
      } else {
        final batch = FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
        batch.set(msgRef, msgPayload);
        batch.update(convRef, {
          'lastMessage': _encrypt(text),
          'lastMessageTime': now,
          'lastSenderId': widget.currentUserId,
          'unreadCounts.${widget.otherUserId}': FieldValue.increment(1),
          'deletedFor.${widget.currentUserId}': FieldValue.delete(),
          'participantPhotos.${widget.currentUserId}': _effectiveMyPhoto,
        });
        await batch.commit();
      }

      // No manual scroll needed – reverse list handles it automatically.
    } catch (_) {
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isSending = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _confirmEdit() async {
    final newText = _inputCtrl.text.trim();
    final msgId = _editingMsgId;
    if (newText.isEmpty || msgId == null) {
      _cancelEdit();
      return;
    }

    if (!await _hasInternetConnection()) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: const Text("No connection, you can't edit the message"),
            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
          ),
        );
      }
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      _editingMsgId = null;
      _isSending = true;
    });
    _inputCtrl.clear();

    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('messages')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .collection('messages')
          .doc(msgId)
          .update({'text': _encrypt(newText), 'edited': true});

      FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .get()
          .then((snap) {
            if (snap.exists) {
              FirebaseFirestore.instance
                  .collection('messages')
                  .doc(widget.conversationId)
                  .collection('messages')
                  .orderBy('timestamp', descending: true)
                  .limit(1)
                  .get()
                  .then((q) {
                    if (q.docs.isNotEmpty && q.docs.first.id == msgId) {
                      snap.reference.update({'lastMessage': _encrypt(newText)});
                    }
                  })
                  .catchError((_) {});
            }
          })
          .catchError((_) {});
    } catch (_) {
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isSending = false);
    }
  }

  void _cancelEdit() {
    setState(() => _editingMsgId = null);
    _inputCtrl.clear();
  }

  void _cancelReply() {
    setState(() => _replyToMsg = null);
  }

  Future<void> _unsentMessage(String msgId) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('messages')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .collection('messages')
          .doc(msgId)
          .update({'unsent': true, 'text': ''});

      FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .get()
          .then((snap) {
            if (snap.exists) {
              FirebaseFirestore.instance
                  .collection('messages')
                  .doc(widget.conversationId)
                  .collection('messages')
                  .orderBy('timestamp', descending: true)
                  .limit(1)
                  .get()
                  .then((q) {
                    if (q.docs.isNotEmpty && q.docs.first.id == msgId) {
                      snap.reference.update({
                        'lastMessage': '🚫 Message unsent',
                      });
                    }
                  })
                  .catchError((_) {});
            }
          })
          .catchError((_) {});
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _addReaction(String msgId, String emoji) async {
    try {
      final msgRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('messages')
          .doc(widget.conversationId)
          .collection('messages')
          .doc(msgId);
      final snap = await msgRef.get();
      if (!snap.exists) return;
      final data = snap.data()!;
      final reactions = Map<String, String>.from(
        data['reactions'] as Map? ?? {},
      );
      if (reactions[widget.currentUserId] == emoji) {
        reactions.remove(widget.currentUserId);
      } else {
        reactions[widget.currentUserId] = emoji;
      }
      await msgRef.update({'reactions': reactions});
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  void _copyText(String text) {
    Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: text));
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: const Text('Copied to clipboard'),
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showMessageMenu(
    BuildContext ctx,
    String msgId,
    String msgText,
    bool isMine,
    bool isUnsent,
    String senderName,
  ) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);
    const destructiveRed = Color(0xFFFF3B30);
    final cardColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
    final primaryText = isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final separatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    const emojis = ['👍', '❤️', '😂', '😮', '😢', '😡'];

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: ctx,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (sheetCtx) => Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: cardColor,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
            topLeft: Radius.circular(20),
            topRight: Radius.circular(20),
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          top: false,
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Container(
                width: 36,
                height: 5,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  color: const Color(0xFF8E8E93).withOpacity(0.5),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2.5),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              if (!isUnsent)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
                    children: emojis
                        .map(
                          (emoji) => GestureDetector(
                            onTap: () {
                              Navigator.of(sheetCtx).pop();
                              _addReaction(msgId, emoji);
                            },
                            child: Container(
                              width: 44,
                              height: 44,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: isDark
                                    ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                                    : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7),
                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              ),
                              child: Center(
                                child: Text(
                                  emoji,
                                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        )
                        .toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              if (!isUnsent) const SizedBox(height: 8),
              if (!isUnsent)
                Divider(height: 1, thickness: 0.5, color: separatorColor),
              if (!isUnsent)
                ListTile(
                  leading: Icon(CupertinoIcons.reply, color: iosBlue, size: 22),
                  title: Text(
                    'Reply',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: primaryText,
                      fontSize: 15,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    ),
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.of(sheetCtx).pop();
                    setState(() {
                      _replyToMsg = {
                        'messageId': msgId,
                        'senderId': isMine
                            ? widget.currentUserId
                            : widget.otherUserId,
                        'senderName': isMine ? 'You' : senderName,
                        'text': msgText,
                      };
                      _editingMsgId = null;
                    });
                  },
                ),
              if (!isUnsent)
                ListTile(
                  leading: Icon(
                    CupertinoIcons.doc_on_doc,
                    color: iosBlue,
                    size: 22,
                  ),
                  title: Text(
                    'Copy',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: primaryText,
                      fontSize: 15,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    ),
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.of(sheetCtx).pop();
                    _copyText(msgText);
                  },
                ),
              if (isMine && !isUnsent)
                ListTile(
                  leading: Icon(
                    CupertinoIcons.pencil,
                    color: iosBlue,
                    size: 22,
                  ),
                  title: Text(
                    'Edit',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: primaryText,
                      fontSize: 15,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    ),
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.of(sheetCtx).pop();
                    setState(() {
                      _editingMsgId = msgId;
                      _replyToMsg = null;
                    });
                    _inputCtrl.text = msgText;
                    _inputCtrl.selection = TextSelection.fromPosition(
                      TextPosition(offset: _inputCtrl.text.length),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              if (isMine && !isUnsent)
                ListTile(
                  leading: Icon(
                    CupertinoIcons.arrow_uturn_left_circle,
                    color: destructiveRed,
                    size: 22,
                  ),
                  title: Text(
                    'Unsend',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: destructiveRed,
                      fontSize: 15,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    ),
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.of(sheetCtx).pop();
                    _unsentMessage(msgId);
                  },
                ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              ListTile(
                title: Center(
                  child: Text(
                    'Cancel',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: iosBlue,
                      fontSize: 15,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetCtx).pop(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _formatMsgTimestamp(DateTime dt) {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final diff = now.difference(dt);
    final h = dt.hour;
    final m = dt.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    final period = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
    final h12 = h % 12 == 0 ? 12 : h % 12;
    final timeStr = '$h12:$m $period';
    if (diff.inDays == 0) return 'Today $timeStr';
    if (diff.inDays == 1) return 'Yesterday $timeStr';
    return '${dt.month}/${dt.day}/${dt.year} $timeStr';
  }

  String _initialsForName(String name) {
    final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
    if (parts.isEmpty || parts.first.isEmpty) return '?';
    final first = parts.first[0].toUpperCase();
    final last = parts.length > 1 && parts.last.isNotEmpty
        ? parts.last[0].toUpperCase()
        : '';
    return '$first$last';
  }

  Widget _buildSkeletonLoader() {
    return SkeletonLoader(isDark: context.isDark, itemCount: 8);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    final bgColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF000000) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final navBg = isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
    final separatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final primaryText = isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final secondaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    final inputBg = isDark ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E) : const Color(0xFFEFEFF4);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    final initials = _initialsForName(_dynamicOtherName);
    final statusH = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top;
    const navH = 48.0;

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value: (isDark ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
          .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        body: Column(
          children: [
            // ── Nav bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Container(
              height: statusH + navH,
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: statusH),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: navBg,
                border: Border(
                  bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: const Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 8,
                          vertical: 8,
                        ),
                        child: Icon(
                          CupertinoIcons.back,
                          color: iosBlue,
                          size: 26,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    GestureDetector(
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      onTap: () {
                        final parts = _dynamicOtherName.trim().split(
                          RegExp(r'\s+'),
                        );
                        final firstName = parts.isNotEmpty ? parts.first : '';
                        final lastName = parts.length > 1
                            ? parts.sublist(1).join(' ')
                            : '';
                        Navigator.of(context).push(
                          PageRouteBuilder(
                            pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => BiographPage(
                              uid: widget.otherUserId,
                              firstName: firstName,
                              lastName: lastName,
                              profilePhotoUrl: _dynamicOtherPhoto,
                              isDark: isDark,
                            ),
                            transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) =>
                                SlideTransition(
                                  position:
                                      Tween<Offset>(
                                        begin: const Offset(1, 0),
                                        end: Offset.zero,
                                      ).animate(
                                        CurvedAnimation(
                                          parent: anim,
                                          curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                  child: child,
                                ),
                            transitionDuration: const Duration(
                              milliseconds: 300,
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                      child: _GradientAvatar(
                        initials: initials,
                        gradientColors: _gradientForName(_dynamicOtherName),
                        size: 36,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        photoUrl: _dynamicOtherPhoto.isNotEmpty
                            ? _dynamicOtherPhoto
                            : null,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: GestureDetector(
                        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                        onTap: () {
                          final parts = _dynamicOtherName.trim().split(
                            RegExp(r'\s+'),
                          );
                          final firstName = parts.isNotEmpty ? parts.first : '';
                          final lastName = parts.length > 1
                              ? parts.sublist(1).join(' ')
                              : '';
                          Navigator.of(context).push(
                            PageRouteBuilder(
                              pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => BiographPage(
                                uid: widget.otherUserId,
                                firstName: firstName,
                                lastName: lastName,
                                profilePhotoUrl: _dynamicOtherPhoto,
                                isDark: isDark,
                              ),
                              transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) =>
                                  SlideTransition(
                                    position:
                                        Tween<Offset>(
                                          begin: const Offset(1, 0),
                                          end: Offset.zero,
                                        ).animate(
                                          CurvedAnimation(
                                            parent: anim,
                                            curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                    child: child,
                                  ),
                              transitionDuration: const Duration(
                                milliseconds: 300,
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            Flexible(
                              child: Text(
                                _dynamicOtherName,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: primaryText,
                                  fontSize: 16,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                  letterSpacing: -0.3,
                                ),
                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                maxLines: 1,
                              ),
                            ),
                            if (_otherHasBlueTick) ...[
                              const SizedBox(width: 4),
                              const Icon(
                                CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                                color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                size: 16,
                              ),
                            ],
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {},
                          child: const Padding(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 8,
                              vertical: 8,
                            ),
                            child: Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.phone_fill,
                              color: iosBlue,
                              size: 20,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {},
                          child: const Padding(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 8,
                              vertical: 8,
                            ),
                            child: Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.video_camera_solid,
                              color: iosBlue,
                              size: 22,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // ── Message list (with reverse for instant bottom display) ─────
            Expanded(
              child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
                switchInCurve: Curves.easeOut,
                switchOutCurve: Curves.easeIn,
                transitionBuilder: (child, animation) =>
                    FadeTransition(opacity: animation, child: child),
                child: !_deletedAtLoaded
                    ? _buildSkeletonLoader()
                    : StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                        key: const ValueKey('message_stream'),
                        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                            .collection('messages')
                            .doc(widget.conversationId)
                            .collection('messages')
                            .orderBy('timestamp')
                            .snapshots(),
                        builder: (context, snapshot) {
                          if (snapshot.connectionState ==
                                  ConnectionState.waiting &&
                              !snapshot.hasData) {
                            return _buildSkeletonLoader();
                          }

                          if (snapshot.hasError) {
                            return Center(
                              child: Text(
                                'Failed to load messages',
                                style: TextStyle(color: secondaryText),
                              ),
                            );
                          }

                          if (!snapshot.hasData) {
                            return _buildSkeletonLoader();
                          }

                          final horizon = _myDeletedAt;
                          final docs = snapshot.data!.docs.where((doc) {
                            if (horizon == null) return true;
                            final ts =
                                (doc.data()
                                        as Map<String, dynamic>)['timestamp']
                                    as Timestamp?;
                            if (ts == null) return false;
                            return ts.toDate().isAfter(horizon);
                          }).toList();

                          // Mark messages as read (once after build)
                          WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
                            _markMessagesRead(docs);
                            _markAsRead();
                          });

                          if (docs.isEmpty) {
                            return Center(
                              child: Padding(
                                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: context.sp(40),
                                ),
                                child: Column(
                                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                  children: [
                                    Container(
                                      width: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
                                      height: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
                                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                        color: isDark
                                            ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
                                            : Colors.white,
                                        boxShadow: [
                                          BoxShadow(
                                            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                            color: iosBlue.withOpacity(
                                              isDark ? 0.18 : 0.12,
                                            ),
                                            blurRadius: 24,
                                            spreadRadius: 2,
                                            offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                                          ),
                                          BoxShadow(
                                            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(
                                              isDark ? 0.20 : 0.06,
                                            ),
                                            blurRadius: 12,
                                            offset: const Offset(0, 3),
                                          ),
                                        ],
                                      ),
                                      child: Icon(
                                        CupertinoIcons.chat_bubble_2_fill,
                                        size: context.sp(30).clamp(24.0, 38.0),
                                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                        color: iosBlue.withOpacity(0.55),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    SizedBox(height: context.hp(22)),
                                    Text(
                                      'No messages yet',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        color: primaryText,
                                        fontSize: context
                                            .sp(19)
                                            .clamp(16.0, 22.0),
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                        letterSpacing: -0.4,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),
                                    Text(
                                      'Send a message to start the conversation.',
                                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        color: secondaryText,
                                        fontSize: context
                                            .sp(14)
                                            .clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                                        height: 1.5,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            );
                          }

                          // ── REVERSE LIST to show latest at bottom without flicker ──
                          final reversedDocs = docs.reversed.toList();

                          // Find latest message sent by current user (for seen indicator)
                          String? lastMineMsgId;
                          for (final doc in reversedDocs) {
                            final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                            if (data['senderId'] == widget.currentUserId &&
                                !(data['unsent'] as bool? ?? false)) {
                              lastMineMsgId = doc.id;
                              break;
                            }
                          }

                          return ListView.builder(
                            controller: _scrollCtrl,
                            reverse: true, // newest at bottom (index 0)
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 12,
                              vertical: 8,
                            ),
                            itemCount: reversedDocs.length,
                            itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                              final doc = reversedDocs[i];
                              final rawData =
                                  doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;

                              final decryptedText = rawData['unsent'] == true
                                  ? ''
                                  : _decrypt(rawData['text'] ?? '');

                              Map<String, dynamic>? decryptedReply;
                              final replyRaw =
                                  rawData['replyTo'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
                              if (replyRaw != null &&
                                  rawData['unsent'] != true) {
                                final encryptedReplyText =
                                    replyRaw['text'] as String? ?? '';
                                decryptedReply = {
                                  ...replyRaw,
                                  'text': _decrypt(encryptedReplyText),
                                };
                              }

                              final isMine =
                                  rawData['senderId'] == widget.currentUserId;
                              final ts = rawData['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;
                              final dt = ts?.toDate();
                              final msgId = doc.id;
                              final unsent =
                                  rawData['unsent'] as bool? ?? false;
                              final edited =
                                  rawData['edited'] as bool? ?? false;
                              final reactionsRaw =
                                  rawData['reactions'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
                              final reactions = reactionsRaw != null
                                  ? Map<String, String>.from(reactionsRaw)
                                  : <String, String>{};

                              // Determine if a timestamp separator should be shown above this message.
                              bool showSeparator = false;
                              if (i == reversedDocs.length - 1) {
                                // Oldest message
                                showSeparator = true;
                              } else if (dt != null) {
                                final prevDoc = reversedDocs[i + 1];
                                final prevData =
