                                    prevDoc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                                final prevTs =
                                    prevData['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;
                                final prevDt = prevTs?.toDate();
                                if (prevDt != null) {
                                  // Show separator if gap >= 30 minutes from the previous (older) message.
                                  if (dt.difference(prevDt).inMinutes.abs() >=
                                      30) {
                                    showSeparator = true;
                                  }
                                }
                              }

                              return Column(
                                children: [
                                  if (showSeparator && dt != null)
                                    Padding(
                                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                        vertical: 10,
                                      ),
                                      child: Text(
                                        _formatMsgTimestamp(dt),
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: secondaryText,
                                          fontSize: 12,
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  _MessageBubble(
                                    messageId: msgId,
                                    text: unsent ? '' : decryptedText,
                                    isMine: isMine,
                                    isDark: isDark,
                                    unsent: unsent,
                                    edited: edited,
                                    replyTo: decryptedReply,
                                    reactions: reactions,
                                    isLastSentByMe: msgId == lastMineMsgId,
                                    seenByOther: _otherUnreadCount == 0,
                                    otherUserName: _dynamicOtherName,
                                    otherPhotoUrl: _dynamicOtherPhoto,
                                    currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
                                    onLongPress: () => _showMessageMenu(
                                      context,
                                      msgId,
                                      decryptedText,
                                      isMine,
                                      unsent,
                                      _dynamicOtherName,
                                    ),
                                    onReactionTap: (emoji) =>
                                        _addReaction(msgId, emoji),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              );
                            },
                          );
                        },
                      ),
              ),
            ),

            // ── Reply banner ───────────────────────────────────────────────
            if (_replyToMsg != null)
              Container(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: navBg,
                  border: Border(
                    top: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                  ),
                ),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 16,
                  vertical: 8,
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 3,
                      height: 36,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            'Reply to ${_replyToMsg!['senderName'] ?? ''}',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                              fontSize: 12,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 2),
                          Text(
                            _replyToMsg!['text'] as String? ?? '',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: secondaryText,
                              fontSize: 12,
                            ),
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            maxLines: 1,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: _cancelReply,
                      child: Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.xmark_circle_fill,
                        color: secondaryText,
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

            // ── Edit banner ────────────────────────────────────────────────
            if (_editingMsgId != null)
              Container(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: navBg,
                  border: Border(
                    top: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                  ),
                ),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 16,
                  vertical: 8,
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 3,
                      height: 36,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFFFF9500),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'Editing message',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Color(0xFFFF9500),
                          fontSize: 13,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: _cancelEdit,
                      child: Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.xmark_circle_fill,
                        color: secondaryText,
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

            // ── Input bar ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Container(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: navBg,
                border: Border(
                  top: BorderSide(
                    color: (_replyToMsg != null || _editingMsgId != null)
                        ? Colors.transparent
                        : separatorColor,
                    width: 0.5,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              child: SafeArea(
                top: false,
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 12,
                    vertical: 8,
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Container(
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: inputBg,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                          ),
                          child: TextField(
                            controller: _inputCtrl,
                            maxLines: null,
                            keyboardType: TextInputType.multiline,
                            textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,
                            style: TextStyle(color: primaryText, fontSize: 16),
                            decoration: InputDecoration(
                              border: InputBorder.none,
                              hintText: _editingMsgId != null
                                  ? 'Edit message…'
                                  : 'Message',
                              hintStyle: TextStyle(color: secondaryText),
                              contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: 16,
                                vertical: 10,
                              ),
                            ),
                            cursorColor: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                            onSubmitted: (_) => _sendMessage(),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      _SendButton(
                        onSend: _isSending ? null : _sendMessage,
                        isLoading: _isSending,
                        isEditMode: _editingMsgId != null,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  Enhanced send button widget
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _SendButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback? onSend;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool isEditMode;

  const _SendButton({
    required this.onSend,
    required this.isLoading,
    required this.isEditMode,
  });

  @override
  State<_SendButton> createState() => _SendButtonState();
}

class _SendButtonState extends State<_SendButton> {
  bool _isPressed = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    final Color baseColor = widget.isEditMode
        ? const Color(0xFFFF9500)
        : const Color(0xFF007AFF);
    final Color lightColor = widget.isEditMode
        ? const Color(0xFFFFB340)
        : const Color(0xFF40A0FF);

    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: widget.onSend == null
          ? null
          : (_) => setState(() => _isPressed = true),
      onTapUp: widget.onSend == null
          ? null
          : (_) => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      onTapCancel: widget.onSend == null
          ? null
          : () => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      onTap: widget.onSend,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 100),
        width: 40,
        height: 40,
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        transform: Matrix4.identity()..scale(_isPressed ? 0.92 : 1.0),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          shape: BoxShape.circle,
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topLeft,
            end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            colors: widget.isLoading
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                ? [baseColor.withOpacity(0.6), baseColor.withOpacity(0.6)]
                : [baseColor, lightColor],
          ),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
              color: baseColor.withOpacity(isDark ? 0.3 : 0.2),
              blurRadius: 8,
              offset: const Offset(0, 4),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: widget.isLoading
            ? const CupertinoActivityIndicator(color: Colors.white, radius: 10)
            : Icon(
                widget.isEditMode
                    ? CupertinoIcons.checkmark_alt
                    : CupertinoIcons.paperplane_fill,
                color: Colors.white,
                size: 22,
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SKELETON LOADER — reusable chat message placeholder
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class SkeletonLoader extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool isDark;
  final int itemCount;

  const SkeletonLoader({super.key, required this.isDark, this.itemCount = 6});

  @override
  State<SkeletonLoader> createState() => _SkeletonLoaderState();
}

class _SkeletonLoaderState extends State<SkeletonLoader>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;
  late Animation<double> _animation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1200),
    )..repeat(reverse: true);

    _animation = Tween<double>(begin: 0.4, end: 0.9).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(parent: _controller, curve: Curves.easeInOutSine),
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final baseColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
        : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA);

    final highlightColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF3A3A3C)
        : const Color(0xFFD1D1D6);

    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _animation,
      builder: (context, child) {
        final double t = _animation.value;
        final Color shimmerColor = Color.lerp(baseColor, highlightColor, t)!;

        return ListView.builder(
          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          shrinkWrap: true,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
          itemCount: widget.itemCount,
          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
            final bool isRight = index % 2 == 1;
            final double screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(context).size.width;
            final double bubbleWidth = (index % 3 == 0)
                ? screenWidth * 0.6
                : (index % 2 == 0 ? screenWidth * 0.4 : screenWidth * 0.5);

            return Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: isRight
                    ? MainAxisAlignment.end
                    : MainAxisAlignment.start,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  if (!isRight) ...[
                    Container(
                      width: 36,
                      height: 36,
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 10),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        color: shimmerColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                  Flexible(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: isRight
                          ? CrossAxisAlignment.end
                          : CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          width: bubbleWidth,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: 16,
                            vertical: 14,
                          ),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: shimmerColor,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
                              topLeft: const Radius.circular(18),
                              topRight: const Radius.circular(18),
                              bottomLeft: Radius.circular(isRight ? 18 : 4),
                              bottomRight: Radius.circular(isRight ? 4 : 18),
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Container(
                                width: double.infinity,
                                height: 10,
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: widget.isDark
                                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                      ? baseColor.withOpacity(0.5)
                                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                      : highlightColor.withOpacity(0.5),
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(5),
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (index % 3 == 0) ...[
                                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                Container(
                                  width: bubbleWidth * 0.6,
                                  height: 10,
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: widget.isDark
                                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                        ? baseColor.withOpacity(0.5)
                                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                        : highlightColor.withOpacity(0.5),
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(5),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (isRight) ...[
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Container(
                      width: 36,
                      height: 36,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        color: shimmerColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ARCHIVE CHATS SCREEN — Full-screen enhanced archive list (opened from Settings)
//  (Now with real‑time participant names & blue tick)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class ArchiveChats extends StatefulWidget {
  final String currentUserId;
  final bool isDark;

  const ArchiveChats({
    super.key,
    required this.currentUserId,
    required this.isDark,
  });

  @override
  State<ArchiveChats> createState() => _ArchiveChatsState();
}

class _ArchiveChatsState extends State<ArchiveChats> {
  final TextEditingController _searchCtrl = TextEditingController();
  String _searchQuery = '';
  int _totalArchived = 0;
  late final FocusNode _searchFocusNode;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _searchFocusNode = FocusNode();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchCtrl.dispose();
    _searchFocusNode.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final primaryText = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
        : const Color(0xFF000000);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);
    final bgColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF000000)
        : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final separatorColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final secondaryText = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);

    if (widget.currentUserId.isEmpty) {
      return Container(color: bgColor);
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: bgColor,
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Container(
            height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 40,
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: bgColor,
              border: Border(
                bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
              ),
            ),
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                    behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                    child: const Padding(
                      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 8),
                      child: Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.back,
                        color: iosBlue,
                        size: 22,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(
                    'Archive',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: primaryText,
                      fontSize: context.sp(25).clamp(15.0, 18.0),
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      letterSpacing: -0.2,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: SafeArea(
              top: false,
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  SizedBox(height: context.sp(6)),
                  Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(16)),
                    child: Container(
                      height: 36,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: widget.isDark
                            ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                            : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                      ),
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: _searchCtrl,
                        focusNode: _searchFocusNode,
                        autofocus: false,
                        onTapOutside: (event) => _searchFocusNode.unfocus(),
                        onChanged: (v) => setState(
                          () => _searchQuery = v.trim().toLowerCase(),
                        ),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: primaryText,
                          fontSize: context.sp(15),
                        ),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          border: InputBorder.none,
                          hintText: 'Search archived users',
                          hintStyle: TextStyle(
                            color: secondaryText,
                            fontSize: context.sp(15),
                          ),
                          prefixIcon: Icon(
                            CupertinoIcons.search,
                            color: secondaryText,
                            size: 16,
                          ),
                          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            vertical: 8,
                          ),
                          isDense: true,
                          suffixIcon: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty
                              ? GestureDetector(
                                  onTap: () {
                                    _searchCtrl.clear();
                                    setState(() => _searchQuery = '');
                                    _searchFocusNode.unfocus();
                                  },
                                  child: Icon(
                                    CupertinoIcons.xmark_circle_fill,
                                    color: secondaryText,
                                    size: 16,
                                  ),
                                )
                              : null,
                        ),
                        cursorColor: iosBlue,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  SizedBox(height: context.sp(8)),
                  Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(16)),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'Archived chats',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: secondaryText,
                            fontSize: context.sp(13).clamp(11.0, 15.0),
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const Spacer(),
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: 8,
                            vertical: 2,
                          ),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                            color: iosBlue.withOpacity(0.1),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            _totalArchived.toString(),
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: iosBlue,
                              fontSize: context.sp(13).clamp(11.0, 15.0),
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  SizedBox(height: context.sp(4)),
                  Expanded(
                    child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                          .collection('conversations')
                          .where(
                            'participants',
                            arrayContains: widget.currentUserId,
                          )
                          .snapshots(),
                      builder: (context, snapshot) {
                        if (snapshot.hasError) {
                          return Center(
                            child: Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                              child: Text(
                                'Could not load archives. Please check your connection.',
                                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: secondaryText,
                                  fontSize: 14,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }
                        if (snapshot.connectionState ==
                            ConnectionState.waiting) {
                          return const Center(
                            child: CupertinoActivityIndicator(),
                          );
                        }
                        if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
                          return _buildEmptyArchiveState(context, primaryText);
                        }
                        final allDocs = snapshot.data!.docs.toList()
                          ..sort((a, b) {
                            final aTime =
                                (a.data()
                                        as Map<
                                          String,
                                          dynamic
                                        >)['lastMessageTime']
                                    as Timestamp?;
                            final bTime =
                                (b.data()
                                        as Map<
                                          String,
                                          dynamic
                                        >)['lastMessageTime']
                                    as Timestamp?;
                            if (aTime == null && bTime == null) return 0;
                            if (aTime == null) return -1;
                            if (bTime == null) return 1;
                            return bTime.compareTo(aTime);
                          });
                        final archivedDocs = allDocs.where((doc) {
                          final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                          final archivedMap =
                              data['archived'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
                          return (archivedMap[widget.currentUserId] as bool?) ??
                              false;
                        }).toList();
                        if (_totalArchived != archivedDocs.length && mounted) {
                          WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
                            if (mounted) {
                              setState(
                                () => _totalArchived = archivedDocs.length,
                              );
                            }
                          });
                        }
                        final filtered = archivedDocs.where((doc) {
                          if (_searchQuery.isEmpty) return true;
                          final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                          final names =
                              (data['participantNames']
                                          as Map<String, dynamic>? ??
                                      {})
                                  .values
                                  .map(
                                    (v) => (v as String? ?? '').toLowerCase(),
                                  )
                                  .join(' ');
                          return names.contains(_searchQuery);
                        }).toList();
                        if (filtered.isEmpty) {
                          if (_searchQuery.isEmpty) {
                            return _buildEmptyArchiveState(
                              context,
                              primaryText,
                            );
                          } else {
                            return Center(
                              child: Text(
                                'No results for "$_searchQuery"',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: secondaryText,
                                  fontSize: context.sp(15),
                                ),
                              ),
                            );
                          }
                        }
                        return RefreshIndicator(
                          onRefresh: () async {
                            await Future.delayed(
                              const Duration(milliseconds: 800),
                            );
                          },
                          color: iosBlue,
                          child: ListView.builder(
                            physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                            itemCount: filtered.length,
                            itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                              final data =
                                  filtered[i].data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                              final conversationId = filtered[i].id;
                              final participants = List<String>.from(
                                data['participants'] ?? [],
                              );
                              final otherUid = participants.firstWhere(
                                (uid) => uid != widget.currentUserId,
                                orElse: () => '',
                              );
                              final participantNames =
                                  data['participantNames']
                                      as Map<String, dynamic>? ??
                                  {};
                              final participantPhotos =
                                  data['participantPhotos']
                                      as Map<String, dynamic>? ??
                                  {};
                              final otherName =
                                  (participantNames[otherUid] as String?)
                                      ?.trim() ??
                                  '';
                              final otherPhoto =
                                  (participantPhotos[otherUid] as String?) ??
                                  '';
                              final lastMessage =
                                  (data['lastMessage'] as String?) ?? '';
                              final lastSenderId =
                                  (data['lastSenderId'] as String?) ?? '';
                              final timestamp =
                                  data['lastMessageTime'] as Timestamp?;
                              final unreadCounts =
                                  data['unreadCounts']
                                      as Map<String, dynamic>? ??
                                  {};
                              final unreadCount =
                                  (unreadCounts[widget.currentUserId]
                                      as int?) ??
                                  0;
                              final otherUnreadCount =
                                  (unreadCounts[otherUid] as int?) ?? 1;
                              final isLastMine =
                                  lastSenderId == widget.currentUserId;
                              return _ConversationTile(
                                conversationId: conversationId,
                                otherUserId: otherUid,
                                otherName: otherName,
                                otherPhotoUrl: otherPhoto,
                                lastMessage: lastMessage,
                                timestamp: timestamp?.toDate(),
                                unreadCount: unreadCount,
                                otherUnreadCount: otherUnreadCount,
                                isLastMine: isLastMine,
                                currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
                                isDark: widget.isDark,
                                bgColor: bgColor,
                                separatorColor: separatorColor,
                                secondaryText: secondaryText,
                                primaryText: primaryText,
                                isArchiveTab: true,
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyArchiveState(BuildContext context, Color primaryText) {
    const accentColor = Color(0xFF007AFF);
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(40)),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Container(
              width: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
              height: context.sp(80).clamp(64.0, 96.0),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                color: widget.isDark
                    ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
                    : const Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    color: accentColor.withOpacity(widget.isDark ? 0.18 : 0.12),
                    blurRadius: 24,
                    spreadRadius: 2,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                  ),
                  BoxShadow(
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    color: Colors.black.withOpacity(
                      widget.isDark ? 0.20 : 0.06,
                    ),
                    blurRadius: 12,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 3),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: Icon(
                CupertinoIcons.archivebox_fill,
                size: context.sp(30).clamp(24.0, 38.0),
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                color: accentColor.withOpacity(0.55),
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: context.hp(22)),
            Text(
              'No Archived Chats',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: primaryText,
                fontSize: context.sp(19).clamp(16.0, 22.0),
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                letterSpacing: -0.4,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),
            Text(
              'Your archived conversations will appear here.',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: widget.isDark
                    ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                    : const Color(0xFF6D6D72),
                fontSize: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                height: 1.5,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  MESSAGE BUBBLE — unchanged except using already decrypted data
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
class _MessageBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  final String messageId;
  final String text;
  final bool isMine;
  final bool isDark;
  final bool unsent;
  final bool edited;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? replyTo;
  final Map<String, String> reactions;
  final bool isLastSentByMe;
  final bool seenByOther;
  final String otherUserName;
  final String otherPhotoUrl;
  final String currentUserId;
  final VoidCallback onLongPress;
  final void Function(String emoji) onReactionTap;

  const _MessageBubble({
    required this.messageId,
    required this.text,
    required this.isMine,
    required this.isDark,
    this.unsent = false,
    this.edited = false,
    this.replyTo,
    this.reactions = const {},
    this.isLastSentByMe = false,
    this.seenByOther = false,
    this.otherUserName = '',
    this.otherPhotoUrl = '',
    this.currentUserId = '',
    required this.onLongPress,
    required this.onReactionTap,
  });

  String _initialsForName(String name) {
    final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
    if (parts.isEmpty || parts.first.isEmpty) return '?';
    final first = parts.first[0].toUpperCase();
    final last = parts.length > 1 && parts.last.isNotEmpty
        ? parts.last[0].toUpperCase()
        : '';
    return '$first$last';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);
    final bubbleBg = isMine
        ? iosBlue
        : (isDark ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E) : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA));
    final textColor = isMine
        ? Colors.white
        : (isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black);
    final secondaryColor = isMine
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.75)
        : (isDark ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93) : const Color(0xFF6D6D72));
    final replyBg = isMine
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.18)
        : (isDark
              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
              ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.08)
              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
              : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.06));

    return GestureDetector(
      onLongPress: onLongPress,
      child: Align(
        alignment: isMine ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: isMine
              ? CrossAxisAlignment.end
              : CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            // Bubble
            Container(
              margin: EdgeInsets.only(
                bottom: reactions.isNotEmpty ? 0 : 4,
                left: isMine ? 60 : 0,
                right: isMine ? 0 : 60,
              ),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: unsent
                    ? (isDark
                          ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                          : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA))
                    : bubbleBg,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
                  topLeft: const Radius.circular(18),
                  topRight: const Radius.circular(18),
                  bottomLeft: Radius.circular(isMine ? 18 : 4),
                  bottomRight: Radius.circular(isMine ? 4 : 18),
                ),
              ),
              child: unsent
                  ? Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 14,
                        vertical: 10,
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Icon(
                            CupertinoIcons.arrow_uturn_left_circle,
                            size: 14,
                            color: isDark
                                ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                                : const Color(0xFF6D6D72),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 5),
                          Text(
                            'Message unsent',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: isDark
                                  ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                                  : const Color(0xFF6D6D72),
                              fontSize: 14,
                              fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                              height: 1.4,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    )
                  : Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 14,
                        vertical: 10,
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          if (replyTo != null) ...[
                            Container(
                              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: 10,
                                vertical: 6,
                              ),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: replyBg,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                border: Border(
                                  left: BorderSide(
                                    color: isMine
                                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                        ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7)
                                        : iosBlue,
                                    width: 3,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              child: Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                children: [
                                  Text(
                                    replyTo!['senderName'] as String? ?? '',
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      color: isMine
                                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                          ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9)
                                          : iosBlue,
                                      fontSize: 11,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                                  Text(
                                    replyTo!['text'] as String? ?? '',
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      color: secondaryColor,
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                    ),
                                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                    maxLines: 2,
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                          Text(
                            text,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: textColor,
                              fontSize: 16,
                              height: 1.4,
                            ),
                          ),
                          if (edited) ...[
                            const SizedBox(height: 2),
                            Text(
                              'edited',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: secondaryColor,
                                fontSize: 10,
                                fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
            // Reactions
            if (reactions.isNotEmpty)
              Container(
                margin: EdgeInsets.only(
                  bottom: 4,
                  left: isMine ? 60 : 0,
                  right: isMine ? 0 : 60,
                ),
                child: Wrap(spacing: 4, children: _buildReactionChips(context)),
              ),
            // Seen / Delivered indicator
            if (isLastSentByMe && seenByOther)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 2, right: 2),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Seen',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: iosBlue,
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 3),
                    _GradientAvatar(
                      initials: _initialsForName(otherUserName),
                      gradientColors: _gradientForName(otherUserName),
                      size: 14,
                      fontSize: 6,
                      photoUrl: otherPhotoUrl.isNotEmpty ? otherPhotoUrl : null,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            if (isLastSentByMe && !seenByOther)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 2, right: 2),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const SizedBox(width: 2),
                    Text(
                      'Delivered',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        color: iosBlue.withOpacity(0.7),
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  List<Widget> _buildReactionChips(BuildContext context) {
    final grouped = <String, List<String>>{};
    reactions.forEach((uid, emoji) {
      grouped.putIfAbsent(emoji, () => []).add(uid);
    });
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    return grouped.entries.map((entry) {
      final emoji = entry.key;
      final count = entry.value.length;
      final iReacted = entry.value.contains(currentUserId);
      return GestureDetector(
        onTap: () => onReactionTap(emoji),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 7, vertical: 3),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: iReacted
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                ? iosBlue.withOpacity(0.18)
                : (isDark ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E) : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA)),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(
              color: iReacted ? iosBlue : Colors.transparent,
              width: 1,
            ),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Text(emoji, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
              if (count > 1) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 3),
                Text(
                  '$count',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11,
                    color: iReacted
                        ? iosBlue
                        : (isDark
                              ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                              : const Color(0xFF6D6D72)),
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    }).toList();
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Used by: MyHomePage nav bar, MyProfile, BiographPage, _ConversationTile,
//          ChatScreen, _UserSearchScreen
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class _GradientAvatar extends StatefulWidget {
  final String initials;
  final List<Color> gradientColors;
  final double size;
  final double fontSize;
  final String? photoUrl;
  final bool isLoading; // true while parent's Firestore fetch is pending

  const _GradientAvatar({
    required this.initials,
    required this.gradientColors,
    required this.size,
    required this.fontSize,
    this.photoUrl,
    this.isLoading = false,
  });

  @override
  State<_GradientAvatar> createState() => _GradientAvatarState();
}

class _GradientAvatarState extends State<_GradientAvatar> {
  // Phase 1 flag — cleared after the blur window expires or photo is ready
  bool _showBlur = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // FIX: Skip blur phase entirely if the photo is already available on
    // first build (e.g., BiographPage where URL is passed in directly, or
    // MyProfile when Firestore responded before the first frame).
    final hasPhoto = widget.photoUrl != null && widget.photoUrl!.isNotEmpty;
    if (!widget.isLoading && hasPhoto) {
      _showBlur = false;
      return;
    }
    // Show the blurred gradient for 350 ms, then reveal the real state.
    Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 350), () {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _showBlur = false);
    });
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(_GradientAvatar oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    // FIX: If the photo URL just became available before the 350ms timer
    // fires, clear the blur immediately so the photo shows without delay.
    if (_showBlur &&
        !widget.isLoading &&
        (widget.photoUrl?.isNotEmpty ?? false)) {
      setState(() => _showBlur = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // ── Phase 1: gradient colour blur ────────────────────────────────────
    if (_showBlur) {
      return _buildBlurState();
    }

    // ── Phase 2: iOS loader while Firestore fetch is pending ─────────────
    if (widget.isLoading) {
      return _buildLoadingState();
    }

    // ── Phase 3: photo or gradient-initials ──────────────────────────────
    final hasPhoto = widget.photoUrl != null && widget.photoUrl!.isNotEmpty;
    return hasPhoto ? _buildPhotoState() : _buildGradientInitials();
  }

  // Blurred gradient — Phase 1
  Widget _buildBlurState() {
    return SizedBox(
      width: widget.size,
      height: widget.size,
      child: ClipOval(
        child: ImageFiltered(
          imageFilter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 14, sigmaY: 14),
          child: Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                colors: widget.gradientColors,
                begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                end: Alignment.bottomRight,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // iOS spinner on gradient bg — Phase 2 / network loading placeholder
  // NO box shadow — glowing effect removed.
  Widget _buildLoadingState() {
    return Container(
      width: widget.size,
      height: widget.size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: widget.gradientColors,
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        // box shadow intentionally omitted (glow removed)
      ),
      child: Center(
        child: CupertinoActivityIndicator(
          color: Colors.white,
          radius: (widget.size * 0.13).clamp(8.0, 14.0),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // Network image — Phase 3 (photo set)
  // NO box shadow — glowing effect removed.
  Widget _buildPhotoState() {
    return SizedBox(
      width: widget.size,
      height: widget.size,
      child: ClipOval(
        child: Image.network(
          widget.photoUrl!,
          width: widget.size,
          height: widget.size,
          fit: BoxFit.cover,
          // FIX [7]: show iOS loader while the network image streams in.
          loadingBuilder: (_, child, loadingProgress) {
            if (loadingProgress == null) return child;
            return _buildLoadingState();
          },
          // On permanent network error fall back to gradient initials
          errorBuilder: (_, _, _) => _buildGradientInitials(),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // Gradient + initials fallback
  // NO box shadow — glowing effect removed.
  Widget _buildGradientInitials() {
    return Container(
      width: widget.size,
      height: widget.size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: widget.gradientColors,
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        // box shadow intentionally omitted (glow removed)
      ),
      child: Center(
        child: Text(
          widget.initials.isEmpty ? '?' : widget.initials,
          style: TextStyle(
            color: Colors.white,
            fontSize: widget.fontSize,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            letterSpacing: 0.5,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  VIEW PICTURE (Improved low-quality profile photo viewer)
//  Used by MyProfile & BiographPage when user taps a set profile picture
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class ViewPicture extends StatelessWidget {
  final String imageUrl;

  const ViewPicture({super.key, required this.imageUrl});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: isDark
          ? const Color(0xFF000000)
          : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          // ── Blurred background using same image ─────────────────────────────
          Positioned.fill(
            child: Stack(
              fit: StackFit.expand,
              children: [
                Image.network(
                  imageUrl,
                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.3),
                  colorBlendMode: BlendMode.darken,
                  loadingBuilder: (context, child, loadingProgress) {
                    if (loadingProgress == null) return child;
                    return const Center(
                      child: CupertinoActivityIndicator(
                        color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                        radius: 14,
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                  errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) =>
                      Container(color: isDark ? Colors.black : Colors.white),
                ),
                BackdropFilter(
                  filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 12, sigmaY: 12),
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  child: Container(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.1)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          // ── Centered non-fullscreen image with polish ───────────────────────
          Center(
            child: ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              child: Container(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  boxShadow: [
                    BoxShadow(
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.25),
                      blurRadius: 14,
                      offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                child: Image.network(
                  imageUrl,
                  width: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.7,
                  height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.7,
                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                  filterQuality: FilterQuality.high, // smoothness improvement
                  loadingBuilder: (context, child, loadingProgress) {
                    if (loadingProgress == null) return child;
                    return const Center(
                      child: CupertinoActivityIndicator(
                        color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                        radius: 14,
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                  errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          Icons.error_outline_rounded,
                          color: isDark ? Colors.white54 : Colors.black54,
                          size: 64,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        Text(
                          'Failed to load image',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: isDark ? Colors.white54 : Colors.black54,
                            fontSize: 16,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),

          // ── Custom iOS-style nav bar (dark/light aware) ────────────────────
          Positioned(
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
            right: 0,
            child: Container(
              height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 44,
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isDark
                    ? const Color(0xFF000000)
                    : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7),
                border: Border(
                  bottom: BorderSide(
                    color: isDark
                        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
                        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8),
                    width: 0.5,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(4)),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    // Back button
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: context.sp(12),
                          vertical: context.sp(4),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.back,
                              color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                              size: context.sp(20),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: context.sp(4)),
                            Text(
                              'Back',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    // Title
                    Text(
                      'Profile Picture',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: isDark
                            ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
                            : const Color(0xFF000000),
                        fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 18.0),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        letterSpacing: -0.2,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    // Balance spacer (matches back button width)
                    SizedBox(
                      width:
                          context.sp(12) * 2 +
                          context.sp(20) +
                          context.sp(4) +
                          32,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  MY PROFILE (logout logic & UI removed – now only profile management + Blue Tick display)
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
class MyProfile extends StatefulWidget {
  const MyProfile({super.key});

  @override
  State<MyProfile> createState() => _MyProfileState();
}

class _MyProfileState extends State<MyProfile> with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  // ── User data ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  String _firstName = '';
  String _lastName = '';
  String _profilePhotoUrl = '';
  bool _isUpdatingPhoto = false;
  bool _isLoadingPhoto = true;
  List<Color> _avatarGradient = [
    const Color(0xFF007AFF),
    const Color(0xFF5AC8FA),
  ];
  bool _hasBlueTick = false; // NEW: Blue Tick status for current user

  // ── NEW: Real-time subscription on the current user's Firestore document.
  //         Any change to firstName / lastName / profilePhotoUrl /
  //         blueTickExpiry on the backend instantly reflects in the UI.
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>>? _userSub;

  // ── Staggered entrance animations ─────────────────────────────────────────
  late final AnimationController _entranceCtrl;
  late final Animation<double> _avatarFade;
  late final Animation<double> _avatarScale;
  late final Animation<double> _infoFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _infoSlide;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initAnimations();
    _loadUser();
  }

  void _initAnimations() {
    _entranceCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 750),
    );

    _avatarFade = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.00, 0.45, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _avatarScale = Tween<double>(begin: 0.65, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.00, 0.50, curve: Curves.elasticOut),
      ),
    );
    _infoFade = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.25, 0.70, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _infoSlide = Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.25), end: Offset.zero)
        .animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _entranceCtrl,
            curve: const Interval(0.25, 0.70, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
          ),
        );

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (mounted) _entranceCtrl.forward();
    });
  }

  Future<void> _loadUser() async {
    final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    if (user == null) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoadingPhoto = false);
      return;
    }

    final email = (user.email ?? '').trim();
    bool foundInPrefs = false;

    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final raw = prefs.getString('nexvibe_saved_accounts_v1');
      if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
        final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
        if (decoded is List) {
          for (final item in decoded) {
            if (item is Map<String, dynamic> && item['email'] == email) {
              final account = _SavedAccount.fromJson(item);
              if (!mounted) return;
              setState(() {
                _firstName = account.firstName;
                _lastName = account.lastName;
                _avatarGradient = account.gradientColors;
                final p = account.profilePhotoUrl;
                if (p != null && p.isNotEmpty) {
                  _profilePhotoUrl = p;
                  _isLoadingPhoto = false;
                }
              });
              foundInPrefs = true;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    if (!foundInPrefs) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _firstName = email.split('@').first;
        _avatarGradient = _gradientForEmail(email);
      });
    }

    if (!mounted) return;

    // ── NEW: subscribe to the user's Firestore doc for real-time sync.
    //  Any backend change to firstName / lastName / profilePhotoUrl /
    //  blueTickExpiry will instantly update this screen.
    await _userSub?.cancel();
    _userSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('users')
        .doc(user.uid)
        .snapshots()
        .listen(
          (doc) {
            if (!mounted) return;
            if (!doc.exists) {
              setState(() => _isLoadingPhoto = false);
              return;
            }
            final data = doc.data()!;
            final newFirst = (data['firstName'] as String?) ?? _firstName;
            final newLast = (data['lastName'] as String?) ?? _lastName;
            final photoUrl = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
            final expiry = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
            final active =
                expiry != null && expiry.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
            setState(() {
              _firstName = newFirst;
              _lastName = newLast;
              _profilePhotoUrl = photoUrl;
              _isLoadingPhoto = false;
              _hasBlueTick = active;
            });
          },
          onError: (_) {
            if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoadingPhoto = false);
          },
        );
  }

  String get _initials {
    final f = _firstName.isNotEmpty ? _firstName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final l = _lastName.isNotEmpty ? _lastName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final result = '$f$l';
    return result.isNotEmpty ? result : '?';
  }

  String get _displayName => '$_firstName $_lastName'.trim();

  void _showConfirmDialog({
    required String title,
    required VoidCallback onConfirm,
  }) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    showCupertinoDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: true,
      builder: (ctx) => CupertinoTheme(
        data: CupertinoThemeData(
          brightness: isDark ? Brightness.dark : Brightness.light,
        ),
        child: CupertinoAlertDialog(
          title: Text(
            title,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
              fontSize: 16,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            CupertinoDialogAction(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
              child: const Text('Cancel'),
            ),
            CupertinoDialogAction(
              isDestructiveAction: true,
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
                onConfirm();
              },
              child: const Text('Yes'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showRemovePhotoConfirm() {
    _showConfirmDialog(
      title: 'Are you sure to remove profile picture?',
      onConfirm: _doRemoveProfilePicture,
    );
  }

  Future<bool> _showSetProfilePictureDialog() async {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    final cardBg = isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
    final primary = isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final secondary = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    final r = await showGeneralDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: true,
      barrierLabel: MaterialLocalizations.of(context).modalBarrierDismissLabel,
      transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 320),
      transitionBuilder: (ctx, anim, _, child) {
        return FadeTransition(
          opacity: anim,
          child: ScaleTransition(
            scale: Tween<double>(begin: 0.92, end: 1.0).animate(
              CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
            ),
            child: child,
          ),
        );
      },
      pageBuilder: (ctx, _, _) {
        return AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: cardBg,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          ),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 68,
                height: 68,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  color: const Color(0xFF007AFF).withOpacity(0.12),
                ),
                child: const Icon(
                  Icons.photo_camera_rounded,
                  color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                  size: 34,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Text(
                'Set profile picture',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  color: primary,
                  height: 1.25,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                'Choose a photo from your gallery. It appears on your avatar in chats, home, and saved accounts.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: secondary, height: 1.4),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(false),
              child: Text('Cancel', style: TextStyle(color: secondary)),
            ),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(true),
              child: const Text(
                'Continue',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
    return r ?? false;
  }

  void _showProfilePhotoSuccess() {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    showCupertinoDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => CupertinoTheme(
        data: CupertinoThemeData(
          brightness: isDark ? Brightness.dark : Brightness.light,
        ),
        child: CupertinoAlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Success'),
          content: const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
            child: Text('Your profile picture was updated.'),
          ),
          actions: [
            CupertinoDialogAction(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
              child: const Text('OK'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _viewProfilePicture() {
    if (_profilePhotoUrl.isEmpty) return;
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => ViewPicture(imageUrl: _profilePhotoUrl),
        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => FadeTransition(
          opacity: CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOut),
          child: child,
        ),
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _changeProfilePicture() async {
    final proceed = await _showSetProfilePictureDialog();
    if (!proceed || !mounted) return;

    try {
      final picker = ImagePicker();
      final picked = await picker.pickImage(
        source: ImageSource.gallery,
        imageQuality: 25,
        maxWidth: 400,
        maxHeight: 400,
      );
      if (picked == null || !mounted) return;

      setState(() => _isUpdatingPhoto = true);

      final file = File(picked.path);
      final url = await _uploadToCloudinary(file);

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (url == null) {
        setState(() => _isUpdatingPhoto = false);
        _showErrorSnack('Upload failed. Please try again.');
        return;
      }

      final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
      if (user == null) {
        setState(() => _isUpdatingPhoto = false);
        return;
      }

      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('users').doc(user.uid).update(
        {'profilePhotoUrl': url},
      );

      final email = (user.email ?? '').trim();
      await _updateSavedAccountsProfilePhotoInPrefs(email, url);
      await _propagateParticipantPhotosForUser(user.uid, url);

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _profilePhotoUrl = url;
          _isUpdatingPhoto = false;
        });
        _showProfilePhotoSuccess();
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isUpdatingPhoto = false);
        _showErrorSnack('Something went wrong. Please try again.');
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _doRemoveProfilePicture() async {
    final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    if (user == null) return;
    try {
      setState(() => _isUpdatingPhoto = true);
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('users').doc(user.uid).update(
        {'profilePhotoUrl': ''},
      );
      final email = (user.email ?? '').trim();
      await _updateSavedAccountsProfilePhotoInPrefs(email, null);
      await _propagateParticipantPhotosForUser(user.uid, '');
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _profilePhotoUrl = '';
          _isUpdatingPhoto = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isUpdatingPhoto = false);
        _showErrorSnack('Could not remove photo. Please try again.');
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _updateSavedAccountsProfilePhotoInPrefs(
    String email,
    String? url,
  ) async {
    if (email.isEmpty) return;
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final raw = prefs.getString('nexvibe_saved_accounts_v1');
      if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
        final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
        if (decoded is List) {
          final updated = decoded.map((item) {
            if (item is Map<String, dynamic> && item['email'] == email) {
              final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(item);
              map['profilePhotoUrl'] = url ?? '';
              return map;
            }
            return item;
