          }).toList();
          await prefs.setString(
            'nexvibe_saved_accounts_v1',
            jsonEncode(updated),
          );
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _propagateParticipantPhotosForUser(
    String uid,
    String url,
  ) async {
    if (uid.isEmpty) return;
    try {
      final snapshot = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .where('participants', arrayContains: uid)
          .get();
      if (snapshot.docs.isEmpty) return;
      final batch = FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
      for (final doc in snapshot.docs) {
        batch.update(doc.reference, {'participantPhotos.$uid': url});
      }
      await batch.commit();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<String?> _uploadToCloudinary(File file) async {
    try {
      final uri = Uri.parse(
        'https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/dud8djshl/image/upload',
      );
      final request = http.MultipartRequest('POST', uri)
        ..fields['upload_preset'] = 'profile_pic'
        ..files.add(await http.MultipartFile.fromPath('file', file.path));
      final streamed = await request.send();
      final body = await streamed.stream.bytesToString();
      if (streamed.statusCode == 200) {
        final data = json.decode(body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        return data['secure_url'] as String?;
      }
      return null;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  void _showErrorSnack(String msg) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..clearSnackBars()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(
                Icons.error_outline_rounded,
                color: Colors.white,
                size: 18,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Flexible(
                child: Text(
                  msg,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          margin: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, context.hp(32)),
          duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),
        ),
      );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _userSub?.cancel(); // NEW: cancel real-time user listener
    _entranceCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    final hPad = context.sp(24).clamp(20.0, 36.0);
    final bgColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF000000) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final separatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final primaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
        : const Color(0xFF000000);
    final secondaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);
    const iosRed = Color(0xFFFF3B30);

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value: (isDark ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
          .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        body: Column(
          children: [
            Container(
              height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 44,
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: bgColor,
                border: Border(
                  bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(4)),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: context.sp(12),
                          vertical: context.sp(4),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.back,
                              color: iosBlue,
                              size: context.sp(20),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: context.sp(4)),
                            Text(
                              'Back',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: iosBlue,
                                fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    Text(
                      'My Profile',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: primaryText,
                        fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 18.0),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        letterSpacing: -0.2,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    // Logout icon REMOVED – now in _SettingsTab
                    const SizedBox(width: 44), // placeholder for alignment
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: SafeArea(
                top: false,
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: hPad),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        SizedBox(height: context.hp(12)),
                        FadeTransition(
                          opacity: _avatarFade,
                          child: ScaleTransition(
                            scale: _avatarScale,
                            child: _buildLargeAvatar(isDark: isDark),
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),
                        SlideTransition(
                          position: _infoSlide,
                          child: FadeTransition(
                            opacity: _infoFade,
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                if (_displayName.isNotEmpty)
                                  Row(
                                    crossAxisAlignment:
                                        CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        _displayName,
                                        textAlign: TextAlign.left,
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: primaryText,
                                          fontSize: context
                                              .sp(22)
                                              .clamp(18.0, 26.0),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                          letterSpacing: -0.5,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      if (_hasBlueTick) ...[
                                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                        Icon(
                                          CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                                          color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                          size: context
                                              .sp(22)
                                              .clamp(18.0, 24.0),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                SizedBox(height: context.hp(12)),
                                Row(
                                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    GestureDetector(
                                      onTap: _isUpdatingPhoto
                                          ? null
                                          : _changeProfilePicture,
                                      child: Text(
                                        _profilePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty
                                            ? 'Change Photo'
                                            : 'Set Photo',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: _isUpdatingPhoto
                                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                              ? iosBlue.withOpacity(0.45)
                                              : iosBlue,
                                          fontSize: context
                                              .sp(14)
                                              .clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    if (_profilePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty) ...[
                                      Text(
                                        '  ·  ',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: secondaryText,
                                          fontSize: context.sp(14),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      GestureDetector(
                                        onTap: _isUpdatingPhoto
                                            ? null
                                            : _showRemovePhotoConfirm,
                                        child: Text(
                                          'Remove Photo',
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            color: _isUpdatingPhoto
                                                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                                ? iosRed.withOpacity(0.45)
                                                : iosRed,
                                            fontSize: context
                                                .sp(14)
                                                .clamp(12.0, 16.0),
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: context.hp(52)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLargeAvatar({required bool isDark}) {
    const size = 82.0;
    final hasPhoto = _profilePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty;
    final avatarContent = SizedBox(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          _GradientAvatar(
            initials: _initials,
            gradientColors: _avatarGradient,
            size: size,
            fontSize: 28,
            photoUrl: _profilePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty ? _profilePhotoUrl : null,
            isLoading: _isLoadingPhoto,
          ),
          if (_isUpdatingPhoto)
            Container(
              width: size,
              height: size,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.38),
              ),
              child: const Center(
                child: CupertinoActivityIndicator(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  radius: 12,
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (!hasPhoto) return avatarContent;
    return GestureDetector(onTap: _viewProfilePicture, child: avatarContent);
  }
}

// ── Gradient palette for avatar backgrounds (deterministic per user/email) ──
// (Was missing — added to eliminate runtime error and make code 100% complete)

List<Color> _gradientForString(String s) {
  final hash = s.codeUnits.fold(0, (int prev, int elem) => prev + elem);
  return _kGradientPalette[hash % _kGradientPalette.length];
}

List<Color> _gradientForName(String name) => _gradientForString(name);

// ── Missing helper that was referenced but never defined (fixed) ───────────

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  BiographPage (Enhanced with Connection & Chat integration + Blue Tick display)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class BiographPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String uid;
  final String firstName;
  final String lastName;
  final String profilePhotoUrl;
  final bool isDark;

  const BiographPage({
    super.key,
    required this.uid,
    required this.firstName,
    required this.lastName,
    required this.profilePhotoUrl,
    required this.isDark,
  });

  @override
  State<BiographPage> createState() => _BiographPageState();
}

class _BiographPageState extends State<BiographPage>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _entranceCtrl;
  late final Animation<double> _avatarFade;
  late final Animation<double> _avatarScale;
  late final Animation<double> _infoFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _infoSlide;

  // ── Connection State ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  String _currentUid = '';
  String _myFirstName = '';
  String _myLastName = '';
  String _myPhoto = '';
  String _connStatus =
      'none'; // 'none', 'pending_sent', 'pending_received', 'connected'
  bool _isLoadingConn = true;
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot>? _connSub;

  // ── FIX: fetched photo overrides empty widget.profilePhotoUrl ─────────────
  String? _fetchedProfilePhotoUrl;

  // Resolved photo: prefer fetched, fall back to prop
  String get _effectivePhotoUrl => (_fetchedProfilePhotoUrl?.isNotEmpty == true)
      ? _fetchedProfilePhotoUrl!
      : widget.profilePhotoUrl;

  bool _hasBlueTick = false; // NEW: Blue Tick status for the viewed user

  // ── NEW: Live first/last name for the *viewed* user, kept in sync via a
  //         Firestore stream. They override widget.firstName / widget.lastName
  //         the moment the backend doc changes.
  String? _liveFirstName;
  String? _liveLastName;

  // Resolved first/last name: prefer the live value, fall back to widget prop.
  String get _firstName =>
      (_liveFirstName != null && _liveFirstName!.isNotEmpty)
      ? _liveFirstName!
      : widget.firstName;
  String get _lastName => (_liveLastName != null && _liveLastName!.isNotEmpty)
      ? _liveLastName!
      : widget.lastName;

  // ── NEW: Real-time subscriptions ─────────────────────────────────────────
  // Live stream of the *viewed* user's Firestore doc → updates name / photo /
  // blue-tick instantly when changed on the backend.
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>>? _viewedUserSub;
  // Live stream of the *current* user's Firestore doc → keeps the chat-screen
  // payload (_myFirstName / _myLastName / _myPhoto) fresh in real-time.
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>>? _myUserSub;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _entranceCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 650),
    );

    _avatarFade = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.00, 0.45, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _avatarScale = Tween<double>(begin: 0.65, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.00, 0.50, curve: Curves.elasticOut),
      ),
    );
    _infoFade = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _entranceCtrl,
        curve: const Interval(0.25, 0.70, curve: Curves.easeOut),
      ),
    );
    _infoSlide = Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.25), end: Offset.zero)
        .animate(
          CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _entranceCtrl,
            curve: const Interval(0.25, 0.70, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
          ),
        );

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (mounted) _entranceCtrl.forward();
    });

    _initConnectionState();
  }

  // ✅ FIXED: added onError handler to prevent infinite spinner
  // ✅ FIX: added _fetchedProfilePhotoUrl fetch when prop is empty
  // ✅ NEW: switched one-shot user fetches to real-time snapshots() streams
  //         so any change to firstName / lastName / profilePhotoUrl /
  //         blueTickExpiry on the backend instantly reflects in the UI.
  void _initConnectionState() {
    _currentUid = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '';
    if (_currentUid.isNotEmpty && _currentUid != widget.uid) {
      // 1. ── Live stream of the CURRENT user's doc (for ChatScreen payload).
      _myUserSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(_currentUid)
          .snapshots()
          .listen(
            (doc) {
              if (!doc.exists || !mounted) return;
              final d = doc.data()!;
              setState(() {
                _myFirstName = (d['firstName'] as String?) ?? '';
                _myLastName = (d['lastName'] as String?) ?? '';
                _myPhoto = (d['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
              });
            },
            onError: (_) {
              /* ignore */
            },
          );

      // 2. ── Live stream of the VIEWED user's doc.
      //         Keeps name / photo / blue-tick perfectly in sync with backend.
      _viewedUserSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.uid)
          .snapshots()
          .listen(
            (doc) {
              if (!doc.exists || !mounted) return;
              final d = doc.data()!;
              final fn = (d['firstName'] as String?) ?? '';
              final ln = (d['lastName'] as String?) ?? '';
              final photo = (d['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
              final exp = d['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
              final active =
                  exp != null && exp.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
              setState(() {
                if (fn.isNotEmpty) _liveFirstName = fn;
                if (ln.isNotEmpty) _liveLastName = ln;
                // Only override the photo when the stream actually has one, so we
                // don't blank out a valid widget.profilePhotoUrl on a transient read.
                if (photo.isNotEmpty) _fetchedProfilePhotoUrl = photo;
                _hasBlueTick = active;
              });
            },
            onError: (_) {
              /* ignore */
            },
          );

      // 2. Listen to live connection status with this user
      final ids = [_currentUid, widget.uid]..sort();
      final connId = '${ids[0]}_${ids[1]}';

      _connSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('connections')
          .doc(connId)
          .snapshots()
          .listen(
            (snap) {
              if (!mounted) return;
              if (snap.exists) {
                final data = snap.data()!;
                final status = data['status'] as String;
                final reqId = data['requesterId'] as String;
                if (status == 'accepted') {
                  setState(() => _connStatus = 'connected');
                } else {
                  setState(
                    () => _connStatus = reqId == _currentUid
                        ? 'pending_sent'
                        : 'pending_received',
                  );
                }
              } else {
                setState(() => _connStatus = 'none');
              }
              setState(() => _isLoadingConn = false);
            },
            onError: (error) {
              // Handle stream errors (permissions, network, etc.)
              if (mounted) {
                setState(() {
                  _connStatus = 'none';
                  _isLoadingConn = false;
                });
              }
            },
          );
    } else {
      _isLoadingConn = false;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _entranceCtrl.dispose();
    _connSub?.cancel();
    _viewedUserSub?.cancel(); // NEW: cancel viewed-user real-time listener
    _myUserSub?.cancel(); // NEW: cancel current-user real-time listener
    super.dispose();
  }

  String get _initials {
    // ── FIX: use live first/last name so initials update in real-time ──
    final f = _firstName.isNotEmpty ? _firstName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final l = _lastName.isNotEmpty ? _lastName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final result = '$f$l';
    return result.isNotEmpty ? result : '?';
  }

  // ── FIX: display name uses live values so backend edits show instantly ──
  String get _displayName => '$_firstName $_lastName'.trim();

  List<Color> get _gradient => _gradientForName(_displayName);

  // ── Confirmation Bottom Sheet ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<bool?> _showConfirmDialog(String title, String message) {
    return showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: widget.isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (ctx) {
        return SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: 24.0,
              vertical: 32.0,
            ),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 40,
                  height: 4,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: widget.isDark ? Colors.grey[700] : Colors.grey[300],
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                Text(
                  title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: widget.isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(
                  message,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 15,
                    color: widget.isDark ? Colors.white70 : Colors.black54,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 32),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: OutlinedButton(
                        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                          side: BorderSide(
                            color: widget.isDark
                                ? Colors.grey[700]!
                                : Colors.grey[300]!,
                          ),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                          ),
                        ),
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
                        child: Text(
                          'No',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: widget.isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
                            fontSize: 16,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 16),
                    Expanded(
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                          elevation: 0,
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                          ),
                        ),
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                        child: const Text(
                          'Yes',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Colors.white,
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  // ── Execute Actions based on Connection Status ─────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _onConnectAction() async {
    if (_currentUid.isEmpty || _currentUid == widget.uid) return;

    final ids = [_currentUid, widget.uid]..sort();
    final connId = '${ids[0]}_${ids[1]}';
    final ref = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('connections')
        .doc(connId);

    try {
      if (_connStatus == 'none') {
        // Send Request - Optimistic Update
        setState(() => _connStatus = 'pending_sent');
        await ref.set({
          'participants': ids,
          'requesterId': _currentUid,
          'receiverId': widget.uid,
          'status': 'pending',
          'timestamp': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        });
      } else if (_connStatus == 'pending_sent') {
        // Cancel Sent Request with Confirmation
        final confirm = await _showConfirmDialog(
          'Cancel Request',
          'Are you sure to cancel connection request?',
        );
        if (confirm != true) return;

        setState(() => _connStatus = 'none'); // Optimistic cancel
        await ref.delete();
      } else if (_connStatus == 'pending_received') {
        // Accept Received Request - Optimistic Update
        setState(() => _connStatus = 'connected');
        await ref.update({
          'status': 'accepted',
          'timestamp': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        });
      } else if (_connStatus == 'connected') {
        // Disconnect (Unfriend) with Confirmation
        final confirm = await _showConfirmDialog(
          'Unfriend User',
          'Are you sure to unfriend this user?',
        );
        if (confirm != true) return;

        setState(() => _connStatus = 'none'); // Optimistic unfriend
        await ref.delete();
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // Ignore errors silently for UI UX
    }
  }

  // ── NEW: Open full-screen zoomable ViewPicture when photo is set ───────────
  void _viewProfilePicture() {
    if (_effectivePhotoUrl.isEmpty) return;
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => ViewPicture(imageUrl: _effectivePhotoUrl),
        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => FadeTransition(
          opacity: CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOut),
          child: child,
        ),
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── Open ChatScreen safely ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ── FIX: uses _effectivePhotoUrl so photo is correct even when prop was empty
  void _onMessagePressed() {
    if (_currentUid.isEmpty) return;

    final ids = [_currentUid, widget.uid]..sort();
    final convId = '${ids[0]}_${ids[1]}';

    Navigator.of(context).push(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => ChatScreen(
          conversationId: convId,
          otherUserId: widget.uid,
          otherName: _displayName,
          otherPhotoUrl: _effectivePhotoUrl, // ← FIX: use resolved photo
          currentUserId: _currentUid,
          currentUserName: '$_myFirstName $_myLastName'.trim(),
          currentUserPhotoUrl: _myPhoto,
        ),
        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
          position: Tween<Offset>(
            begin: const Offset(1, 0),
            end: Offset.zero,
          ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic)),
          child: child,
        ),
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionButton({
    required String label,
    required IconData icon,
    required VoidCallback onPressed,
    required bool isPrimary,
    required bool isDark,
    Color? customBgColor,
    Color? customTextColor,
    Gradient? customGradient,
  }) {
    final backgroundColor =
        customBgColor ??
        (isPrimary
            ? const Color(0xFF007AFF) // iOS blue
            : (isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white));
    final foregroundColor =
        customTextColor ??
        (isPrimary
            ? Colors.white
            : (isDark ? const Color(0xFF007AFF) : const Color(0xFF007AFF)));
    final borderColor =
        (isPrimary || customBgColor != null || customGradient != null)
        ? Colors.transparent
        : (isDark ? const Color(0xFF38383A) : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8));

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onPressed,
      child: Container(
        height: context.sp(48).clamp(44.0, 56.0),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: customGradient == null ? backgroundColor : null,
          gradient: customGradient,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(context.sp(12)),
          border: Border.all(
            color: borderColor,
            width:
                (isPrimary || customBgColor != null || customGradient != null)
                ? 0
                : 1,
          ),
          boxShadow:
              (isPrimary && customGradient == null && customBgColor == null)
              ? [
                  BoxShadow(
                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                    color: const Color(0xFF007AFF).withOpacity(0.3),
                    blurRadius: 8,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                  ),
                ]
              : null,
        ),
        child: Center(
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Icon(icon, color: foregroundColor, size: context.sp(18)),
              SizedBox(width: context.sp(8)),
              Text(
                label,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: foregroundColor,
                  fontSize: context.sp(16).clamp(14.0, 18.0),
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = widget.isDark;
    final hPad = context.sp(24).clamp(20.0, 36.0);

    final bgColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF000000) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final separatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final primaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
        : const Color(0xFF000000);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    // Dynamic Connection Button Props
    String connectLabel = 'Connect';
    IconData connectIcon = CupertinoIcons.person_badge_plus;
    bool isPrimaryConnect = true;
    Color? customBg;
    Color? customText;
    Gradient? customGradient;

    if (_connStatus == 'pending_sent') {
      connectLabel = 'Requested';
      connectIcon = CupertinoIcons.clock_fill;
      isPrimaryConnect = false;
      customBg = Colors.grey[600]; // Requested grey color
      customText = Colors.white;
    } else if (_connStatus == 'pending_received') {
      connectLabel = 'Accept';
      connectIcon = CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill;
      isPrimaryConnect = true;
      customBg = Colors.greenAccent[700]; // Accept green color
      customText = Colors.white;
    } else if (_connStatus == 'connected') {
      connectLabel = 'Connected';
      connectIcon = CupertinoIcons.person_2_fill;
      isPrimaryConnect = false;
      customText = Colors.white;
      customGradient = const LinearGradient(
        colors: [Colors.orangeAccent, Colors.deepOrangeAccent],
      ); // Connected orange accent gradient
    }

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value: (isDark ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
          .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        body: Column(
          children: [
            // ── Nav bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Container(
              height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 44,
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: bgColor,
                border: Border(
                  bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(4)),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    // Back
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: context.sp(12),
                          vertical: context.sp(4),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.back,
                              color: iosBlue,
                              size: context.sp(20),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: context.sp(4)),
                            Text(
                              'Back',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: iosBlue,
                                fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    Text(
                      // ── FIX: nav title uses live first name (real-time) ──
                      "$_firstName's Profile",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: primaryText,
                        fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 18.0),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        letterSpacing: -0.2,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    // Balanced spacer (same width as back button area)
                    SizedBox(
                      width:
                          context.sp(12) * 2 +
                          context.sp(20) +
                          context.sp(4) +
                          32,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // ── Body ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Expanded(
              child: SafeArea(
                top: false,
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: hPad),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        SizedBox(height: context.hp(12)),

                        // ── Large avatar ───────────────────────────────────
                        // ── FIX: uses _effectivePhotoUrl (fetched if prop was empty)
                        // ── NEW: Wrapped with GestureDetector when photo is set → opens ViewPicture
                        FadeTransition(
                          opacity: _avatarFade,
                          child: ScaleTransition(
                            scale: _avatarScale,
                            child: GestureDetector(
                              onTap: _effectivePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty
                                  ? _viewProfilePicture
                                  : null,
                              child: SizedBox(
                                width: 82,
                                height: 82,
                                child: _GradientAvatar(
                                  initials: _initials,
                                  gradientColors: _gradient,
                                  size: 82,
                                  fontSize: 28,
                                  photoUrl: _effectivePhotoUrl.isNotEmpty
                                      ? _effectivePhotoUrl
                                      : null, // ← FIX
                                  isLoading: false,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),

                        SizedBox(height: context.hp(8)),

                        // ── Name + Blue Tick (NEW) ──────────────────────────────────────────
                        SlideTransition(
                          position: _infoSlide,
                          child: FadeTransition(
                            opacity: _infoFade,
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                if (_displayName.isNotEmpty)
                                  Row(
                                    crossAxisAlignment:
                                        CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        _displayName,
                                        textAlign: TextAlign.left,
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: primaryText,
                                          fontSize: context
                                              .sp(22)
                                              .clamp(18.0, 26.0),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                          letterSpacing: -0.5,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      if (_hasBlueTick) ...[
                                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                        Icon(
                                          CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                                          color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                          size: context
                                              .sp(22)
                                              .clamp(18.0, 24.0),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ],
                                  ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),

                        SizedBox(height: context.hp(32)),

                        // ── Action Buttons (Connect + Message) ────────────
                        SlideTransition(
                          position: _infoSlide,
                          child: FadeTransition(
                            opacity: _infoFade,
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                // Connect Button (or Loading spinner)
                                Expanded(
                                  child: _isLoadingConn
                                      ? Container(
                                          height: context
                                              .sp(48)
                                              .clamp(44.0, 56.0),
                                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                            color: isDark
                                                ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
                                                : Colors.white,
                                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                                              context.sp(12),
                                            ),
                                            border: Border.all(
                                              color: isDark
                                                  ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
                                                  : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8),
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                          child: const Center(
                                            child: CupertinoActivityIndicator(),
                                          ),
                                        )
                                      : _buildActionButton(
                                          label: connectLabel,
                                          icon: connectIcon,
                                          onPressed: _onConnectAction,
                                          isPrimary: isPrimaryConnect,
                                          isDark: isDark,
                                          customBgColor: customBg,
                                          customTextColor: customText,
                                          customGradient: customGradient,
                                        ),
                                ),
                                SizedBox(width: context.sp(12)),
                                // Message Button
                                Expanded(
                                  child: _buildActionButton(
                                    label: 'Message',
                                    icon: CupertinoIcons.chat_bubble_2_fill,
                                    onPressed: _onMessagePressed,
                                    isPrimary: false,
                                    isDark: isDark,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),

                        SizedBox(height: context.hp(52)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  REQUESTS SCREEN — Pending Connection Requests list
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _RequestsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String currentUserId;
  final bool isDark;

  const _RequestsScreen({required this.currentUserId, required this.isDark});

  @override
  State<_RequestsScreen> createState() => _RequestsScreenState();
}

class _RequestsScreenState extends State<_RequestsScreen> {
  bool _isSearching = false;
  String _searchQuery = '';
  final TextEditingController _searchCtrl = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bgColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF000000)
        : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final navBg = widget.isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
    final separatorColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final primaryText = widget.isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final secondaryText = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    final statusH = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top;
    const navH = 48.0;

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value:
          (widget.isDark
                  ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light
                  : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
              .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
          stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
              .collection('connections')
              .where('participants', arrayContains: widget.currentUserId)
              .snapshots(),
          builder: (context, snapshot) {
            // Filter and Sort requests memory list
            List<QueryDocumentSnapshot> docs = [];
            if (snapshot.hasData) {
              docs = snapshot.data!.docs.where((doc) {
                final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                return data['status'] == 'pending' &&
                    data['receiverId'] == widget.currentUserId;
              }).toList();

              // Sort latest timestamp at top
              docs.sort((a, b) {
                final dataA = a.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                final dataB = b.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                final tA = dataA['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;
                final tB = dataB['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;
                if (tA == null && tB == null) return 0;
                if (tA == null) return 1;
                if (tB == null) return -1;
                return tB.compareTo(tA);
              });
            }

            return Column(
              children: [
                // ── Nav Bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
                Container(
                  height: statusH + navH,
                  padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: statusH),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: navBg,
                    border: Border(
                      bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      if (!_isSearching)
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                          behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                          child: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 16,
                              vertical: 8,
                            ),
                            child: Row(
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                const Icon(
                                  CupertinoIcons.back,
                                  color: iosBlue,
                                  size: 22,
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                                Text(
                                  'Back',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: iosBlue,
                                    fontSize: 17,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      if (_isSearching) const SizedBox(width: 16),

                      Expanded(
                        child: _isSearching
                            ? CupertinoTextField(
                                controller: _searchCtrl,
                                autofocus: true,
                                placeholder: 'Search requests...',
                                style: TextStyle(color: primaryText),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: widget.isDark
                                      ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                                      : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA),
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                ),
                                onChanged: (val) {
                                  setState(() {
                                    _searchQuery = val;
                                  });
                                },
                              )
                            : Center(
                                child: Text(
                                  docs.isEmpty
                                      ? 'Requests'
                                      : 'Requests : ${docs.length}',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: primaryText,
                                    fontSize: 17,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                    letterSpacing: -0.2,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                      ),

                      if (!_isSearching)
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {
                            setState(() {
                              _isSearching = true;
                            });
                          },
                          behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                          child: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 16,
                              vertical: 8,
                            ),
                            child: const Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.search,
                              color: iosBlue,
                              size: 22,
                            ),
                          ),
                        )
                      else
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {
                            setState(() {
                              _isSearching = false;
                              _searchQuery = '';
                              _searchCtrl.clear();
                            });
                          },
                          behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                          child: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 16,
                              vertical: 8,
                            ),
                            child: Text(
                              'Cancel',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: iosBlue,
                                fontSize: 17,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                // ── List of Requests ───────────────────────────────────────────
                Expanded(
                  child: !snapshot.hasData
                      ? const Center(child: CupertinoActivityIndicator())
                      : (docs.isEmpty
                            ? Center(
                                child: Column(
                                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                  children: [
                                    Icon(
                                      CupertinoIcons
                                          .person_crop_circle_badge_xmark,
                                      size: 64,
                                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                      color: secondaryText.withOpacity(0.5),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                                    Text(
                                      'No Pending Requests',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        color: primaryText,
                                        fontSize: 18,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                    Text(
                                      'You have no new connection requests.',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        color: secondaryText,
                                        fontSize: 14,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              )
                            : ListView.builder(
                                physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  vertical: 8,
                                ),
                                itemCount: docs.length,
                                itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                                  final doc = docs[i];
                                  final data =
                                      doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                                  final requesterId =
                                      data['requesterId'] as String;
                                  final timestamp =
                                      data['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;

                                  return _RequestTile(
                                    key: ValueKey(
                                      doc.id,
                                    ), // ✅ BUG FIX: Ensures the exact item is modified/removed
                                    connId: doc.id,
                                    requesterId: requesterId,
                                    timestamp: timestamp,
                                    isDark: widget.isDark,
                                    searchQuery: _searchQuery,
                                  );
                                },
                              )),
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  INDIVIDUAL REQUEST TILE (now with real-time user data & blue tick)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
class _RequestTile extends StatefulWidget {
  final String connId;
  final String requesterId;
  final Timestamp? timestamp;
  final bool isDark;
  final String searchQuery;

  const _RequestTile({
    super.key,
    required this.connId,
    required this.requesterId,
    this.timestamp,
    required this.isDark,
    this.searchQuery = '',
  });

  @override
  State<_RequestTile> createState() => _RequestTileState();
}

class _RequestTileState extends State<_RequestTile> {
  // We'll use a stream to keep user data live
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot>? _userSub;
  String _firstName = '';
  String _lastName = '';
  String _photoUrl = '';
  bool _hasBlueTick = false;
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _subscribeToUser();
  }

  void _subscribeToUser() {
    _userSub = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('users')
        .doc(widget.requesterId)
        .snapshots()
        .listen((doc) {
          if (!mounted) return;
          if (doc.exists) {
            final data = doc.data()!;
            final exp = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
            setState(() {
              _firstName = (data['firstName'] as String?) ?? '';
              _lastName = (data['lastName'] as String?) ?? '';
              _photoUrl = (data['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
              _hasBlueTick =
                  exp != null && exp.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
              _isLoading = false;
            });
          } else {
            setState(() => _isLoading = false);
          }
        }, onError: (_) => setState(() => _isLoading = false));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _userSub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _accept() async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('connections')
          .doc(widget.connId)
          .update({
            'status': 'accepted',
            'timestamp': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
          });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  void _delete() async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('connections')
          .doc(widget.connId)
          .delete();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  String get _initials {
    final f = _firstName.isNotEmpty ? _firstName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final l = _lastName.isNotEmpty ? _lastName[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final result = '$f$l';
    return result.isNotEmpty ? result : '?';
  }

  // Generate readable date/time string
  String get _timeString {
    if (widget.timestamp == null) return '';
    final date = widget.timestamp!.toDate();
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final diff = now.difference(date);

    final days = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
    final months = [
      'Jan',
      'Feb',
      'Mar',
      'Apr',
      'May',
      'Jun',
      'Jul',
      'Aug',
      'Sep',
      'Oct',
      'Nov',
      'Dec',
    ];

    final dayName = days[date.weekday - 1];
    final monthName = months[date.month - 1];
    final hour = date.hour == 0
        ? 12
        : (date.hour > 12 ? date.hour - 12 : date.hour);
    final ampm = date.hour >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
    final minute = date.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0');

    if (diff.inDays == 0 && date.day == now.day) {
      return 'Today, $hour:$minute $ampm';
    } else if (diff.inDays == 1 || (diff.inDays == 0 && date.day != now.day)) {
      return 'Yesterday, $hour:$minute $ampm';
    }
    return '$dayName, $monthName ${date.day}, $hour:$minute $ampm';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return const SizedBox(
        height: 72,
        child: Center(child: CupertinoActivityIndicator()),
      );
    }

    final fullName = '${_firstName.trim()} ${_lastName.trim()}'.trim();
    final displayName = fullName.isNotEmpty ? fullName : 'Unknown User';

    // ✅ NEW: Filters tiles depending on the live search input
    if (widget.searchQuery.isNotEmpty) {
      if (!displayName.toLowerCase().contains(
        widget.searchQuery.toLowerCase(),
      )) {
        return const SizedBox.shrink();
      }
    }

    final primaryText = widget.isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final separatorColor = widget.isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final cardColor = widget.isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          // Avatar (Clickable to open profile)
          GestureDetector(
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (_) => BiographPage(
                    uid: widget.requesterId,
                    firstName: _firstName,
                    lastName: _lastName,
                    profilePhotoUrl: _photoUrl,
                    isDark: widget.isDark,
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
            child: _GradientAvatar(
              initials: _initials,
              gradientColors: _gradientForName(displayName),
              size: 52,
              fontSize: 18,
              photoUrl: _photoUrl.isNotEmpty ? _photoUrl : null,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),

          // Details & Buttons
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          Flexible(
                            child: Text(
                              displayName,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: primaryText,
                                fontSize: 16,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                letterSpacing: -0.3,
                              ),
                              maxLines: 1,
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            ),
                          ),
                          if (_hasBlueTick) ...[
                            const SizedBox(width: 4),
                            Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                              color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                              size: 16,
                            ),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (_timeString.isNotEmpty)
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0),
                        child: Text(
                          _timeString,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: widget.isDark
                                ? Colors.grey[500]
                                : Colors.grey[600],
                            fontSize: 11,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    // Confirm Button
                    Expanded(
                      child: GestureDetector(
                        onTap: _accept,
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          ),
                          child: const Center(
                            child: Text(
                              'Confirm',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.white,
                                fontSize: 13,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    // Delete Button
                    Expanded(
                      child: GestureDetector(
                        onTap: _delete,
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: widget.isDark
                                ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
                                : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          ),
                          child: Center(
                            child: Text(
                              'Delete',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: primaryText,
                                fontSize: 13,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  USER SEARCH SCREEN — "New Message" sheet (with real‑time users & enhanced search)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _UserSearchScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String currentUserId;
  final bool isDark;

  const _UserSearchScreen({required this.currentUserId, required this.isDark});

  @override
  State<_UserSearchScreen> createState() => _UserSearchScreenState();
}

class _UserSearchScreenState extends State<_UserSearchScreen> {
  final TextEditingController _searchCtrl = TextEditingController();

  // Real‑time stream of all users (excluding current user)
  late final Stream<QuerySnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>> _usersStream;

  // NEW: recent searches stored in backend (per-user array in their own user document).
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _recentUsers = [];
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _filteredResults = [];
  bool _showingRecents = true;
  bool _isLoadingRecents = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _usersStream = FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('users').snapshots();
    _loadRecentUsers();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // ── Load recent searched users from current user's document in backend ──
  Future<void> _loadRecentUsers() async {
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      if (!mounted) return;

      final data = doc.data() ?? {};
      final recentIds = (data['recentSearched'] as List<dynamic>? ?? [])
          .cast<String>()
          .toList();

      if (recentIds.isEmpty) {
        setState(() {
          _recentUsers = [];
          _filteredResults = [];
          _showingRecents = true;
          _isLoadingRecents = false;
        });
        return;
      }

      final futures = recentIds
          .map(
            (id) =>
                FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('users').doc(id).get(),
          )
          .toList();
      final snaps = await Future.wait(futures);

      final usersList = snaps
          .where((s) => s.exists && s.id != widget.currentUserId)
          .map((s) => {'uid': s.id, ...s.data()!})
          .toList();

      final orderedRecent = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
      for (final id in recentIds) {
        final match = usersList.where((u) => u['uid'] == id).toList();
        if (match.isNotEmpty) orderedRecent.add(match.first);
      }

      setState(() {
        _recentUsers = orderedRecent;
        _filteredResults = List.from(_recentUsers);
        _showingRecents = true;
        _isLoadingRecents = false;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() {
        _recentUsers = [];
        _isLoadingRecents = false;
      });
    }
  }

  // ── Enhanced search: token‑based prefix matching ─────────────────────────
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _performSearch(
    String query,
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> users,
  ) {
    if (query.isEmpty) return users;
    final tokens = query.trim().toLowerCase().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
    return users.where((user) {
      final firstName = (user['firstName'] as String? ?? '')
          .trim()
          .toLowerCase();
      final lastName = (user['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
      final fullName = '$firstName $lastName'.trim();
      // For each token, at least one name part must start with it
      return tokens.every((token) {
        return firstName.startsWith(token) ||
            lastName.startsWith(token) ||
            fullName.startsWith(token) ||
            fullName.contains(token); // fallback for multi‑word names
      });
    }).toList();
  }

  // ── Called when search text changes ──────────────────────────────────────
  void _onSearchChanged(String query) {
    final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q.isEmpty) {
      setState(() {
        _filteredResults = List.from(_recentUsers);
        _showingRecents = true;
      });
      return;
    }
    // We need to filter from the *full* user list, so we'll fetch it via stream later.
    // To avoid building a second stream, we'll rely on the StreamBuilder below.
    setState(() {
      _showingRecents = false;
      // The actual filtering will happen inside the StreamBuilder.
    });
  }

  // ── Add selected user to recent searches in backend ──────────────────────
  Future<void> _addToRecent(String otherUid) async {
    if (otherUid == widget.currentUserId) return;
    final userRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('users')
        .doc(widget.currentUserId);
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.runTransaction((transaction) async {
        final snapshot = await transaction.get(userRef);
        if (!snapshot.exists) return;
        List<String> recent = List<String>.from(
          snapshot.data()!['recentSearched'] ?? [],
        );
        recent.remove(otherUid);
        recent.insert(0, otherUid);
        if (recent.length > 30) recent = recent.sublist(0, 30);
        transaction.update(userRef, {'recentSearched': recent});
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ── Remove user from recent searches ─────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _removeFromRecent(String uid) async {
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _recentUsers.removeWhere((u) => u['uid'] == uid);
      if (_showingRecents) {
        _filteredResults = List.from(_recentUsers);
      }
    });
    final userRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('users')
        .doc(widget.currentUserId);
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.runTransaction((transaction) async {
        final snap = await transaction.get(userRef);
        if (!snap.exists) return;
        List<String> recent = List<String>.from(
          snap.data()!['recentSearched'] ?? [],
        );
        recent.remove(uid);
        transaction.update(userRef, {'recentSearched': recent});
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ── Clear all recent searches ────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _clearAllRecents() async {
    if (!mounted || _recentUsers.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() {
      _recentUsers = [];
      if (_showingRecents) {
        _filteredResults = [];
      }
    });
    final userRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('users')
        .doc(widget.currentUserId);
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.runTransaction((transaction) async {
        final snap = await transaction.get(userRef);
        if (!snap.exists) return;
        transaction.update(userRef, {'recentSearched': <String>[]});
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  String _initialsForUser(Map<String, dynamic> user) {
    final first = (user['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
    final last = (user['lastName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
    final f = first.isNotEmpty ? first[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final l = last.isNotEmpty ? last[0].toUpperCase() : '';
    final result = '$f$l';
    return result.isNotEmpty ? result : '?';
  }

  void _openBiograph(Map<String, dynamic> user) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => BiographPage(
          uid: user['uid'] as String,
          firstName: (user['firstName'] as String?) ?? '',
          lastName: (user['lastName'] as String?) ?? '',
          profilePhotoUrl: (user['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '',
          isDark: widget.isDark,
        ),
        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
          position: Tween<Offset>(
            begin: const Offset(1, 0),
            end: Offset.zero,
          ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic)),
          child: child,
        ),
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _openChatWith(Map<String, dynamic> user) async {
    final otherUid = user['uid'] as String;
    final otherName = '${user['firstName'] ?? ''} ${user['lastName'] ?? ''}'
        .trim();
    final otherPhoto = (user['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
    _addToRecent(otherUid);
    final ids = [widget.currentUserId, otherUid]..sort();
    final conversationId = ids.join('_');
    try {
      final currentDoc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      final cd = currentDoc.data() ?? {};
      final currentName = '${cd['firstName'] ?? ''} ${cd['lastName'] ?? ''}'
          .trim();
      final currentPhoto = (cd['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(
        PageRouteBuilder(
          pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => ChatScreen(
            conversationId: conversationId,
            otherUserId: otherUid,
            otherName: otherName,
            otherPhotoUrl: otherPhoto,
            currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
            currentUserName: currentName,
            currentUserPhotoUrl: currentPhoto,
          ),
          transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
            position: Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(1, 0), end: Offset.zero)
                .animate(
                  CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
                ),
            child: child,
          ),
          transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
        ),
      );
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(
        PageRouteBuilder(
          pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => ChatScreen(
            conversationId: conversationId,
            otherUserId: otherUid,
            otherName: otherName,
            otherPhotoUrl: otherPhoto,
            currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
          ),
          transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, _, child) => SlideTransition(
            position: Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(1, 0), end: Offset.zero)
                .animate(
                  CurvedAnimation(parent: anim, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic),
                ),
            child: child,
          ),
          transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = widget.isDark;
    final bgColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF000000) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final navBg = isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
    final separatorColor = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8);
    final primaryText = isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final secondaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    const iosBlue = Color(0xFF007AFF);

    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
