      value: (isDark ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
          .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        body: Column(
          children: [
            // ── Nav bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Container(
              height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 52,
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: navBg,
                border: Border(
                  bottom: BorderSide(color: separatorColor, width: 0.5),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: const Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 8,
                          vertical: 4,
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          'Cancel',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: iosBlue,
                            fontSize: 17,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'Search Users',
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: primaryText,
                          fontSize: 17,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          letterSpacing: -0.2,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 74),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            // ── Search field ───────────────────────────────────────────────
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
              child: Container(
                height: 36,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: isDark
                      ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                      : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: TextField(
                  controller: _searchCtrl,
                  onChanged: _onSearchChanged,
                  autofocus: false,
                  style: TextStyle(color: primaryText, fontSize: 15),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    border: InputBorder.none,
                    hintText: 'Search by name',
                    hintStyle: TextStyle(color: secondaryText, fontSize: 15),
                    prefixIcon: Icon(
                      CupertinoIcons.search,
                      color: secondaryText,
                      size: 16,
                    ),
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                    isDense: true,
                    suffixIcon: _searchCtrl.text.isNotEmpty
                        ? GestureDetector(
                            onTap: () {
                              _searchCtrl.clear();
                              _onSearchChanged('');
                            },
                            child: Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.clear_thick_circled,
                              color: secondaryText,
                              size: 16,
                            ),
                          )
                        : null,
                  ),
                  cursorColor: iosBlue,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            // ── Section label ──────────────────────────────────────────────
            if (!_isLoadingRecents)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 2, 16, 8),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      _showingRecents ? 'Recent' : 'Results',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: secondaryText,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        letterSpacing: 0.4,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    if (_showingRecents && _filteredResults.isNotEmpty)
                      GestureDetector(
                        onTap: _clearAllRecents,
                        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                        child: const Text(
                          'Clear All',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: iosBlue,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                      )
                    else
                      Text(
                        '${_filteredResults.length} ${_filteredResults.length == 1 ? 'person' : 'people'}',
                        style: TextStyle(color: secondaryText, fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            // ── Results ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            Expanded(
              child: _isLoadingRecents
                  ? const Center(child: CupertinoActivityIndicator())
                  : StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>>(
                      stream: _usersStream,
                      builder: (context, snapshot) {
                        if (snapshot.connectionState ==
                            ConnectionState.waiting) {
                          return const Center(
                            child: CupertinoActivityIndicator(),
                          );
                        }
                        if (snapshot.hasError) {
                          return Center(child: Text('Error loading users'));
                        }
                        final allUsers = snapshot.data!.docs
                            .where((doc) => doc.id != widget.currentUserId)
                            .map((doc) => {'uid': doc.id, ...doc.data()})
                            .toList();
                        // Sort alphabetically by full name
                        allUsers.sort((a, b) {
                          final aName =
                              '${a['firstName'] ?? ''} ${a['lastName'] ?? ''}'
                                  .trim()
                                  .toLowerCase();
                          final bName =
                              '${b['firstName'] ?? ''} ${b['lastName'] ?? ''}'
                                  .trim()
                                  .toLowerCase();
                          return aName.compareTo(bName);
                        });
                        final query = _searchCtrl.text.trim().toLowerCase();
                        List<Map<String, dynamic>> displayList;
                        if (query.isEmpty) {
                          displayList = _recentUsers;
                          _showingRecents = true;
                        } else {
                          displayList = _performSearch(query, allUsers);
                          _showingRecents = false;
                        }
                        // Update _filteredResults for the count display
                        WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
                          if (mounted) {
                            setState(() {
                              _filteredResults = displayList;
                            });
                          }
                        });
                        if (displayList.isEmpty) {
                          return Center(
                            child: Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: 40,
                              ),
                              child: Column(
                                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                children: [
                                  Icon(
                                    CupertinoIcons.person_2_fill,
                                    size: 52,
                                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                    color: secondaryText.withOpacity(0.4),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                                  Text(
                                    query.isEmpty
                                        ? 'No recent searches yet'
                                        : 'No results for "$query"',
                                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      color: secondaryText,
                                      fontSize: 15,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }
                        return ListView.separated(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                          physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                          itemCount: displayList.length,
                          separatorBuilder: (_, _) => Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 80),
                            child: Divider(
                              height: 1,
                              thickness: 0.5,
                              color: separatorColor,
                            ),
                          ),
                          itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                            final user = displayList[i];
                            final firstName =
                                (user['firstName'] as String? ?? '').trim();
                            final lastName = (user['lastName'] as String? ?? '')
                                .trim();
                            final fullName = '$firstName $lastName'.trim();
                            final photo =
                                (user['profilePhotoUrl'] as String?) ?? '';
                            final initials = _initialsForUser(user);
                            final exp = user['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
                            final hasBlueTick =
                                exp != null &&
                                exp.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
                            final uid = user['uid'] as String;
                            Widget tile = InkWell(
                              onTap: () => _openChatWith(user),
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              splashColor: iosBlue.withOpacity(0.08),
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              highlightColor: iosBlue.withOpacity(0.04),
                              child: Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 16,
                                  vertical: 10,
                                ),
                                child: Row(
                                  children: [
                                    GestureDetector(
                                      onTap: () => _openBiograph(user),
                                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                                      child: _GradientAvatar(
                                        initials: initials,
                                        gradientColors: _gradientForName(
                                          fullName,
                                        ),
                                        size: 50,
                                        fontSize: 17,
                                        photoUrl: photo.isNotEmpty
                                            ? photo
                                            : null,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                                    Expanded(
                                      child: Column(
                                        crossAxisAlignment:
                                            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                        children: [
                                          Row(
                                            children: [
                                              Flexible(
                                                child: Text(
                                                  fullName,
                                                  style: TextStyle(
                                                    color: primaryText,
                                                    fontSize: 16,
                                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                                    letterSpacing: -0.2,
                                                  ),
                                                  overflow:
                                                      TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                                  maxLines: 1,
                                                ),
                                              ),
                                              if (hasBlueTick) ...[
                                                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                                                Icon(
                                                  CupertinoIcons
                                                      .checkmark_seal_fill,
                                                  color: const Color(
                                                    0xFF007AFF,
                                                  ),
                                                  size: 16,
                                                ),
                                              ],
                                            ],
                                          ),
                                          const SizedBox(height: 2),
                                          Text(
                                            'Tap to message',
                                            style: TextStyle(
                                              color: secondaryText,
                                              fontSize: 13,
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                        ],
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    if (_showingRecents)
                                      GestureDetector(
                                        onTap: () => _removeFromRecent(uid),
                                        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                                        child: Padding(
                                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4.0),
                                          child: Icon(
                                            CupertinoIcons.xmark_circle_fill,
                                            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                            color: secondaryText.withOpacity(
                                              0.7,
                                            ),
                                            size: 18,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    if (_showingRecents)
                                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                    Icon(
                                      CupertinoIcons.chevron_right,
                                      size: 14,
                                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                      color: secondaryText.withOpacity(0.6),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            );
                            if (_showingRecents) {
                              return Dismissible(
                                key: ValueKey(uid),
                                direction: DismissDirection.endToStart,
                                onDismissed: (_) => _removeFromRecent(uid),
                                background: Container(
                                  color: Colors.red,
                                  alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 20),
                                  child: const Icon(
                                    CupertinoIcons.delete,
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                child: tile,
                              );
                            }
                            return tile;
                          },
                        );
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  BLUE TICK VERIFICATION SCREEN (Updated with real-time token balance, purchase packages,
//  confirmation dialog, token deduction, expiry logic, and status display)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class BlueTick extends StatefulWidget {
  const BlueTick({super.key});

  @override
  State<BlueTick> createState() => _BlueTickState();
}

class _BlueTickState extends State<BlueTick> {
  String _currentUserId = '';
  int _tokens = 0;
  Timestamp? _blueTickExpiry;
  Timestamp? _blueTickPurchasedAt;
  late Stream<DocumentSnapshot> _userStream;
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    if (user != null) {
      _currentUserId = user.uid;
      _userStream = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(_currentUserId)
          .snapshots();
    }
  }

  bool get _hasActiveBlueTick {
    if (_blueTickExpiry == null) return false;
    return _blueTickExpiry!.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime d) {
    final months = [
      'Jan',
      'Feb',
      'Mar',
      'Apr',
      'May',
      'Jun',
      'Jul',
      'Aug',
      'Sep',
      'Oct',
      'Nov',
      'Dec',
    ];
    return '${d.day} ${months[d.month - 1]} ${d.year}';
  }

  int _getDaysLeft() {
    if (_blueTickExpiry == null) return 0;
    final diff = _blueTickExpiry!.toDate().difference(DateTime.now());
    return diff.inDays > 0 ? diff.inDays : 0;
  }

  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> _packages = [
    {'label': '1 Month', 'months': 1, 'cost': 10},
    {'label': '3 Months', 'months': 3, 'cost': 25},
    {'label': '6 Months', 'months': 6, 'cost': 50},
    {'label': '9 Months', 'months': 9, 'cost': 75},
    {'label': '12 Months', 'months': 12, 'cost': 100},
  ];

  Future<void> _purchasePackage(int months, int cost) async {
    if (_tokens < cost) {
      _showInfoDialog(
        'Not Enough Tokens',
        'Not enough token balance, please watch ad and try again',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (_hasActiveBlueTick) {
      _showInfoDialog(
        'Active Package',
        'You cannot purchase a new package while your current Blue Tick is active.',
      );
      return;
    }
    final confirm = await _showConfirmPurchase(months, cost);
    if (confirm != true || !mounted) return;
    try {
      final now = Timestamp.now();
      final expiryDate = now.toDate().add(Duration(days: months * 30));
      final expiry = Timestamp.fromDate(expiryDate);
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(_currentUserId)
          .update({
            'tokens': _tokens - cost,
            'blueTickExpiry': expiry,
            'blueTickPurchasedAt': now,
          });
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text('Blue Tick package purchased successfully!'),
            backgroundColor: Color(0xFF007AFF),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        _showInfoDialog(
          'Error',
          'Failed to purchase package. Please try again.',
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<bool?> _showConfirmPurchase(int months, int cost) {
    return showCupertinoDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => CupertinoAlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Confirm Purchase'),
        content: Text(
          'Buy $months month${months > 1 ? 's' : ''} Blue Tick for $cost tokens?',
        ),
        actions: [
          CupertinoDialogAction(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          CupertinoDialogAction(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(true),
            child: const Text('Purchase'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showInfoDialog(String title, String message) {
    showCupertinoDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => CupertinoAlertDialog(
        title: Text(title),
        content: Text(message),
        actions: [
          CupertinoDialogAction(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
            child: const Text('OK'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDark;
    final bgColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF000000) : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final primaryText = isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final cardColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;
    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value: (isDark ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
          .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        body: Column(
          children: [
            Container(
              height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 44,
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: bgColor,
                border: Border(
                  bottom: BorderSide(
                    color: isDark
                        ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
                        : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8),
                    width: 0.5,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: context.sp(4)),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: context.sp(12),
                          vertical: context.sp(4),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.back,
                              color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                              size: context.sp(20),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: context.sp(4)),
                            Text(
                              'Back',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(15.0, 19.0),
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    SizedBox(width: context.sp(8)),
                    Text(
                      'Activate Verification',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: primaryText,
                        fontSize: context.sp(17).clamp(13.0, 17.0),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                        letterSpacing: -0.2,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: StreamBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
                stream: _userStream,
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  if (snapshot.hasData && snapshot.data!.exists) {
                    final data =
                        snapshot.data!.data() as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
                    _tokens = data['tokens'] as int? ?? 0;
                    _blueTickExpiry = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
                    _blueTickPurchasedAt =
                        data['blueTickPurchasedAt'] as Timestamp?;
                    _isLoading = false;
                  }
                  return SafeArea(
                    top: false,
                    child: SingleChildScrollView(
                      physics: const ClampingScrollPhysics(),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: context.sp(16),
                        vertical: context.sp(16),
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            width: double.infinity,
                            padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(16)),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: cardColor,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                              boxShadow: [
                                BoxShadow(
                                  color: isDark
                                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                      ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.3)
                                      : const Color(
                                          0xFF007AFF,
                                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                        ).withOpacity(0.08),
                                  blurRadius: 16,
                                  offset: const Offset(0, 8),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                Container(
                                  padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(10)),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    gradient: const LinearGradient(
                                      colors: [
                                        Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                        Color(0xFF5AC8FA),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                  ),
                                  child: Icon(
                                    CupertinoIcons.sparkles,
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    size: context.sp(24),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                SizedBox(width: context.sp(12)),
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Column(
                                    crossAxisAlignment:
                                        CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        'Token Balance',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: isDark
                                              ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                                              : const Color(0xFF6D6D72),
                                          fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      Text(
                                        _tokens.toString(),
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: primaryText,
                                          fontSize: context.sp(28),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ),
                                if (_isLoading)
                                  const CupertinoActivityIndicator(),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 24),
                          if (_hasActiveBlueTick) ...[
                            Container(
                              width: double.infinity,
                              padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(16)),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: cardColor,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                                border: Border.all(
                                  color: const Color(
                                    0xFF007AFF,
                                    // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                  ).withOpacity(0.3),
                                ),
                              ),
                              child: Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                children: [
                                  Row(
                                    children: [
                                      Icon(
                                        CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                                        color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                        size: context.sp(22),
                                      ),
                                      SizedBox(width: context.sp(8)),
                                      Text(
                                        'Blue Tick Active',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                          fontSize: context.sp(16),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                  SizedBox(height: context.sp(12)),
                                  if (_blueTickPurchasedAt != null)
                                    Row(
                                      mainAxisAlignment:
                                          MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                                      children: [
                                        Text(
                                          'Purchased on',
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            color: isDark
                                                ? Colors.white70
                                                : Colors.black54,
                                            fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                        Text(
                                          _formatDate(
                                            _blueTickPurchasedAt!.toDate(),
                                          ),
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            color: primaryText,
                                            fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                  Row(
                                    mainAxisAlignment:
                                        MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        'Expires on',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: isDark
                                              ? Colors.white70
                                              : Colors.black54,
                                          fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      Text(
                                        _formatDate(_blueTickExpiry!.toDate()),
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: primaryText,
                                          fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                  Row(
                                    mainAxisAlignment:
                                        MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        'Days left',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: isDark
                                              ? Colors.white70
                                              : Colors.black54,
                                          fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      Text(
                                        '${_getDaysLeft()} days',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                          fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 24),
                          ],
                          Text(
                            'Purchase Packages',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: primaryText,
                              fontSize: context.sp(17),
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          if (_hasActiveBlueTick)
                            Container(
                              padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(12)),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30).withOpacity(0.1),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                children: [
                                  Icon(
                                    CupertinoIcons.lock_fill,
                                    color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                                    size: 18,
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Text(
                                      'Packages are locked until current Blue Tick package expires.',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                                        fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          ..._packages.map((pkg) {
                            final bool isLocked = _hasActiveBlueTick;
                            final bool canAfford =
                                _tokens >= (pkg['cost'] as int);
                            final bool enabled = !isLocked && canAfford;
                            return GestureDetector(
                              onTap: enabled
                                  ? () => _purchasePackage(
                                      pkg['months'] as int,
                                      pkg['cost'] as int,
                                    )
                                  : null,
                              child: Container(
                                margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: context.sp(12)),
                                padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(16)),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: cardColor,
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                                  border: Border.all(
                                    color: enabled
                                        ? const Color(
                                            0xFF007AFF,
                                            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                          ).withOpacity(0.2)
                                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                        : Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.2),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                child: Row(
                                  children: [
                                    Column(
                                      crossAxisAlignment:
                                          CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        Text(
                                          pkg['label'] as String,
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            color: primaryText,
                                            fontSize: context.sp(16),
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                        Text(
                                          '${pkg['cost']} tokens',
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            color: isDark
                                                ? Colors.white70
                                                : Colors.black54,
                                            fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                    const Spacer(),
                                    CupertinoButton(
                                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                                      onPressed: enabled
                                          ? () => _purchasePackage(
                                              pkg['months'] as int,
                                              pkg['cost'] as int,
                                            )
                                          : null,
                                      child: Container(
                                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                          horizontal: context.sp(20),
                                          vertical: context.sp(8),
                                        ),
                                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                          color: enabled
                                              ? const Color(0xFF007AFF)
                                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                              : Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.3),
                                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                                            20,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                        child: Text(
                                          isLocked
                                              ? 'Locked'
                                              : (canAfford
                                                    ? 'Purchase'
                                                    : 'Low Balance'),
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            color: enabled
                                                ? Colors.white
                                                : Colors.grey,
                                            fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            );
                          }),
                          const SizedBox(height: 40),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SETTINGS TAB – Enhanced with AdMob Rewarded Ads & Token Storage
//  (real‑time token sync + daily reset fix + section grouping)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _SettingsTab extends StatefulWidget {
  final Color bgColor;
  final Color cardColor;
  final Color secondaryText;
  final bool isDark;
  final String currentUserId;

  const _SettingsTab({
    required this.bgColor,
    required this.cardColor,
    required this.secondaryText,
    required this.isDark,
    required this.currentUserId,
  });

  @override
  State<_SettingsTab> createState() => _SettingsTabState();
}

class _SettingsTabState extends State<_SettingsTab>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  // AdMob test ad unit ID (rewarded)
  static const String _adUnitId = 'ca-app-pub-3940256099942544/5224354917';

  RewardedAd? _rewardedAd;
  bool _isAdLoading = false;
  bool _isLoggingOut = false;
  int _earnedTokens = 0;

  // Cooldown tracking (now dynamic based on daily count)
  DateTime? _cooldownEnd;
  Timer? _cooldownTimer;
  bool get _isInCooldown =>
      _cooldownEnd != null && _cooldownEnd!.isAfter(DateTime.now());

  // Daily ad tracking
  int _dailyAdCount = 0;
  String _lastAdCountDate =
      ''; // NEW: track last known date for real‑time reset
  static const int _maxDailyAds = 10;
  static const int _shortCooldownSeconds = 30;
  static const int _longCooldownSeconds = 60;

  // Ad load error state
  String? _adLoadError;
  bool get _isAdReady =>
      _rewardedAd != null && !_isInCooldown && !_isDailyLimitReached;

  // Daily limit reached?
  bool get _isDailyLimitReached => _dailyAdCount >= _maxDailyAds;

  // Connectivity
  StreamSubscription<List<ConnectivityResult>>? _connectivitySubscription;
  bool _isOffline = false;

  // Real‑time token sync
  StreamSubscription<DocumentSnapshot>? _tokenSubscription;

  // Blue Tick active status with real‑time sync + auto‑expiry timer
  bool _hasActiveBlueTick = false;
  Timestamp? _blueTickExpiry;
  Timer? _expiryCheckTimer;
  Timer? _dailyResetTimer; // NEW: timer for midnight reset check

  // Pulsing animation for active Blue Tick indicator dot
  late AnimationController _pulseController;
  late Animation<double> _pulseAnimation;

  // Change Name cooldown state
  DateTime? _nameChangeCooldownEnd;
  Timer? _nameChangeCooldownTimer;
  bool get _canChangeName =>
      _nameChangeCooldownEnd == null ||
      _nameChangeCooldownEnd!.isBefore(DateTime.now());

  String get _nameChangeCooldownText {
    if (_nameChangeCooldownEnd == null) return '';
    final remaining = _nameChangeCooldownEnd!.difference(DateTime.now());
    if (remaining.inSeconds <= 0) return '';
    final days = remaining.inDays;
    final hours = remaining.inHours.remainder(24);
    if (days > 0) {
      return '$days day${days > 1 ? 's' : ''} ${hours}h';
    } else {
      return '${hours}h ${remaining.inMinutes.remainder(60)}m';
    }
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadTokenBalance();
    _loadDailyAdState();
    _loadCooldownState();
    _loadRewardedAd();
    _initConnectivityListener();
    _subscribeToTokenUpdates();
    _expiryCheckTimer = Timer.periodic(
      const Duration(minutes: 1),
      (_) => _checkBlueTickExpiry(),
    );

    // NEW: Start a timer that checks for daily reset every minute
    _dailyResetTimer = Timer.periodic(
      const Duration(minutes: 1),
      (_) => _checkDailyReset(),
    );

    // Pulsing animation setup
    _pulseController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1400),
    )..repeat(reverse: true);
    _pulseAnimation = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(parent: _pulseController, curve: Curves.easeInOut),
    );

    // Load name change cooldown
    _loadNameChangeCooldown();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pulseController.dispose();
    _expiryCheckTimer?.cancel();
    _dailyResetTimer?.cancel(); // NEW: cancel the reset timer
    _tokenSubscription?.cancel();
    _cooldownTimer?.cancel();
    _nameChangeCooldownTimer?.cancel();
    _rewardedAd?.dispose();
    _connectivitySubscription?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // ─── Real‑time token listener (keeps SettingsTab dashboard in sync) ───────
  void _subscribeToTokenUpdates() {
    if (widget.currentUserId.isEmpty) return;
    _tokenSubscription = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('users')
        .doc(widget.currentUserId)
        .snapshots()
        .listen((snap) {
          if (!snap.exists || !mounted) return;
          final data = snap.data() ?? {};
          final tokens = data['tokens'] as int? ?? 0;
          _blueTickExpiry = data['blueTickExpiry'] as Timestamp?;
          final active =
              _blueTickExpiry != null &&
              _blueTickExpiry!.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());

          // Sync daily ad count from Firestore
          final adCount = data['dailyAdCount'] as int?;
          final adDate = data['adCountDate'] as String?;
          final todayStr = _todayDateString();
          if (adCount != null && adDate == todayStr) {
            _dailyAdCount = adCount;
            _lastAdCountDate = todayStr; // NEW: update last known date
          }

          if (mounted) {
            setState(() {
              _earnedTokens = tokens;
              _hasActiveBlueTick = active;
            });
          }
          SharedPreferences.getInstance().then((prefs) {
            prefs.setInt('earned_tokens', tokens);
            prefs.setInt('daily_ad_count', _dailyAdCount);
            prefs.setString('ad_count_date', todayStr);
          });
        });
  }

  // Auto‑checks expiry every minute
  void _checkBlueTickExpiry() {
    if (!mounted || _blueTickExpiry == null) return;
    final active = _blueTickExpiry!.toDate().isAfter(DateTime.now());
    if (active != _hasActiveBlueTick) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _hasActiveBlueTick = active);
      }
    }
  }

  // ─── NEW: Real‑time daily reset check ─────────────────────────────────────
  void _checkDailyReset() {
    final today = _todayDateString();
    if (_lastAdCountDate.isNotEmpty && _lastAdCountDate != today) {
      // A new day has begun – reset the daily ad count
      _dailyAdCount = 0;
      _lastAdCountDate = today;
      _updateFirestoreDailyAdState(0, today);
      SharedPreferences.getInstance().then((prefs) {
        prefs.setInt('daily_ad_count', 0);
        prefs.setString('ad_count_date', today);
      });
      if (mounted) setState(() {});
    }
  }

  // ─── Connectivity Listener ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _initConnectivityListener() {
    _connectivitySubscription = Connectivity().onConnectivityChanged.listen((
      results,
    ) {
      final bool nowOffline = results.contains(ConnectivityResult.none);
      if (_isOffline != nowOffline) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _isOffline = nowOffline);
        if (!nowOffline && _rewardedAd == null && !_isAdLoading) {
          _loadRewardedAd();
        }
      }
    });
    Connectivity().checkConnectivity().then((results) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isOffline = results.contains(ConnectivityResult.none));
      }
    });
  }

  // ─── Daily Ad State (persisted to Firestore + SharedPreferences) ──────────
  String _todayDateString() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    return '${now.year}-${now.month}-${now.day}';
  }

  Future<void> _loadDailyAdState() async {
    final todayStr = _todayDateString();
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      if (doc.exists) {
        final data = doc.data()!;
        final adCount = data['dailyAdCount'] as int?;
        final adDate = data['adCountDate'] as String?;
        if (adCount != null && adDate == todayStr) {
          _dailyAdCount = adCount;
          _lastAdCountDate = todayStr; // NEW
        } else {
          _dailyAdCount = 0;
          _lastAdCountDate = todayStr; // NEW
          await _updateFirestoreDailyAdState(0, todayStr);
        }
      } else {
        _dailyAdCount = 0;
        _lastAdCountDate = todayStr; // NEW
        await _updateFirestoreDailyAdState(0, todayStr);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Fallback to SharedPreferences
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final count = prefs.getInt('daily_ad_count') ?? 0;
      final dateStr = prefs.getString('ad_count_date');
      if (dateStr == todayStr) {
        _dailyAdCount = count;
        _lastAdCountDate = todayStr; // NEW
      } else {
        _dailyAdCount = 0;
        _lastAdCountDate = todayStr; // NEW
        await prefs.setInt('daily_ad_count', 0);
        await prefs.setString('ad_count_date', todayStr);
      }
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() {});
  }

  Future<void> _updateFirestoreDailyAdState(int count, String date) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .set({
            'dailyAdCount': count,
            'adCountDate': date,
          }, SetOptions(merge: true));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Failed to update Firestore daily ad state: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _incrementDailyAdCount() async {
    _dailyAdCount++;
    final todayStr = _todayDateString();
    _lastAdCountDate = todayStr; // NEW
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setInt('daily_ad_count', _dailyAdCount);
    await prefs.setString('ad_count_date', todayStr);
    await _updateFirestoreDailyAdState(_dailyAdCount, todayStr);
  }

  // ─── Cooldown persistence ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _loadCooldownState() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final cooldownEndStr = prefs.getString('cooldown_end');
    if (cooldownEndStr != null) {
      final end = DateTime.parse(cooldownEndStr);
      if (end.isAfter(DateTime.now())) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _cooldownEnd = end);
        _startCooldownTimer();
      } else {
        await prefs.remove('cooldown_end');
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveCooldownState() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    if (_cooldownEnd != null) {
      await prefs.setString('cooldown_end', _cooldownEnd!.toIso8601String());
    } else {
      await prefs.remove('cooldown_end');
    }
  }

  void _startCooldownTimer() {
    _cooldownTimer?.cancel();
    _cooldownTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (timer) {
      if (!mounted) {
        timer.cancel();
        return;
      }
      if (_cooldownEnd == null || DateTime.now().isAfter(_cooldownEnd!)) {
        setState(() => _cooldownEnd = null);
        _saveCooldownState();
        timer.cancel();
        if (!_isOffline && _rewardedAd == null) _loadRewardedAd();
      } else {
        setState(() {});
      }
    });
  }

  String get _cooldownRemainingText {
    if (_cooldownEnd == null) return '';
    final remaining = _cooldownEnd!.difference(DateTime.now());
    if (remaining.inSeconds <= 0) return '';
    return '${remaining.inSeconds}s';
  }

  // ─── Reset time left for daily limit ───────────────────────────────────────
  String get _resetTimeLeftText {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final tomorrow = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day + 1);
    final diff = tomorrow.difference(now);
    final hours = diff.inHours;
    final minutes = diff.inMinutes.remainder(60);
    if (hours > 0) {
      return 'Resets in ${hours}h ${minutes}m';
    } else {
      return 'Resets in ${minutes}m';
    }
  }

  // ─── Load rewarded ad ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _loadRewardedAd() {
    if (_isAdLoading) return;
    if (_isOffline) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _adLoadError = 'No internet');
      return;
    }
    if (_isDailyLimitReached) return;

    _isAdLoading = true;
    _adLoadError = null;
    if (mounted) setState(() {});

    RewardedAd.load(
      adUnitId: _adUnitId,
      request: const AdRequest(),
      rewardedAdLoadCallback: RewardedAdLoadCallback(
        onAdLoaded: (ad) {
          _rewardedAd = ad;
          _isAdLoading = false;
          _adLoadError = null;
          if (mounted) setState(() {});

          ad.fullScreenContentCallback = FullScreenContentCallback(
            onAdDismissedFullScreenContent: (ad) {
              ad.dispose();
              _rewardedAd = null;
              _loadRewardedAd();
            },
            onAdFailedToShowFullScreenContent: (ad, error) {
              ad.dispose();
              _rewardedAd = null;
              _adLoadError = 'Display failed';
              _loadRewardedAd();
            },
          );
        },
        onAdFailedToLoad: (error) {
          debugPrint('RewardedAd failed to load: $error');
          _isAdLoading = false;
          _rewardedAd = null;

          if (error.code == 0) {
            _adLoadError = 'Ad blocked/Network';
          } else if (error.code == 1) {
            _adLoadError = 'Invalid request';
          } else if (error.code == 2) {
            _adLoadError = 'Network error';
          } else if (error.code == 3) {
            _adLoadError = 'No ad fill';
          } else {
            _adLoadError = 'Load failed (${error.code})';
          }

          if (mounted) setState(() {});

          if (error.code != 3) {
            Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 30), () {
              if (mounted && !_isOffline) _loadRewardedAd();
            });
          }
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── Token balance from Firestore ──────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _loadTokenBalance() async {
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      if (doc.exists && doc.data()?.containsKey('tokens') == true) {
        final tokens = doc.get('tokens') as int;
        if (mounted) setState(() => _earnedTokens = tokens);
        final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
        await prefs.setInt('earned_tokens', tokens);
      } else {
        final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() => _earnedTokens = prefs.getInt('earned_tokens') ?? 0);
        }
        await _updateFirestoreTokens(_earnedTokens);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _earnedTokens = prefs.getInt('earned_tokens') ?? 0);
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _updateFirestoreTokens(int newBalance) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .set({'tokens': newBalance}, SetOptions(merge: true));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Failed to update Firestore tokens: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _incrementTokens(int amount) async {
    final newBalance = _earnedTokens + amount;
    if (mounted) setState(() => _earnedTokens = newBalance);
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setInt('earned_tokens', newBalance);
    await _updateFirestoreTokens(newBalance);
  }

  // ─── Token formatting with K/M ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  String _formatTokens(int tokens) {
    if (tokens >= 1000000) {
      final value = tokens / 1000000;
      return '${value.toStringAsFixed(value.truncateToDouble() == value ? 0 : 1)}M';
    } else if (tokens >= 1000) {
      final value = tokens / 1000;
      return '${value.toStringAsFixed(value.truncateToDouble() == value ? 0 : 1)}K';
    } else {
      return tokens.toString();
    }
  }

  // ─── Watch Ad (dynamic cooldown + daily limit) ─────────────────────────────
  void _watchAd() {
    if (_isDailyLimitReached) {
      _showInfoDialog(
        'Daily Limit Reached',
        'You’ve watched the maximum ads for today. $_resetTimeLeftText.',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (_isInCooldown) {
      _showInfoDialog(
        'Cooldown Active',
        'Please wait $_cooldownRemainingText before watching another ad.',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (_isOffline) {
      _showInfoDialog(
        'No Internet',
        'Please connect to the internet to watch an ad.',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (_rewardedAd == null) {
      String message = _adLoadError ?? 'The ad is not ready yet.';
      _showInfoDialog('Ad Not Ready', message);
      _loadRewardedAd();
      return;
    }

    _rewardedAd!.show(
      onUserEarnedReward: (AdWithoutView ad, RewardItem reward) {
        _incrementTokens(1);
        _incrementDailyAdCount();

        int cooldownSeconds = _dailyAdCount <= 5
            ? _shortCooldownSeconds
            : _longCooldownSeconds;

        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _cooldownEnd = DateTime.now().add(
              Duration(seconds: cooldownSeconds),
            );
          });
        }
        _saveCooldownState();
        _startCooldownTimer();

        _rewardedAd = null;
        _loadRewardedAd();
      },
    );
  }

  void _showInfoDialog(String title, String message) {
    showCupertinoDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => CupertinoAlertDialog(
        title: Text(title),
        content: Text(message),
        actions: [
          CupertinoDialogAction(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
            child: const Text('OK'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── Logout Logic ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _showConfirmDialog({
    required String title,
    required VoidCallback onConfirm,
  }) {
    final isDark = widget.isDark;
    showCupertinoDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: true,
      builder: (ctx) => CupertinoTheme(
        data: CupertinoThemeData(
          brightness: isDark ? Brightness.dark : Brightness.light,
        ),
        child: CupertinoAlertDialog(
          title: Text(
            title,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
              fontSize: 16,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            CupertinoDialogAction(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
              child: const Text('Cancel'),
            ),
            CupertinoDialogAction(
              isDestructiveAction: true,
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
                onConfirm();
              },
              child: const Text('Yes'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showLogoutConfirm() {
    _showConfirmDialog(
      title: 'Are you sure to log out this account?',
      onConfirm: _handleLogout,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _handleLogout() async {
    if (_isLoggingOut) return;
    if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoggingOut = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseAuth.instance.signOut();
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pushAndRemoveUntil(
        PageRouteBuilder(
          pageBuilder: (_, a, _) => const AccountLogin(),
          transitionsBuilder: (_, a, _, child) => FadeTransition(
            opacity: CurvedAnimation(parent: a, curve: Curves.easeOut),
            child: child,
          ),
          transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
        ),
        (route) => false,
      );
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoggingOut = false);
    }
  }

  // ─── Navigation to BlueTick ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _navigateToBlueTick() {
    Navigator.of(
      context,
    ).push(CupertinoPageRoute(builder: (_) => const BlueTick()));
  }

  // ─── Change Name Cooldown ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _loadNameChangeCooldown() async {
    if (widget.currentUserId.isEmpty) return;
    final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('users')
        .doc(widget.currentUserId)
        .get();
    if (!doc.exists) return;
    final data = doc.data()!;
    final ts = data['lastNameChangeAt'] as Timestamp?;
    if (ts != null) {
      final changedAt = ts.toDate();
      final cooldownEnd = changedAt.add(const Duration(days: 7));
      if (cooldownEnd.isAfter(DateTime.now())) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _nameChangeCooldownEnd = cooldownEnd);
        _startNameChangeCooldownTimer();
      }
    }
  }

  void _startNameChangeCooldownTimer() {
    _nameChangeCooldownTimer?.cancel();
    _nameChangeCooldownTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(minutes: 1), (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (_nameChangeCooldownEnd != null &&
          _nameChangeCooldownEnd!.isBefore(DateTime.now())) {
        setState(() => _nameChangeCooldownEnd = null);
      } else {
        setState(() {});
      }
    });
  }

  // Show password modal (slider down popup)
  void _showChangeNamePasswordModal() {
    final isDark = widget.isDark;
    final controller = TextEditingController();
    bool obscure = true;
    bool isLoading = false;

    showCupertinoModalPopup<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, setModalState) {
          return CupertinoTheme(
            data: CupertinoThemeData(
              brightness: isDark ? Brightness.dark : Brightness.light,
            ),
            child: Container(
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
              ),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white,
                borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                  top: Radius.circular(12),
                ),
              ),
              child: SafeArea(
                top: false,
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Container(
                      width: 36,
                      height: 4,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: isDark ? Colors.grey[700] : Colors.grey[400],
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    DefaultTextStyle.merge(
                      style: const TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none),
                      child: Text(
                        'Verify Password',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 17,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                      child: DefaultTextStyle.merge(
                        style: const TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none),
                        child: Text(
                          'Enter your password to change your name',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 13,
                            color: isDark
                                ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                                : const Color(0xFF6D6D72),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                      child: CupertinoTextField(
                        controller: controller,
                        placeholder: 'Password',
                        obscureText: obscure,
                        autocorrect: false,
                        enableSuggestions: false,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          vertical: 12,
                          horizontal: 12,
                        ),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: isDark
                              ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                              : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                          border: Border.all(color: Colors.transparent),
                        ),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
                          decoration: TextDecoration.none,
                        ),
                        placeholderStyle: TextStyle(
                          color: isDark
                              ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                              : const Color(0xFF8E8E93),
                          decoration: TextDecoration.none,
                        ),
                        suffix: CupertinoButton(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                          onPressed: () =>
                              setModalState(() => obscure = !obscure),
                          child: Icon(
                            obscure
                                ? CupertinoIcons.eye_slash
                                : CupertinoIcons.eye,
                            size: 20,
                            color: isDark
                                ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
                                : const Color(0xFF8E8E93),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: CupertinoButton(
                            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
                            child: Text(
                              'Cancel',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        Expanded(
                          child: CupertinoButton(
                            onPressed: isLoading
                                ? null
                                : () async {
                                    final password = controller.text.trim();
                                    if (password.isEmpty) {
                                      _showInfoDialog(
                                        'Error',
                                        'Password cannot be empty',
                                      );
                                      return;
                                    }
                                    setModalState(() => isLoading = true);
                                    try {
                                      final user =
                                          FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
                                      if (user == null) throw 'Not logged in';
                                      final credential =
                                          EmailAuthProvider.credential(
                                            email: user.email!,
                                            password: password,
                                          );
                                      await user.reauthenticateWithCredential(
                                        credential,
                                      );
                                      // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
                                      Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
                                      // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
                                      Navigator.of(context).push(
                                        CupertinoPageRoute(
                                          builder: (_) => ChangeName(
                                            currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
                                            isDark: widget.isDark,
                                            onNameChanged: () {
                                              setState(() {
                                                _nameChangeCooldownEnd =
                                                    DateTime.now().add(
                                                      const Duration(days: 7),
                                                    );
                                              });
                                              _startNameChangeCooldownTimer();
                                              FirebaseFirestore.instance
                                                  .collection('users')
                                                  .doc(widget.currentUserId)
                                                  .set({
                                                    'lastNameChangeAt':
                                                        FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                                                  }, SetOptions(merge: true));
                                            },
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    } catch (e) {
                                      setModalState(() => isLoading = false);
                                      _showInfoDialog(
                                        'Authentication Failed',
                                        'Incorrect password.',
                                      );
                                    }
                                  },
                            child: isLoading
                                ? const CupertinoActivityIndicator()
                                : Text(
                                    'Verify',
                                    style: const TextStyle(
                                      color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── Watch Ad Button Builder ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildWatchAdButton() {
    final bool isButtonEnabled = _isAdReady;

    String buttonText;
    Widget? buttonChild;
    bool isWatchAdState = false;

    if (_isDailyLimitReached) {
      buttonText = 'Limit reached';
    } else if (_isInCooldown) {
      buttonText = _cooldownRemainingText;
    } else if (_isAdLoading) {
      buttonText = 'Loading...';
    } else if (_adLoadError != null || _rewardedAd == null) {
      buttonText = _adLoadError ?? 'Unavailable';
    } else {
      buttonText = 'Watch Ad';
      isWatchAdState = true;
    }

    final Color textColor = isWatchAdState
        ? Colors.white
        : (widget.isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black);

    if (!isWatchAdState &&
        (_adLoadError != null || _rewardedAd == null) &&
        !_isDailyLimitReached &&
        !_isInCooldown &&
        !_isAdLoading) {
      buttonChild = Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(CupertinoIcons.refresh, size: 14, color: textColor),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Flexible(
            child: Text(
              buttonText,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: textColor),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              maxLines: 1,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      );
    } else {
      buttonChild = Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          if (isWatchAdState) ...[
            Icon(CupertinoIcons.play_arrow_solid, size: 14, color: textColor),
            const SizedBox(width: 4),
          ],
          Flexible(
            child: Text(
              buttonText,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: textColor),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              maxLines: 1,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      );
    }

    return CupertinoButton(
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: context.sp(10),
        vertical: context.sp(6),
      ),
      color: isButtonEnabled
          ? const Color(0xFF007AFF)
          : CupertinoColors.systemGrey.withValues(alpha: 0.8),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      onPressed: isButtonEnabled
          ? _watchAd
          : (_isAdLoading || _isInCooldown)
          ? null
          : _isDailyLimitReached
