          ? () => _showInfoDialog('Daily Limit', _resetTimeLeftText)
          : () {
              _loadRewardedAd();
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(content: Text('Trying to load ad...')),
              );
            },
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(
          maxWidth: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.35,
        ),
        child: buttonChild,
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── Build Method ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      color: widget.bgColor,
      child: SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: context.sp(16),
            vertical: context.sp(16),
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              // ─── Token Dashboard Card ─────────────────────────────────────
              Container(
                width: double.infinity,
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(16)),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: widget.cardColor,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  boxShadow: [
                    BoxShadow(
                      color: widget.isDark
                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                          ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.3)
                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                          : const Color(0xFF007AFF).withOpacity(0.08),
                      blurRadius: 16,
                      offset: const Offset(0, 8),
                    ),
                  ],
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: widget.isDark
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.05)
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        : const Color(0xFF007AFF).withOpacity(0.15),
                    width: 1,
                  ),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.all(context.sp(10)),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            gradient: const LinearGradient(
                              colors: [Color(0xFF007AFF), Color(0xFF5AC8FA)],
                              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                            ),
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                            boxShadow: [
                              BoxShadow(
                                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                color: const Color(0xFF007AFF).withOpacity(0.3),
                                blurRadius: 8,
                                offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          child: Icon(
                            CupertinoIcons.sparkles,
                            color: Colors.white,
                            size: context.sp(24),
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(width: context.sp(12)),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                'Token Balance',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: widget.secondaryText,
                                  fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                  letterSpacing: 0.5,
                                ),
                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                maxLines: 1,
                              ),
                              SizedBox(height: context.sp(2)),
                              Text(
                                _formatTokens(_earnedTokens),
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: widget.isDark
                                      ? Colors.white
                                      : Colors.black,
                                  fontSize: context.sp(24),
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  height: 1.1,
                                ),
                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                maxLines: 1,
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(width: context.sp(8)),
                        Flexible(
                          child: Align(
                            alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                            child: _buildWatchAdButton(),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    SizedBox(height: context.sp(16)),

                    // Daily Ads Tracker with Progress Bar
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'Daily Ad Token',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: widget.isDark
                                ? Colors.white
                                : Colors.black87,
                            fontSize: context.sp(13),
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          '${(_maxDailyAds - _dailyAdCount).clamp(0, _maxDailyAds)}/$_maxDailyAds Left',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: _isDailyLimitReached
                                ? const Color(0xFFFF3B30)
                                : const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                            fontSize: context.sp(13),
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),

                    // Progress Bar
                    Stack(

                                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                ? const Color(0xFFFF3B30).withOpacity(0.15)
                                : (widget.isDark
                                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                      ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.1)
                                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                      : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05)),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          ),
                        ),
                        FractionallySizedBox(
                          widthFactor:
                              ((_maxDailyAds - _dailyAdCount).clamp(
                                        0,
                                        _maxDailyAds,

                                      _maxDailyAds)
                                  .toDouble(),

                          ),

                      ],

                    // MODIFIED hint text

                      alignment: Alignment.centerRight,

                        'Refreshes at 00:00 hours',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: context.sp(10),
                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                          color: widget.secondaryText.withOpacity(0.7),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),

                    // Limit Reached Timer Alert
                    if (_isDailyLimitReached) ...[
                      SizedBox(height: context.sp(12)),
                      Container(
                        width: double.infinity,

                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                          color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30).withOpacity(0.1),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),

                            // ignore: deprecated_member_use

                          ),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            Icon(
                              CupertinoIcons.timer,
                              size: context.sp(16),
                              color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: context.sp(6)),
                            Text(
                              _resetTimeLeftText,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                                fontSize: context.sp(13),
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),

              // ─── NEW: Section Header for Settings Options ─────────────────
              Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                  left: context.sp(4),
                  bottom: context.sp(8),
                ),
                child: Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                  child: Text(
                    'Account Settings',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: context.sp(14),
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,

              // Settings Options Group
              Container(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: widget.cardColor,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),

                  children: [

                      icon: CupertinoIcons.archivebox_fill,

                      onTap: () {

                            pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => ArchiveChats(
                              currentUserId: widget.currentUserId,
                              isDark: widget.isDark,

                                  position:

                                        begin: const Offset(0, 1),
                                        end: Offset.zero,
                                      ).animate(
                                        CurvedAnimation(
                                          parent: anim,
                                          curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                                        ),

                              milliseconds: 300,

                    _buildSettingsOption(
                      icon: CupertinoIcons.checkmark_seal_fill,
                      title: 'Verification Badge',
