                      trailing: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          if (_hasActiveBlueTick) ...[
                            AnimatedBuilder(
                              animation: _pulseAnimation,
                              builder: (context, child) {
                                final pulseFactor = _pulseAnimation.value;
                                return Container(
                                  width: context.sp(8),
                                  height: context.sp(8),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: const Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                    boxShadow: [
                                      BoxShadow(
                                        color: const Color(
                                          0xFF007AFF,
                                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                                        ).withOpacity(0.85),
                                        blurRadius: 4.0 + (pulseFactor * 8.0),
                                        spreadRadius: 0.5 + (pulseFactor * 1.0),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: context.sp(6)),
                          ],
                          Icon(
                            CupertinoIcons.chevron_right,
                            size: context.sp(16),
                            color: widget.secondaryText,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    _buildDivider(),
                    _buildSettingsOption(
                      icon: CupertinoIcons.person,
                      title: 'Change Name',
                      onTap: _canChangeName
                          ? _showChangeNamePasswordModal
                          : () {
                              _showInfoDialog(
                                'Name Change Cooldown',
                                'You can change your name again in $_nameChangeCooldownText.',
                              );
                            },
                      trailing: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          if (!_canChangeName)
                            Text(
                              _nameChangeCooldownText,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 13,
                                color: widget.secondaryText,
                              ),
                            ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 4),
                          Icon(
                            CupertinoIcons.chevron_right,
                            size: 16,
                            color: widget.secondaryText,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    _buildDivider(),
                    _buildSettingsOption(
                      icon: CupertinoIcons.square_arrow_right,
                      title: 'Log Out',
                      iconColor: const Color(0xFFFF3B30),
                      trailing: _isLoggingOut
                          ? const CupertinoActivityIndicator(radius: 10)
                          : const Icon(CupertinoIcons.chevron_right, size: 16),
                      onTap: _isLoggingOut ? () {} : _showLogoutConfirm,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDivider() {
    return Divider(
      height: 0.5,
      thickness: 0.5,
      indent: 56,
      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
      color: widget.secondaryText.withOpacity(0.2),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSettingsOption({
    required IconData icon,
    required String title,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
    Color? iconColor,
    Widget? trailing,
  }) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: context.sp(16),
          vertical: context.sp(14),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(
              icon,
              size: context.sp(24),
              color: iconColor ?? const Color(0xFF007AFF),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: context.sp(16)),
            Text(
              title,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: widget.isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
                fontSize: context.sp(16).clamp(14, 18),
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              ),
            ),
            const Spacer(),
            trailing ??
                Icon(
                  CupertinoIcons.chevron_right,
                  size: context.sp(16),
                  color: widget.secondaryText,
                ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CHANGE NAME SCREEN – Full-screen with nav bar like ArchiveChats
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class ChangeName extends StatefulWidget {
  final String currentUserId;
  final bool isDark;
  final VoidCallback onNameChanged;

  const ChangeName({
    super.key,
    required this.currentUserId,
    required this.isDark,
    required this.onNameChanged,
  });

  @override
  State<ChangeName> createState() => _ChangeNameState();
}

class _ChangeNameState extends State<ChangeName> {
  final _firstNameController = TextEditingController();
  final _lastNameController = TextEditingController();
  bool _isLoading = true;
  bool _isSaving = false;
  String? _currentFirstName;
  String? _currentLastName;
  bool _hasChanges = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadCurrentName();
    _firstNameController.addListener(_onTextChanged);
    _lastNameController.addListener(_onTextChanged);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _firstNameController.removeListener(_onTextChanged);
    _lastNameController.removeListener(_onTextChanged);
    _firstNameController.dispose();
    _lastNameController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onTextChanged() {
    final newHasChanges =
        _firstNameController.text.trim() != (_currentFirstName ?? '') ||
        _lastNameController.text.trim() != (_currentLastName ?? '');
    if (_hasChanges != newHasChanges) {
      setState(() => _hasChanges = newHasChanges);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadCurrentName() async {
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      if (doc.exists) {
        final data = doc.data()!;
        final firstName = data['firstName'] as String? ?? '';
        final lastName = data['lastName'] as String? ?? '';
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _currentFirstName = firstName;
            _currentLastName = lastName;
            _firstNameController.text = firstName;
            _lastNameController.text = lastName;
          });
          _onTextChanged(); // initial check
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Failed to load name: $e');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveName() async {
    final newFirst = _firstNameController.text.trim();
    final newLast = _lastNameController.text.trim();
    if (newFirst.isEmpty || newLast.isEmpty) {
      _showError('First name and last name cannot be empty.');
      return;
    }
    if (newFirst == _currentFirstName && newLast == _currentLastName) {
      _showError('No changes detected.');
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isSaving = true);
    try {
      // Update Firestore
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(widget.currentUserId)
          .set({
            'firstName': newFirst,
            'lastName': newLast,
            'lastNameChangeAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
          }, SetOptions(merge: true));

      // Also update participantNames in all conversations where this user participates
      // (optional, but recommended for consistency)
      final conversations = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('conversations')
          .where('participants', arrayContains: widget.currentUserId)
          .get();
      final batch = FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
      for (final doc in conversations.docs) {
        batch.update(doc.reference, {
          'participantNames.${widget.currentUserId}': '$newFirst $newLast',
        });
      }
      await batch.commit();

      widget.onNameChanged();
      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.of(context).pop();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _showError('Failed to update name. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isSaving = false);
    }
  }

  void _showError(String message) {
    showCupertinoDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => CupertinoAlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Error'),
        content: Text(message),
        actions: [
          CupertinoDialogAction(
            child: const Text('OK'),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = widget.isDark;
    final primaryText = isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
    final secondaryText = isDark
        ? const Color(0xFF8E8E93)
        : const Color(0xFF6D6D72);
    final bgColor = isDark ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFFF2F2F7);
    final cardColor = isDark ? const Color(0xFF1C1C1E) : Colors.white;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: bgColor,
      body: Column(
        children: [
          // Navigation bar (height 40 + status padding)
          Container(
            height: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 40,
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: bgColor,
              border: Border(
                bottom: BorderSide(
                  color: isDark
                      ? const Color(0xFF38383A)
                      : const Color(0xFFC6C6C8),
                  width: 0.5,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                    behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                    child: const Padding(
                      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 8),
                      child: Icon(
                        CupertinoIcons.back,
                        color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                        size: 22,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(
                    'Change Name',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: primaryText,
                      fontSize: context.sp(20).clamp(16.0, 20.0),
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const Spacer(),
                  if (!_isLoading && _hasChanges)
                    CupertinoButton(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                      onPressed: _isSaving ? null : _saveName,
                      child: _isSaving
                          ? const CupertinoActivityIndicator()
                          : const Text(
                              'Save',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: Color(0xFF007AFF),
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              ),
                            ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          // Content
          Expanded(
            child: SafeArea(
              top: false,
              child: _isLoading
                  ? const Center(child: CupertinoActivityIndicator())
                  : SingleChildScrollView(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          // Current name display
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: cardColor,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            ),
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                Icon(
                                  CupertinoIcons.person_crop_circle,
                                  size: 32,
                                  color: isDark
                                      ? Colors.white54
                                      : Colors.black54,
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                                Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(
                                      'Current name',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        fontSize: 13,
                                        color: secondaryText,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                    Text(
                                      '$_currentFirstName $_currentLastName',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        fontSize: 18,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                        color: primaryText,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 24),
                          // First name field
                          Text(
                            'First Name',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 13,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                              color: secondaryText,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          CupertinoTextField(
                            controller: _firstNameController,
                            placeholder: 'Enter first name',
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: cardColor,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            ),
                            style: TextStyle(color: primaryText),
                            placeholderStyle: TextStyle(color: secondaryText),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 20),
                          // Last name field
                          Text(
                            'Last Name',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 13,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                              color: secondaryText,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          CupertinoTextField(
                            controller: _lastNameController,
                            placeholder: 'Enter last name',
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: cardColor,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            ),
                            style: TextStyle(color: primaryText),
                            placeholderStyle: TextStyle(color: secondaryText),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 32),
                          // Info text about 7-day cooldown
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: isDark
                                  ? const Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
                                  : const Color(0xFFE5E5EA),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            ),
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                Icon(
                                  CupertinoIcons.info_circle,
                                  size: 18,
                                  color: secondaryText,
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Text(
                                    'You can change your name once every 7 days.',
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      fontSize: 13,
                                      color: secondaryText,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ACCOUNT CREATION SCREEN
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class AccountCreation extends StatefulWidget {
  const AccountCreation({super.key});

  @override
  State<AccountCreation> createState() => _AccountCreationState();
}

class _AccountCreationState extends State<AccountCreation>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  // ── Profile picture ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  File? _profileImage;

  // ── Text controllers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  final _firstNameCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _lastNameCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _emailCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _retypeCtrl = TextEditingController();

  // ── Focus nodes ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  final _firstNameFocus = FocusNode();
  final _lastNameFocus = FocusNode();
  final _emailFocus = FocusNode();
  final _passwordFocus = FocusNode();
  final _retypeFocus = FocusNode();

  // ── Form state ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  DateTime? _dob;
  String? _gender;

  bool _emailChecked = false;
  bool _emailAvailable = false;
  bool _isCheckingEmail = false;
  String? _emailError;

  bool _showPassword = false;
  bool _showRetype = false;

  bool _isCreatingAccount = false;

  // ── Section visibility flags (animated reveal) ────────────────────────────────
  bool _dobVisible = false;
  bool _genderVisible = false;
  bool _emailVisible = false;
  bool _passwordVisible = false;
  bool _finishVisible = false;

  // ── Animation controllers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  late final AnimationController _dobCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _genderCtrl;
  late final AnimationController _emailCtrl2;
  late final AnimationController _passwordCtrl2;
  late final AnimationController _finishCtrl;

  late final Animation<double> _dobFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _dobSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _genderFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _genderSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _emailFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _emailSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _passwordFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _passwordSlide;
  late final Animation<double> _finishFade;
  late final Animation<Offset> _finishSlide;

  // ── Scroll ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  final _scrollCtrl = ScrollController();

  // ── GlobalKeys for scroll-to ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  final _dobKey = GlobalKey();
  final _genderKey = GlobalKey();
  final _emailKey = GlobalKey();
  final _passwordKey = GlobalKey();
  final _finishKey = GlobalKey();

  // ── Completion getters ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  bool get _nameComplete =>
      _firstNameCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty &&
      _lastNameCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty;
  bool get _dobComplete => _dob != null;
  bool get _genderComplete => _gender != null;
  bool get _emailComplete => _emailChecked && _emailAvailable;
  bool get _passwordMatch =>
      _passwordCtrl.text.isNotEmpty &&
      _retypeCtrl.text.isNotEmpty &&
      _passwordCtrl.text == _retypeCtrl.text;
  bool get _passwordComplete =>
      _passwordCtrl.text.length >= 6 && _passwordMatch;
  bool get _allComplete =>
      _nameComplete &&
      _dobComplete &&
      _genderComplete &&
      _emailComplete &&
      _passwordComplete;

  // ── Init ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    AnimationController makeCtrl() => AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 480),
    );

    _dobCtrl = makeCtrl();
    _genderCtrl = makeCtrl();
    _emailCtrl2 = makeCtrl();
    _passwordCtrl2 = makeCtrl();
    _finishCtrl = makeCtrl();

    Animation<double> makeFade(AnimationController c) =>
        CurvedAnimation(parent: c, curve: Curves.easeOut);
    Animation<Offset> makeSlide(AnimationController c) => Tween<Offset>(
      begin: const Offset(0, 0.18),
      end: Offset.zero,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: c, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic));

    _dobFade = makeFade(_dobCtrl);
    _dobSlide = makeSlide(_dobCtrl);
    _genderFade = makeFade(_genderCtrl);
    _genderSlide = makeSlide(_genderCtrl);
    _emailFade = makeFade(_emailCtrl2);
    _emailSlide = makeSlide(_emailCtrl2);
    _passwordFade = makeFade(_passwordCtrl2);
    _passwordSlide = makeSlide(_passwordCtrl2);
    _finishFade = makeFade(_finishCtrl);
    _finishSlide = makeSlide(_finishCtrl);

    _firstNameCtrl.addListener(_onNameChanged);
    _lastNameCtrl.addListener(_onNameChanged);
    _emailCtrl.addListener(_onEmailTyped);
    _passwordCtrl.addListener(_onPwChanged);
    _retypeCtrl.addListener(_onPwChanged);
  }

  // ── Listeners ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _onNameChanged() {
    final shouldRevealDob = _nameComplete && !_dobVisible;
    setState(() {
      if (shouldRevealDob) {
        _dobVisible = true;
      }
    });
    if (shouldRevealDob) {
      _dobCtrl.forward().then((_) => _scrollToKey(_dobKey));
    }
  }

  void _onEmailTyped() {
    if (_emailChecked) {
      setState(() {
        _emailChecked = false;
        _emailAvailable = false;
        _emailError = null;
      });
    }
  }

  void _onPwChanged() {
    final shouldRevealFinish = _passwordComplete && !_finishVisible;
    setState(() {
      if (shouldRevealFinish) {
        _finishVisible = true;
      }
    });
    if (shouldRevealFinish) {
      _finishCtrl.forward().then((_) => _scrollToKey(_finishKey));
    }
  }

  void _scrollToKey(GlobalKey key) {
    Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 280), () {
      final ctx = key.currentContext;
      if (ctx == null || !mounted) return;
      Scrollable.ensureVisible(
        // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
        ctx,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        alignment: 0.05,
      );
    });
  }

  // ── Dispose ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _dobCtrl.dispose();
    _genderCtrl.dispose();
    _emailCtrl2.dispose();
    _passwordCtrl2.dispose();
    _finishCtrl.dispose();
    _firstNameCtrl.dispose();
    _lastNameCtrl.dispose();
    _emailCtrl.dispose();
    _passwordCtrl.dispose();
    _retypeCtrl.dispose();
    _firstNameFocus.dispose();
    _lastNameFocus.dispose();
    _emailFocus.dispose();
    _passwordFocus.dispose();
    _retypeFocus.dispose();
    _scrollCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // ── Pick profile image ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _pickProfileImage() async {
    try {
      final picker = ImagePicker();
      final picked = await picker.pickImage(
        source: ImageSource.gallery,
        imageQuality: 85,
      );
      if (picked != null && mounted) {
        setState(() => _profileImage = File(picked.path));
      }
    } catch (_) {
      _showErrorSnack('Could not open gallery. Check permissions.');
    }
  }

  // ── Upload to Cloudinary ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<String?> _uploadToCloudinary(File file) async {
    try {
      final uri = Uri.parse(
        'https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/dud8djshl/image/upload',
      );
      final request = http.MultipartRequest('POST', uri)
        ..fields['upload_preset'] = 'profile_pic'
        ..files.add(await http.MultipartFile.fromPath('file', file.path));
      final streamed = await request.send();
      final body = await streamed.stream.bytesToString();
      if (streamed.statusCode == 200) {
        final data = json.decode(body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        return data['secure_url'] as String?;
      }
      return null;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // ── Check email availability ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _checkEmail() async {
    final email = _emailCtrl.text.trim().toLowerCase();
    final emailRegex = RegExp(r'^[\w.+\-]+@[\w\-]+\.[a-zA-Z]{2,}$');
    if (email.isEmpty || !emailRegex.hasMatch(email)) {
      setState(() => _emailError = 'Enter a valid email address');
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _isCheckingEmail = true;
      _emailError = null;
    });
    try {
      final snap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .where('email', isEqualTo: email)
          .limit(1)
          .get();
      final available = snap.docs.isEmpty;
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _emailChecked = true;
        _emailAvailable = available;
        _emailError = available ? null : 'This email is already registered';
        _isCheckingEmail = false;
      });
      if (available) {
        _emailFocus.unfocus();
        if (!_passwordVisible) {
          setState(() => _passwordVisible = true);
          _passwordCtrl2.forward().then((_) => _scrollToKey(_passwordKey));
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isCheckingEmail = false;
        _emailError = 'Network error. Please try again.';
      });
    }
  }

  // ── DOB picker ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _pickDate() async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final initial = _dob ?? DateTime(now.year - 18, now.month, now.day);
    final picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: initial,
      firstDate: DateTime(1900),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year - 6, now.month, now.day),
      builder: (ctx, child) {
        final isDark = ctx.isDark;
        return Theme(
          data: Theme.of(ctx).copyWith(
            colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
              seedColor: ctx.colors.primary,
              brightness: isDark ? Brightness.dark : Brightness.light,
            ),
          ),
          child: child!,
        );
      },
    );
    if (picked != null && mounted) {
      setState(() => _dob = picked);
      if (!_genderVisible) {
        setState(() => _genderVisible = true);
        _genderCtrl.forward().then((_) => _scrollToKey(_genderKey));
      }
    }
  }

  // ── Select gender ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _selectGender(String g) {
    setState(() => _gender = g);
    if (!_emailVisible) {
      setState(() => _emailVisible = true);
      _emailCtrl2.forward().then((_) => _scrollToKey(_emailKey));
    }
  }

  // ── Finish flow ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _onFinish() async {
    if (!_allComplete) return;
    if (_profileImage == null) {
      _showProfilePicDialog();
      return;
    }
    await _createAccount(photoFile: _profileImage);
  }

  // ── Profile-pic dialog ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _showProfilePicDialog() {
    final c = context.colors;
    showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (dialogCtx) => Dialog(
        backgroundColor: c.cardBg,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22)),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 28, 24, 24),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              // Icon
              Container(
                width: 68,
                height: 68,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                  color: c.warning.withOpacity(0.14),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  Icons.photo_camera_rounded,
                  color: c.warning,
                  size: 34,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 18),
              Text(
                'You forgot to set\nprofile picture!',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  color: c.primaryText,
                  height: 1.3,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                'A profile picture helps others recognise you.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 13,
                  color: c.secondaryText,
                  height: 1.5,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 26),
              Row(
                children: [
                  // Skip
                  Expanded(
                    child: GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () {
                        Navigator.of(dialogCtx).pop();
                        _createAccount(photoFile: null);
                      },
                      child: Container(
                        height: 50,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: c.fieldBg,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
                          border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                        ),
                        child: Center(
                          child: Text(
                            'Skip',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: c.primaryText,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              fontSize: 15,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  // Set
                  Expanded(
                    child: GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () async {
                        Navigator.of(dialogCtx).pop();
                        await _pickProfileImage();
                        if (!mounted) return;
                        if (_profileImage != null) {
                          await _createAccount(photoFile: _profileImage);
                        }
                      },
                      child: Container(
                        height: 50,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          gradient: LinearGradient(
                            colors: [c.primary, c.primaryDeep],
                          ),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
                          boxShadow: [
                            BoxShadow(
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.35),
                              blurRadius: 10,
                              offset: const Offset(0, 3),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        child: const Center(
                          child: Text(
                            'Set Photo',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              fontSize: 15,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── Create account ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Future<void> _createAccount({File? photoFile}) async {
    if (_isCreatingAccount) return;
    setState(() => _isCreatingAccount = true);
    try {
      // 1. Upload photo if provided
      String photoUrl = '';
      if (photoFile != null) {
        photoUrl = await _uploadToCloudinary(photoFile) ?? '';
      }

      // 2. Create Firebase Auth user
      final cred = await FirebaseAuth.instance.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
        email: _emailCtrl.text.trim().toLowerCase(),
        password: _passwordCtrl.text,
      );

      // 3. Store profile in Firestore
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .doc(cred.user!.uid)
          .set({
            'uid': cred.user!.uid,
            'firstName': _firstNameCtrl.text.trim(),
            'lastName': _lastNameCtrl.text.trim(),
            'email': _emailCtrl.text.trim().toLowerCase(),
            'gender': _gender,
            'dob': _dob != null ? Timestamp.fromDate(_dob!) : null,
            'profilePhotoUrl': photoUrl,
            'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
          });

      // 4. Navigate back to EntryScreen with success message
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pushAndRemoveUntil(
        PageRouteBuilder(
          pageBuilder: (_, a, _) => const EntryScreen(
            successMessage: 'You just created your account.',
          ),
          transitionsBuilder: (_, a, _, child) =>
              FadeTransition(opacity: a, child: child),
          transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 2000),
        ),
        (_) => false,
      );
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isCreatingAccount = false);
      _showErrorSnack(e.message ?? 'Authentication failed. Try again.');
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isCreatingAccount = false);
      _showErrorSnack('Something went wrong. Please try again.');
    }
  }

  // ── Error snack ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _showErrorSnack(String msg) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    final c = context.colors;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..clearSnackBars()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(
                Icons.error_outline_rounded,
                color: Colors.white,
                size: 18,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Flexible(
                child: Text(
                  msg,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          backgroundColor: c.error,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          margin: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, context.hp(32)),
          duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),
        ),
      );
  }

  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  BUILD
  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = context.colors;
    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value:
          (context.isDark
                  ? SystemUiOverlayStyle.light
                  : SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark)
              .copyWith(statusBarColor: Colors.transparent),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        body: Stack(
          children: [
            const _MeshBackground(),
            SafeArea(
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  // ── Nav Bar (height 40) ─────────────────────────────────────
                  _NavBar(
                    profileImage: _profileImage,
                    onBackTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                    onAvatarTap: _pickProfileImage,
                    colors: c,
                  ),

                  // ── Scrollable form body ───────────────────────────────────
                  Expanded(
                    child: SingleChildScrollView(
                      controller: _scrollCtrl,
                      physics: const ClampingScrollPhysics(),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: context.sp(18).clamp(14.0, 26.0),
                        vertical: 16,
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          // NAME section (always visible)
                          _SectionCard(
                            colors: c,
                            icon: Icons.person_rounded,
                            title: 'Name',
                            isComplete: _nameComplete,
                            child: _buildNameSection(c),
                          ),

                          // DOB section
                          if (_dobVisible)
                            _AnimatedSection(
                              sectionKey: _dobKey,
                              fade: _dobFade,
                              slide: _dobSlide,
                              child: _SectionCard(
                                colors: c,
                                icon: Icons.cake_rounded,
                                title: 'Date of Birth',
                                isComplete: _dobComplete,
                                child: _buildDobSection(c),
                              ),
                            ),

                          // GENDER section
                          if (_genderVisible)
                            _AnimatedSection(
                              sectionKey: _genderKey,
                              fade: _genderFade,
                              slide: _genderSlide,
                              child: _SectionCard(
                                colors: c,
                                icon: Icons.wc_rounded,
                                title: 'Gender',
                                isComplete: _genderComplete,
                                child: _buildGenderSection(c),
                              ),
                            ),

                          // EMAIL section
                          if (_emailVisible)
                            _AnimatedSection(
                              sectionKey: _emailKey,
                              fade: _emailFade,
                              slide: _emailSlide,
                              child: _SectionCard(
                                colors: c,
                                icon: Icons.email_rounded,
                                title: 'Email',
                                isComplete: _emailComplete,
                                child: _buildEmailSection(c),
                              ),
                            ),

                          // PASSWORD section
                          if (_passwordVisible)
                            _AnimatedSection(
                              sectionKey: _passwordKey,
                              fade: _passwordFade,
                              slide: _passwordSlide,
                              child: _SectionCard(
                                colors: c,
                                icon: Icons.lock_rounded,
                                title: 'Password',
                                isComplete: _passwordComplete,
                                child: _buildPasswordSection(c),
                              ),
                            ),

                          // FINISH BUTTON
                          if (_finishVisible)
                            _AnimatedSection(
                              sectionKey: _finishKey,
                              fade: _finishFade,
                              slide: _finishSlide,
                              child: _buildFinishButton(c),
                            ),

                          const SizedBox(height: 40),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            // ── Loading overlay ────────────────────────────────────────────
            if (_isCreatingAccount)
              Container(
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.55),
                child: Center(
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: 36,
                      vertical: 30,
                    ),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: c.cardBg,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
                      boxShadow: [
                        BoxShadow(
                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                          color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.2),
                          blurRadius: 30,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        SizedBox(
                          width: 40,
                          height: 40,
                          child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                            color: c.primary,
                            strokeWidth: 3,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 18),
                        Text(
                          'Creating your account…',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: c.primaryText,
                            fontSize: 15,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── Name Section ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildNameSection(AppColors c) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        _StyledField(
          colors: c,
          controller: _firstNameCtrl,
          focus: _firstNameFocus,
          hint: 'First Name',
          icon: Icons.badge_rounded,
          nextFocus: _lastNameFocus,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        _StyledField(
          colors: c,
          controller: _lastNameCtrl,
          focus: _lastNameFocus,
          hint: 'Last Name',
          icon: Icons.badge_outlined,
          textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
          onSubmitted: () => _lastNameFocus.unfocus(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // ── DOB Section ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildDobSection(AppColors c) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: _pickDate,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: c.fieldBg,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
          border: Border.all(
            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
            color: _dob != null ? c.success.withOpacity(0.55) : c.cardBorder,
          ),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.calendar_today_rounded,
              color: _dob != null ? c.success : c.secondaryText,
              size: 20,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Text(
              _dob != null
                  ? DateFormat('MMMM d, yyyy').format(_dob!)
                  : 'Choose Date of Birth',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: _dob != null ? c.primaryText : c.secondaryText,
                fontSize: 15,
                fontWeight: _dob != null ? FontWeight.w500 : FontWeight.w400,
              ),
            ),
            const Spacer(),
            Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, color: c.secondaryText, size: 22),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── Gender Section ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildGenderSection(AppColors c) {
    const options = ['Male', 'Female', 'Other'];
    return Row(
      children: options.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
        final isLast = entry.key == options.length - 1;
        final g = entry.value;
        final selected = _gender == g;
        return Expanded(
          child: Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(right: isLast ? 0 : 8),
            child: GestureDetector(
              onTap: () => _selectGender(g),
              child: AnimatedContainer(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
                curve: Curves.easeOut,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: selected
                      ? LinearGradient(colors: [c.primary, c.primaryDeep])
                      : null,
                  color: selected ? null : c.fieldBg,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: selected ? c.primary : c.cardBorder,
                    width: selected ? 1.5 : 0.8,
                  ),
                  boxShadow: selected
                      ? [
                          BoxShadow(
                            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                            color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.30),
                            blurRadius: 10,
                            offset: const Offset(0, 3),
                          ),
                        ]
                      : null,
                ),
                child: Center(
                  child: Text(
                    g,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: selected ? Colors.white : c.primaryText,
                      fontWeight: selected ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.w400,
                      fontSize: 14,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }

  // ── Email Section ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildEmailSection(AppColors c) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: _StyledField(
                colors: c,
                controller: _emailCtrl,
                focus: _emailFocus,
                hint: 'your@email.com',
                icon: Icons.alternate_email_rounded,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
                onSubmitted: _checkEmail,
                showSuccess: _emailComplete,
                showError: _emailError != null,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: _isCheckingEmail ? null : _checkEmail,
              child: AnimatedContainer(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 18,
                  vertical: 14.5,
                ),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: LinearGradient(colors: [c.primary, c.primaryDeep]),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
                  boxShadow: [
                    BoxShadow(
                      // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                      color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.30),
                      blurRadius: 8,
                      offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                child: _isCheckingEmail
                    ? const SizedBox(
                        width: 20,
                        height: 20,
                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                          strokeWidth: 2,
                          color: Colors.white,
                        ),
                      )
                    : const Text(
                        'Check',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          fontSize: 14,
                        ),
                      ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        if (_emailError != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 7),
          _StatusRow(
            icon: Icons.error_outline_rounded,
            text: _emailError!,
            color: c.error,
          ),
        ],
        if (_emailComplete) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 7),
          _StatusRow(
            icon: Icons.check_circle_rounded,
            text: 'Email is available!',
            color: c.success,
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  // ── Password Section ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildPasswordSection(AppColors c) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        _StyledField(
          colors: c,
          controller: _passwordCtrl,
          focus: _passwordFocus,
          hint: 'Set Password',
          icon: Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
          obscure: !_showPassword,
          nextFocus: _retypeFocus,
          suffix: GestureDetector(
            onTap: () => setState(() => _showPassword = !_showPassword),
            child: Icon(
              _showPassword
                  ? Icons.visibility_off_rounded
                  : Icons.visibility_rounded,
              color: c.secondaryText,
              size: 20,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        _StyledField(
          colors: c,
          controller: _retypeCtrl,
          focus: _retypeFocus,
          hint: 'Retype Password',
          icon: Icons.lock_rounded,
          obscure: !_showRetype,
          textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
          onSubmitted: () => _retypeFocus.unfocus(),
          showSuccess: _passwordMatch && _retypeCtrl.text.isNotEmpty,
          showError: _retypeCtrl.text.isNotEmpty && !_passwordMatch,
          suffix: GestureDetector(
            onTap: () => setState(() => _showRetype = !_showRetype),
            child: Icon(
              _showRetype
                  ? Icons.visibility_off_rounded
                  : Icons.visibility_rounded,
              color: c.secondaryText,
              size: 20,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        if (_retypeCtrl.text.isNotEmpty && !_passwordMatch) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 7),
          _StatusRow(
            icon: Icons.error_outline_rounded,
            text: 'Passwords do not match',
            color: context.colors.error,
          ),
        ],
        if (_passwordMatch && _retypeCtrl.text.isNotEmpty) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 7),
          _StatusRow(
            icon: Icons.check_circle_rounded,
            text: 'Passwords match!',
            color: context.colors.success,
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  // ── Finish Button ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildFinishButton(AppColors c) {
    final enabled = _allComplete;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
      child: AnimatedOpacity(
        opacity: enabled ? 1.0 : 0.45,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
        child: GestureDetector(
          onTap: enabled ? _onFinish : null,
          child: Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: 47,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                colors: [c.primary, c.primaryDeep, c.accent],
                stops: const [0.0, 0.55, 1.0],
              ),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(17),
              boxShadow: enabled
                  ? [
                      BoxShadow(
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.45),
                        blurRadius: 20,
                        offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                      ),
                    ]
                  : null,
            ),
            child: const Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.check_circle_rounded, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                SizedBox(width: 10),
                Text(
                  'Finish & Create Account',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 15,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    letterSpacing: -0.3,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  NAV BAR WIDGET (height 40)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _NavBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final File? profileImage;
  final VoidCallback onBackTap;
  final VoidCallback onAvatarTap;
  final AppColors colors;

  const _NavBar({
    required this.profileImage,
    required this.onBackTap,
    required this.onAvatarTap,
    required this.colors,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    return Container(
      height: 40,
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: context.sp(14).clamp(12.0, 20.0),
      ),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        color: c.background.withOpacity(0.95),
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: c.divider, width: 0.5)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          // Back
          GestureDetector(
            onTap: onBackTap,
            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
            child: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.chevron_left_rounded, color: c.primary, size: 28),
                Text(
                  'Back',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: c.primary,
                    fontSize: 17,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          // Title (centered)
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              'Create Account',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: c.primaryText,
                fontSize: 16,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          // Avatar
          GestureDetector(
            onTap: onAvatarTap,
            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
            child: Stack(
              alignment: Alignment.bottomRight,
              children: [
                AnimatedContainer(
                  duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
                  width: 30,
                  height: 30,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    gradient: profileImage == null
                        ? LinearGradient(colors: c.brandGradient)
                        : null,
                    border: Border.all(
                      color: profileImage != null
                          ? c.success
                          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                          : c.primary.withOpacity(0.45),
                      width: 1.5,
                    ),
                    boxShadow: profileImage != null
                        ? [
                            BoxShadow(
                              // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                              color: c.success.withOpacity(0.35),
                              blurRadius: 8,
                            ),
                          ]
                        : null,
                  ),
                  child: profileImage != null
                      ? ClipOval(
                          child: Image.file(
                            profileImage!,
                            fit: BoxFit.cover,
                            width: 30,
                            height: 30,
                          ),
                        )
                      : const Icon(
                          Icons.person_rounded,
                          color: Colors.white,
                          size: 17,
                        ),
                ),
                // Camera badge
                if (profileImage == null)
                  Container(
                    width: 11,
                    height: 11,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      color: c.primary,
                      border: Border.all(color: c.background, width: 1),
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(
                      Icons.add_rounded,
                      color: Colors.white,
                      size: 7,
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ANIMATED SECTION WRAPPER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _AnimatedSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final GlobalKey sectionKey;
  final Animation<double> fade;
  final Animation<Offset> slide;
  final Widget child;

  const _AnimatedSection({
    required this.sectionKey,
    required this.fade,
    required this.slide,
    required this.child,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      key: sectionKey,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
      child: FadeTransition(
        opacity: fade,
        child: SlideTransition(position: slide, child: child),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SECTION CARD
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _SectionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppColors colors;
  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final bool isComplete;
  final Widget child;

  const _SectionCard({
    required this.colors,
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    required this.isComplete,
    required this.child,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    return AnimatedContainer(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: c.cardBg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(
          // ignore: deprecated_member_use
          color: isComplete ? c.success.withOpacity(0.45) : c.cardBorder,
          width: isComplete ? 1.3 : 0.8,
        ),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            // ignore: deprecated_member_use
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(c.isDark ? 0.18 : 0.05),
            blurRadius: 12,
            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                AnimatedContainer(
                  duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(7),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    color: isComplete
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        ? c.success.withOpacity(0.13)
                        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                        : c.primary.withOpacity(0.10),
                  ),
                  child: Icon(
                    isComplete ? Icons.check_rounded : icon,
                    color: isComplete ? c.success : c.primary,
                    size: 17,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Text(
                  title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: c.primaryText,
                    fontSize: 15,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
                if (isComplete) ...[
                  const Spacer(),
                  Text(
                    'Done',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: c.success,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            child,
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  STYLED TEXT FIELD
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _StyledField extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppColors colors;
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final FocusNode focus;
  final String hint;
  final IconData icon;
  final bool obscure;
  final Widget? suffix;
  final TextInputType? keyboardType;
  final TextInputAction textInputAction;
  final FocusNode? nextFocus;
  final VoidCallback? onSubmitted;
  final bool showSuccess;
  final bool showError;

  const _StyledField({
    required this.colors,
    required this.controller,
    required this.focus,
    required this.hint,
    required this.icon,
    this.obscure = false,
    this.suffix,
    this.keyboardType,
    this.textInputAction = TextInputAction.next,
    this.nextFocus,
    this.onSubmitted,
    this.showSuccess = false,
    this.showError = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    final borderColor = showSuccess
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        ? c.success.withOpacity(0.55)
        : showError
        // ignore: deprecated_member_use
        ? c.error.withOpacity(0.55)
        : c.cardBorder;
    final focusBorderColor = showSuccess ? c.success : c.primary;

    return TextField(
      controller: controller,
      focusNode: focus,
      obscureText: obscure,
      keyboardType: keyboardType,
      textInputAction: textInputAction,
      onSubmitted: (_) {
        if (nextFocus != null) {
          FocusScope.of(context).requestFocus(nextFocus);
        } else {
          focus.unfocus();
        }
        onSubmitted?.call();
      },
      style: TextStyle(
        color: c.primaryText,
        fontSize: 15,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
      ),
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        hintText: hint,
        hintStyle: TextStyle(color: c.secondaryText, fontSize: 14),
        prefixIcon: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          child: Icon(
            icon,
            color: showSuccess ? c.success : c.secondaryText,
            size: 20,
          ),
        ),
        suffixIcon: suffix != null
            ? Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 12), child: suffix)
            : null,
        filled: true,
        fillColor: c.fieldBg,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: 16,
          vertical: 14,
        ),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: borderColor, width: 0.8),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: focusBorderColor, width: 1.5),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  STATUS ROW (error / success hint below a field)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class _StatusRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String text;
  final Color color;
  const _StatusRow({
    required this.icon,
    required this.text,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Icon(icon, color: color, size: 13),
        const SizedBox(width: 5),
        Text(
          text,
          style: TextStyle(
            color: color,
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
